H3 正反例对照

67 组 / 134 条视频

每组的正例、反例均已收到标注，所有判断均为“合理”。正例展示目标效果，反例展示对应的故障。

标注快照 2026-11-25 10:40（UTC+8）

显示 67 组

01-02 · 视频指令遵循 · 时间戳偏差—P1

寿司广告的三次切点

考点三个镜头是否按指定时刻切换？
观察方法按顺序记下刀切鱼柳、手捏寿司、黑盘成品首次入镜的三个时间，再逐一核对。工序都拍到了，切点仍可能提早。

正例

3 秒切到刀切鱼柳，7 秒切到手捏寿司，12 秒切到黑盘成品。

正例原始 prompt

15秒电影感日料广告。深色木质料理台，左上方暖光，浅景深，舒缓纯音乐。0–3秒俯拍整条带冰三文鱼，白衣厨师扶住鱼头尾；3–7秒切极近微距，厨刀切入橙红鱼柳；7–12秒切近景，厨师给饭团覆鱼片、按压，用银镊点上绿叶；12–15秒切成品展示。 最后一镜中，成品在哑光黑盘正中，日文书法「一期一会、極上の味」从左到右写出，墨色渐浓，末笔落定微光定格。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in an overhead high-angle shot, the camera pushes in at slow speed toward a whole, fresh salmon resting atop a bed of crushed glistening ice on a dark oiled wood counter. A middle-aged male chef wearing a crisp white cotton sushi uniform stands behind the counter, his bare hands carefully holding and stabilizing the fish by its silver-scaled head on the left and tapered tail on the right. Warm directional key light spills from the upper-left corner, highlighting melting water droplets and iridescent scales against the rich shadowed wood, creating a shallow depth of field. [Shot 2] At 00:03.000, the camera cuts to an extreme macro shot as the camera pans right at slow speed, tracking the blade of a polished single-bevel yanagiba knife slicing cleanly through a thick, vibrant orange-red salmon fillet on a dark wooden cutting board. The razor-sharp edge glides through the flesh at a low diagonal angle, parting the distinct white intramuscular fat striations with smooth precision, revealing glistening moisture across the cut surface under the warm upper-left rim light. [Shot 3] At 00:07.000, the camera cuts to a medium close-up from an eye-level angle holding a static shot of the chef's hands crafting a piece of nigiri. His deft fingers gently drape the sliced salmon fillet over an oval mound of vinegared sushi rice, executing a precise two-stroke press to mold the sushi firmly. He then lifts a pair of slender silver culinary tweezers and delicately sets a single fresh microgreen leaf onto the center of the fish, his hands moving with practiced rhythm against the out-of-focus dark wooden interior. [Shot 4] At 00:12.000, the camera cuts to a close-up slightly high-angle shot as the camera pushes in at slow speed toward the finished salmon nigiri positioned squarely in the center of a circular matte black ceramic plate. Traditional Japanese calligraphy appears on screen, writing from left to right across the upper half of the frame in flowing sumi ink strokes to form "一期一会、極上の味", the ink density deepening from a soft wash to dense charcoal black, culminating in a subtle glint across the final brushstroke before settling into a pristine freeze-frame.
overall_soundscape: The delicate crunch and rustle of crushed ice shifting under the fish is clearly heard in the room tone, followed by the crisp, pronounced hiss and glide of the chef's knife parting the tender salmon fillet. Soft, audible finger presses against the moist rice grains accompany the assembly, punctuated by a distinct metallic click of tweezers setting the garnish. A muted, resonant ceramic tap concludes the sequence as the plate settles on the counter, accompanied by a quiet, warm interior kitchen ambience.
non_diegetic_music: A slow-tempo acoustic score featuring gentle solo piano chords intertwined with sustained, resonant koto plucks and a quiet, low-register cello pad underneath. The arrangement maintains a consistent, understated dynamic without crescendo, tapering to a sustained single note at the final rest.

反例

工序顺序相同，三个镜头却分别在 1.4、5、10.4 秒提前出现。

反例原始 prompt

15秒电影感日料广告。深色木质料理台，左上方暖光，浅景深，舒缓纯音乐。0–1.4秒俯拍整条带冰三文鱼，白衣厨师扶住鱼头尾；1.4–5秒切极近微距，厨刀切入橙红鱼柳；5–10.4秒切近景，厨师给饭团覆鱼片、按压，用银镊点上绿叶；10.4–15秒切成品展示。 最后一镜中，成品在哑光黑盘正中，日文书法「一期一会、極上の味」从左到右写出，墨色渐浓，末笔落定微光定格。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a high-angle overhead static shot looks directly down onto a whole fresh Atlantic salmon embedded in a thick bed of crushed glistening ice atop a dark polished walnut sushi counter. An adult East Asian male chef in a crisp white cotton uniform with rolled cuffs extends his clean hands into frame from above, firmly resting his left palm over the tapered tail fin and cupping his right hand beside the gills near the fish's head. Warm directional light beams in from the upper left, casting sparkling specular reflections across the silver-scaled skin and wet ice crystals, while the dark counter perimeter blurs softly in shallow depth of field. [Shot 2] At 00:01.400, the camera cuts to an extreme close-up macro shot with a slow push in, framing a thick cut of glistening orange-pink salmon fillet resting on an end-grain cutting board. The chef draws a long, razor-sharp steel Yanagiba knife angled diagonally through the tender flesh; the polished blade cleanly parts the pale white intramuscular fat striations in a continuous stroke. A single pristine, translucent slice separates effortlessly from the main fillet and drapes smoothly onto the wooden surface under the warm upper-left key light. [Shot 3] At 00:05.000, the camera cuts to a close-up static shot focusing on the chef's hands working over the dark wood counter. In his cupped left hand sits a warm, compact mound of seasoned sushi rice; he drapes the salmon slice over the rice ball, using the index and middle fingers of his right hand to apply two deliberate, rhythmic pressing motions that mold the fish snugly around the grain. He picks up a pair of slender silver culinary tweezers and delicately places a vibrant miniature green shiso leaf directly atop the center of the salmon, completing the nigiri. [Shot 4] At 00:10.400, the camera cuts to an eye-level medium close-up static shot centered on a round matte-black ceramic plate resting on the dark wooden counter. The finished salmon nigiri sits at the exact center of the plate, its glazed orange flesh and green garnish bathed in warm spotlighting. Japanese calligraphy text reading "一期一会、極上の味" writes itself dynamically from left to right across the dark space above the plate in flowing black sumi ink, with the stroke opacity thickening progressively until the final dot drops, triggering a delicate glint of light across the glistening fish as the frame holds still.
overall_soundscape: The faint crinkle and cold shifting of crushed ice under hand pressure is clearly heard at first, immediately followed by the distinct wet whisper of a sharp steel blade parting raw fish and tapping cleanly against the wooden board. Pronounced soft squishes and fingertip rustles accompany the shaping of the rice ball, punctuated by a delicate metallic click as the silver tweezers set the garnish, before settling into a pristine, quiet ambient room tone with a faint, resonant chime marking the final stroke.
non_diegetic_music: A slow-tempo acoustic arrangement featuring solo wooden shakuhachi flute paired with gentle plucked koto notes and sustained low cello pads, maintaining an even, unhurried dynamic across the entire sequence without crescendo.

01-03 · 视频指令遵循 · 核心动作缺失—P0

谁进了我的客厅

考点开场有没有完整拍到屋主进门？
观察方法只回放第一镜，从屋主第一次露面看到客厅出现。找出她的手碰门、推开门和身体跨过门槛的连续动作。

正例

屋主在门外伸手推门，随后跨过门槛进屋。

正例原始 prompt

现实都市剧。开场屋主站在关着的门外，伸手推开门，从门外跨入客厅，镜头跟过门槛。 中景越过屋主肩膀，看见另一名女子正在沙发上吃水果。屋主愣住，用急切女声问：“你是谁？怎么在我家？”沙发上的女子翘起二郎腿，语带轻蔑：“你前夫把这房子给我了，从今天起，这儿归我。”近景，屋主变脸快步上前：“这房子写的是我名字，滚出去！”对方愣一下起身：“你叫谁滚呢？这就是我的房子！”屋主掏出手机冷笑：“行，那我报警，让警察告诉你这房子到底归谁！”

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a medium shot, a tracking shot follows a slender Asian woman in her early thirties with sleek shoulder-length black hair, dressed in a beige wool trench coat, white collared silk blouse, and tailored black trousers, standing outside a locked dark-wood apartment door in a brightly lit residential corridor. She grasps the metallic handle, pushes the heavy door open, and steps across the threshold into the entryway of a modern, stylish apartment illuminated by recessed warm ceiling spotlights, revealing polished tile flooring and an open living room beyond. [Shot 2] At 00:02.700, the camera cuts to an over-the-shoulder medium shot looking past the homeowner's right shoulder, performing a slow push in toward the spacious living room. In the midground, lounging upon an L-shaped grey fabric sofa, sits a woman in her mid-twenties with wavy chestnut-dyed hair, dramatic makeup, and a fitted emerald-green knit dress, spearing a piece of honeydew melon with a small silver fork from a crystal bowl on the white marble coffee table. The homeowner, an on-screen adult female speaker with an urgent, alarmed voice (S1), stiffens in place and demands anxiously, <d>[Chinese] 你是谁？怎么在我家？</d> The lounging intruder, an on-screen female speaker with an arrogant, dismissive tone (S2), crosses one leg over the other, rests her elbow on the sofa cushion, and retorts with a mocking half-smile, <d>[Chinese] 你前夫把这房子给我了，从今天起，这儿归我。</d> [Shot 3] At 00:07.400, the camera cuts to a medium close-up of the homeowner (S1) as the camera holds a static shot. Her facial expression tightens with rage as she knits her brows, swiftly taking two aggressive steps forward into the living room while pointing an accusatory finger forward, shouting forcefully, <d>[Chinese] 这房子写的是我名字，滚出去！</d> [Shot 4] At 00:10.100, the camera cuts to a medium shot of the woman in the green dress (S2) beside the coffee table, executing a tilt up as she momentarily blinks in surprise, then quickly flares her nostrils in anger and abruptly stands up from the cushions to face her rival, yelling back aggressively, <d>[Chinese] 你叫谁滚呢？这就是我的房子！</d> [Shot 5] At 00:12.300, the camera cuts to a medium close-up of the homeowner (S1) as the camera pushes in slightly. She lets out a cold, dismissive sneer, thrusts her hand into her trench coat pocket, and retrieves a sleek black smartphone, tapping the glass screen with her manicured thumb as she declares with icy firmness, <d>[Chinese] 行，那我报警，让警察告诉你这房子到底归谁！</d>
overall_soundscape: A distinct metallic click of the door latch turning followed by the pronounced creak of the wooden apartment door swinging inward, immediately accompanied by loud high-heeled shoe clacks tapping across the polished marble floor. A faint clink of the silver fork scraping against the crystal glass fruit bowl is clearly heard from the sofa, followed by the audible rustle of knit fabric as legs cross. The tense verbal exchange is punctuated by the sudden dull thud and rustle of sofa cushions as the intruder pushes herself upward, ending with the crisp friction sound of the smartphone sliding out of a wool coat pocket and a firm screen tap.
non_diegetic_music: A taut, minimalist suspense score featuring sustained low cello drones, sparse staccato violin pulses, and a subtle electronic ticking element, maintaining a steady moderate tempo and a flat, unswelling dynamic profile throughout.

反例

第一镜从屋主已站在沙发旁开始，直接跳过推门和进屋。

反例原始 prompt

现实都市剧。开场屋主已经站在客厅沙发旁边，门始终关闭，镜头直接从室内开始。 中景越过屋主肩膀，看见另一名女子正在沙发上吃水果。屋主愣住，用急切女声问：“你是谁？怎么在我家？”沙发上的女子翘起二郎腿，语带轻蔑：“你前夫把这房子给我了，从今天起，这儿归我。”近景，屋主变脸快步上前：“这房子写的是我名字，滚出去！”对方愣一下起身：“你叫谁滚呢？这就是我的房子！”屋主掏出手机冷笑：“行，那我报警，让警察告诉你这房子到底归谁！”

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, over-the-shoulder medium shot where the camera pushes in past the right shoulder of an on-screen adult female homeowner in her early thirties (S1), who stands rigid beside an L-shaped gray sectional sofa. In the entrance foyer background, the solid dark wood apartment door remains closed. Seated in the midground center of the modern living room, an on-screen young adult female in her late twenties with wavy chestnut hair (S2), dressed in an emerald green silk pajama set, reclines casually with a glass bowl of cut fruit in her lap. Warm overhead LED ceiling spotlights and a brushed-brass floor lamp illuminate the polished marble coffee table and light oak floorboards. Freezing in disbelief, the homeowner with an urgent, sharp voice (S1) clenches her hands and asks, <d>[Chinese] 你是谁？怎么在我家？</d> Unfazed, the young adult female in green pajamas with a sharp, arrogant tone (S2) crosses her legs, places her metal fruit fork into the bowl with a distinct clatter, and smirks dismissively, <d>[Chinese] 你前夫把这房子给我了，从今天起，这儿归我。</d> [Shot 2] At 00:05.500, the camera cuts to a close-up tracking shot following the homeowner (S1) as her face darkens with rage and she strides rapidly toward the coffee table. The direct ceiling light emphasizes her narrowed eyes and furrowed brow over her tailored beige trench coat, and the furious homeowner (S1) points an accusatory finger toward the sofa and shouts forcefully, <d>[Chinese] 这房子写的是我名字，滚出去！</d> [Shot 3] At 00:08.800, the shot transitions to a medium shot where the camera pedestals up as the woman from Shot 1 (S2) stiffens in sudden astonishment before pushing off the cushions and rising abruptly to her feet. Standing defensively beside the marble coffee table, tossing her hair back with an incensed expression, the woman from Shot 1 (S2) snaps angrily, <d>[Chinese] 你叫谁滚呢？这就是我的房子！</d> [Shot 4] At 00:11.800, the camera cuts to a medium-close-up shot pushing in slowly as the homeowner (S1) stops in her tracks and lets out a cold, derisive sneer. Reaching smoothly into her trench coat pocket, she pulls out a sleek black smartphone, angles the glowing screen forward, and holding up the smartphone, the homeowner (S1) sneers with icy mockery, <d>[Chinese] 行，那我报警，让警察告诉你这房子到底归谁！</d>
overall_soundscape: A low, steady hum of apartment central air conditioning forms the quiet ambient room tone throughout the indoor space. As the intruder shifts on the couch, the distinct metallic clink of a fruit fork contacting glass is clearly heard, followed by the pronounced, loud clack of leather heeled boots stomping rapidly across the wooden floor. An audible rustle of silk fabric and sofa cushion decompression occurs as the seated woman abruptly rises, ending with the clear, crisp mechanical tap of fingers striking a glass smartphone screen dominant in the foreground.
non_diegetic_music: A low-frequency synthesizer drone anchored by sparse, mid-tempo electronic pulse beats and bowed acoustic bass notes, maintaining an even, unswelling dynamic profile without percussive crashes.

01-04 · 视频指令遵循 · 景别错误

冰淇淋广告开场景别

考点第一镜是否用了指定的特写？
观察方法暂停在开场第一幅清楚画面，看脸和蛋筒占多大面积。后面洒糖时的微距镜头另计。

正例

脸和手里的蛋筒占满开场画面。

正例原始 prompt

一位深色及肩发、自然淡妆的年轻女子担任全片主角，外观连续。创建一个超写实的高级冰淇淋广告，呈现奢华夏日生活美学。场景设在一家优雅的手工冰淇淋店内，大理石柜台、柔和的室内装饰、玻璃展示柜、温暖的阳光、柔和的散景、电影级调色、真实的反射和高级商业灯光。自然过渡到黄金时刻的迷人欧式街道，两旁是咖啡馆和鲜花。
广告以电影级特写开场，脸部与手中蛋筒占满画面，她对着镜头温暖微笑，手持一个装满草莓和香草冰淇淋的华夫筒。她慢慢旋转蛋筒，展示其奶油质地和多彩配料。
过渡到极致的微距镜头，彩虹糖粒轻轻洒在冰淇淋上，随后是浓郁闪亮的巧克力酱倾泻而下。捕捉奢华的冰淇淋融化特写、松脆配料、新鲜草莓片和华夫筒的细腻纹理。
她将蛋筒缓缓举到唇边，享受一口，然后自然微笑，品尝奶油风味。相机以电影级细节捕捉她真实的表情和融化的冰淇淋。
场景转换到户外，她漫步在迷人的欧式街道上，手持冰淇淋，温暖的阳光洒在她的脸上。她回头自信地微笑，继续走过咖啡馆和鲜花点缀的人行道，发丝在微风中轻轻飘动。
广告在冰淇淋店外优雅的主镜头中结束。她将冰淇淋举到脸旁，与镜头保持眼神交流，微笑着用温柔的普通话女声说道：“这是我最喜欢的一家冰激凌，每一口都让生活更甜蜜。”相机缓缓推进，突出奢华的冰淇淋和她容光焕发的表情，然后淡出。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, close-up framing opens inside a luminous luxury artisanal gelato boutique, where the camera pushes in slowly at eye level. Positioned center-frame is a young East Asian female protagonist in her early twenties with sleek, dark shoulder-length hair parted neatly, subtle natural makeup, and dewy skin, wearing an elegant ivory sleeveless silk blouse. She holds a freshly baked golden-brown waffle cone filled with generous swirls of pastel-pink strawberry gelato and pale ivory vanilla ice cream directly in front of her chest. In the background, a pristine white Carrara marble counter, brass-trimmed glass display freezer, and warm interior pendant lamps create soft circular bokeh under soft commercial diffusion lighting and warm afternoon sunbeams streaming through floor-to-ceiling windows. She looks straight into the lens with a warm, welcoming smile while gently rotating the cone clockwise between her slender fingers to showcase its creamy velvet texture. [Shot 2] At 00:03.200, the camera cuts to an extreme macro close-up focusing tightly on the ice cream scoops, where the camera tilts down slowly. Glossy, rich dark chocolate syrup cascades down over the peak of the vanilla and strawberry scoops, pooling into the textured crevices alongside a scatter of vibrant pastel rainbow sprinkles and thin, glossy fresh strawberry slices clinging to the side. Bright studio rim lights glisten across the reflective chocolate drizzle and glistening micro-droplets of melting cream as a few crisp waffle crumbs cling to the cone's diamond-patterned rim. [Shot 3] At 00:06.000, the camera cuts to a medium close-up inside the boutique as the camera pushes in slowly. The young woman from Shot 1 lifts the waffle cone gracefully to her lips, tilting her head slightly as she takes a delicate, savored taste of the creamy vanilla gelato. Her eyes flutter closed for a brief moment of pure sensory pleasure before she reopens them, revealing a blissful, natural smile as her cheeks soften in quiet delight against the warm, sun-dappled interior background. [Shot 4] At 00:08.800, the camera cuts to a medium tracking shot outdoors, tracking backward along a picturesque European cobblestone pedestrian street bathed in radiant golden hour sunlight. The young woman from Shot 1 strolls forward at a relaxed pace, holding the ice cream cone in her right hand while her dark hair flutters gently in an afternoon breeze. Sidewalk bistro tables, striped canvas awnings, and terracotta flower boxes brimming with deep pink geraniums flank the sidewalk in soft focus. She turns her head over her right shoulder to cast a confident, radiant glance back toward the camera before turning forward again with an effortless gait. [Shot 5] At 00:11.800, the camera cuts to an eye-level medium master shot positioned on the sidewalk outside the ice cream shop's sage-green paneled wooden facade and polished brass door fixtures, where the camera pushes in smoothly toward her. Standing gracefully center-frame, the young female on-screen protagonist with a gentle, soothing voice (S1) holds the artisanal waffle cone elegantly beside her cheek, maintains direct eye contact with the lens, and smiles warmly as she speaks softly, <d>[Chinese] 这是我最喜欢的一家冰激凌，每一口都让生活更甜蜜。</d> The camera glides closer, accentuating her glowing complexion and the luxurious dessert before the image softly fades out.
overall_soundscape: A gentle boutique brass door chime dings softly, followed by the faint, distinct wet drizzle and thick drop of warm chocolate fudge. Audible fabric rustle accompanies her gentle movements, transitioning into distinct rhythmic heel taps on outdoor cobblestone pavement beneath the muted murmur of open-air cafe patrons and a light breeze rustling through flower leaves.
non_diegetic_music: Acoustic nylon-string guitar fingerpicking accompanied by a warm upright acoustic bass and subtle brushed snare, mid-tempo, maintaining a gentle and steady dynamic level throughout.

反例

开场看见整排展示柜和人物全身，脸和蛋筒只占一小角。

反例原始 prompt

一位深色及肩发、自然淡妆的年轻女子担任全片主角，外观连续。创建一个超写实的高级冰淇淋广告，呈现奢华夏日生活美学。场景设在一家优雅的手工冰淇淋店内，大理石柜台、柔和的室内装饰、玻璃展示柜、温暖的阳光、柔和的散景、电影级调色、真实的反射和高级商业灯光。自然过渡到黄金时刻的迷人欧式街道，两旁是咖啡馆和鲜花。
广告以店内大全景开场，全身的女子和蛋筒仅占画面一小角，大理石柜台与整排玻璃展示柜都在画内，她对着镜头温暖微笑，手持一个装满草莓和香草冰淇淋的华夫筒。她慢慢旋转蛋筒，展示其奶油质地和多彩配料。
过渡到极致的微距镜头，彩虹糖粒轻轻洒在冰淇淋上，随后是浓郁闪亮的巧克力酱倾泻而下。捕捉奢华的冰淇淋融化特写、松脆配料、新鲜草莓片和华夫筒的细腻纹理。
她将蛋筒缓缓举到唇边，享受一口，然后自然微笑，品尝奶油风味。相机以电影级细节捕捉她真实的表情和融化的冰淇淋。
场景转换到户外，她漫步在迷人的欧式街道上，手持冰淇淋，温暖的阳光洒在她的脸上。她回头自信地微笑，继续走过咖啡馆和鲜花点缀的人行道，发丝在微风中轻轻飘动。
广告在冰淇淋店外优雅的主镜头中结束。她将冰淇淋举到脸旁，与镜头保持眼神交流，微笑着用温柔的普通话女声说道：“这是我最喜欢的一家冰激凌，每一口都让生活更甜蜜。”相机缓缓推进，突出奢华的冰淇淋和她容光焕发的表情，然后淡出。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in an extreme wide shot, the camera maintains a static shot framing the expansive, sun-drenched interior of an artisanal ice cream boutique. Towards the lower-right midground, a slender East Asian woman in her early twenties with shoulder-length dark brown hair and natural subtle makeup wears a sleeveless ivory linen summer dress, occupying a modest portion of the frame. She stands beside an elongated white Carrara marble counter featuring brass hardware, while a gleaming glass display cabinet stretches across the background showcasing neatly sculpted mounds of colorful gelato. Soft morning sunlight filters through tall arched French windows, casting warm geometric light beams and soft bokeh across pastel-toned walls. The woman faces the lens with a warm, welcoming smile, slowly rotating a golden waffle cone topped with pale pink strawberry and ivory vanilla scoops between her fingers to display its creamy texture. 02-03 · 运动质量 · 背景人／物持续结构错误

观察方法看0–7秒左上角背景仙鹤的头颈结构。九尾狐的造型和运动独立。

正例

左上角的鹤只有一根颈和一只头，扇翅时仍能辨清。

正例原始 prompt

仙侠九尾狐于青丘桃林出镜，镜头慢推，狐尾轻扫、抬眸抚瓣，它说道：“这上古密林也不过如此”，竹笛背景音+桃林风声，画面清冷有质感。 0–7秒画面左上方一直有一只仙鹤，能辨出一根颈、一只头和两翼，随慢推自然扇翅。

02-08 · 运动质量 · 不合理转场／切镜／跳帧

拿筋膜枪的切镜

考点切镜有没有跳过握住、举起产品的动作？
观察方法3秒看切点前后的手和产品是否能接上，后面有动机的肩部产品特写不扣。

正例

手从肩颈移到桌面，握紧把手，再举起筋膜枪。

正例原始 prompt

9:16写实短视频广告，办公室午休。年轻女生坐在办公椅，桌上有电脑、咖啡和成堆文件。开头固定中景，完整交代手从肩颈移向桌面、握住把手、把产品举起的过程，3秒仍在同一连续动作中。 她揉着后脖颈和肩膀，举起小巧的筋膜枪，单一清亮女声按自然口型口播：“你们久坐肩颈酸的时候，真的别硬扛。我以前一到下午，脖子就像被水泥糊住一样。”她开到低档轻轻按在肩颈，头发和衣服轻微振动。切肩部按摩头近景：“这个我喜欢的是它不重，女生单手拿也不累。下班前按个三五分钟，肩膀会松很多。”她靠回椅背放松，另一手调档，最后放进抽屉，又拿起来对镜头晃一下：“经常久坐、低头、肩颈紧的，真的可以备一个。现在直播间有券，到手价很香。”

正例最终 PE prompt

02-09 · 运动质量 · 物理不合理/凭空消失出现

手到嘴之间的薄脆饼干

考点饼干在手和嘴之间是否受到支撑？
观察方法看1–3秒指尖松开后饼干有无支撑。判的是零食悬浮。

正例

指尖始终捏着饼干，饼干跟随手送入口。

正例原始 prompt

15秒“同程金卡”商旅口播。机场贵宾休息区两张相邻沙发，男生一开始坐在靠窗的那张，始终用同一自然男声。0–3秒他慵懒靠着，撕开小包装薄脆饼干。1–3秒他用指尖捏住饼干送入口内，饼干沿手的路径移动。 “如果你旅行预算不多，但又想让自己旅途上舒服一点的……”3–6秒咽下，拿零食的手指一下镜头：“……一定要记得订机票前，先去领一下这个金卡。”6–12秒切中景，他停止进食，身体前倾，手部比划：“不仅有机会兑换机场贵宾厅，提前值机、安静候机都舒服很多，而且住酒店如果没订满的话，还可能免费升级房型！”12–15秒看向镜头，手指屏幕下方：“这个卡是限时免费领的，打开同程搜金卡188就能找到，趁活动还在赶紧去试试吧！”

正例最终 PE prompt

02-12 · 运动质量 · 动作肢体僵硬／不协调

桌边戏剧动作的停顿

考点动作进行到一半时是否反复僵住？
观察方法找多次动作走到一半突然僵住再启动的时刻，与演员有意收势后的停顿区分。

正例

伸手、转身、收势各自动作连贯。

正例原始 prompt

稳定镜头无抖动。桌子左右各坐一人，男生突然起身拍桌，女生跳上桌面，两人在桌面和桌边完成一段编排好的电影动作表演，以手势交错和位置变化呈现冲突，不出现伤情。从拍桌、上桌到桌边交锋动作依次衔接，身体转身、伸手和收势保留动势。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, wide shot at eye level maintaining a Static Shot that frames a weathered solid oak refectory table centered horizontally in a dimly lit industrial rehearsal studio with polished concrete floors and large paned factory windows in the background. Overhead, two warm industrial metal pendant lamps cast directional pools of warm light down onto the tabletop, contrasting with cool diffuse daylight filtering from behind. On the left side of the table sits an athletic young East Asian man in his early twenties, sporting short neat black hair, dressed in a fitted dark gray long-sleeve cotton shirt, black utility trousers, and dark combat boots. Seated opposite him on the right side is an agile young East Asian woman in her early twenties, her sleek black hair tied into a high ponytail, wearing a zipped olive-green canvas jacket over a black top, dark stretch trousers, and flexible black training sneakers. Without warning, the young man abruptly shoots up from his wooden chair, leaning his torso forward and slamming both open palms hard onto the oak tabletop. Anticipating the motion, the woman instantly pushes off her chair, planting her hands on the rim and leaping fluidly upward to land in a low athletic crouch atop the wooden surface. The man thrusts an open palm toward her midsection, but the woman swivels her hips, deflecting his wrist inward with a forearm parry. Rising into a half-turn atop the table, she whips an open-hand strike toward his chest, which he absorbs and redirects by shifting his weight backward along the side of the table. Maintaining fluid momentum, she executes a controlled spin on the balls of her feet, sweeping her arm out to clear his reach before sliding off the table edge onto the concrete floor beside him. In immediate close proximity, both fighters exchange three rapid, choreographed hand-trapping blocks, their forearms crossing cleanly in mid-air without delivering injury, before simultaneously springing backward a half-step into balanced, wide defensive postures with fists raised and chests heaving in controlled recovery.
overall_soundscape: A loud, echoing thud resonates as the man's palms slam into the heavy wood, instantly followed by the distinct sharp tap and scuff of rubber sneaker soles landing onto the tabletop. Pronounced fabric rustles and clear, sharp swishes of displaced air accompany each swift punch, parry, and body rotation. Audible impacts of forearms clashing in rapid parries ring through the space, concluding with the squeak of sneakers gripping the concrete floor and distinct synchronized exhalations as both fighters snap into their final defensive stances.
non_diegetic_music: A taut, fast-tempo cinematic percussion ensemble featuring dry wooden rim clicks, steady deep taiko strikes, and low synthetic sub-bass pulses, maintaining a consistent rhythmic drive without melodic instrumentation or dynamic swells.

反例

伸手途中和转身一半等多处突然定住半拍，又从原姿势启动。

反例原始 prompt

稳定镜头无抖动。桌子左右各坐一人，男生突然起身拍桌，女生跳上桌面，两人在桌面和桌边完成一段编排好的电影动作表演，以手势交错和位置变化呈现冲突，不出现伤情。拍桌、上桌到桌边交锋的动作顺序相同，但在伸手途中、转身一半和收势前各僵住半拍，画面冻住，动作肢体僵硬，再从冻结姿势突然启动；摄影机持续拍摄。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a wide shot captures a brightly lit industrial loft space with textured concrete walls, exposed ceiling pipes, and a long, weathered wooden trestle table positioned horizontally across the center of the frame. The camera holds a static shot with no movement or camera shake. On the left side of the table sits a slender young East Asian man in his early twenties with messy black hair, wearing an unbuttoned olive-green canvas overshirt over a white cotton t-shirt and loose black cargo trousers. On the right sits an athletic young East Asian woman with a high black ponytail, dressed in a fitted charcoal-gray athletic zip-up top and black joggers with white leather trainers. Overhead industrial pendant lamps cast soft, directional downlight across the scuffed wood grain, complemented by cool rim lighting spilling through large multi-pane factory windows in the background. Without warning, the young man abruptly stands up and slams his open right palm onto the tabletop, sending a cloud of chalky dust into the air, yet his outstretched arm rigidly freezes in place for half a beat with locked elbows and wide, glaring eyes. The young woman instantly crouches and leaps onto the tabletop, landing in a low cat-like stance before springing forward. Just as the young man snaps out of his frozen state and extends both arms forward to intercept her flying palm strike, both of their limbs freeze rigidly mid-reach, hovering inches apart in an exaggerated, completely motionless tableau. A split-second later, they burst into rapid motion again; the woman spins across the wooden surface with a sweeping kick while the man ducks under her leg and vaults onto the bench. Halfway through her mid-air torso spin, her body freezes dead in mid-rotation, suspended horizontally with one leg outstretched and hair rigid in the air, while the man halts mid-crouch with stiff, angular fingers. Suddenly releasing from the freeze, they collide in a rapid flurry of parrying forearms, cross-arm deflections, and swift stance changes along the edge of the tabletop, executing a sharp, rehearsed martial arts sequence without making damaging contact. Right as they recoil to prepare their concluding strikes, both performers lock into a third stiff, tense freeze for half a beat, their clenched fists and flared shoulders completely immobilized. Finally snapping into motion, they smoothly pull back their fists and sink into a symmetrical low-stance standoff facing each other along the table's perimeter.
overall_soundscape: A loud, echoing palm thud slams down against the wooden tabletop, instantly followed by the screech of chair legs dragging across the concrete floor. Swift fabric swishes and sharp sneaker squeaks accompany the leap onto the wood and continue unchanged during each rigid freeze-frame beat. Between the freezes, rapid, audible clacks and slaps of skin-on-skin forearm blocks resonate cleanly, accompanied by pronounced bursts of focused exhalations, terminating in a crisp, synchronized foot-stomp on the floor.
non_diegetic_music: A sharp, syncopated electronic breakbeat with punchy acoustic kick drums and dry metallic snares drives the rhythm at a brisk tempo, continuing unchanged through the three frozen moments and the bursts of movement.

说故事的男人的脸

考点面颊是否跟随说话和换气自然放松？

正例

正例原始 prompt

02-15 · 运动质量 · 运动过快／抽搐

餐桌左侧女孩的头

考点是否只有左侧女孩的头短时抽动？
观察方法在5–6秒看左边女孩头部，相机稳定、旁人活动正常，可以排除全画面抖动。

正例

抬眼再低头时，头颈连续转动。

正例原始 prompt

一家四口围坐在餐桌旁吃晚餐。父亲盛汤，母亲递过一盘蔬菜，女儿用筷子摆弄着食物，儿子伸手去拿鸡腿。晚餐氛围轻松活泼，背景是厨房。音效设计：家人的闲聊声、筷子碰碗的声音、汤锅里的汤勺声、孩子们的笑声，充满温馨的家庭环境音。 女儿坐在画面左侧。5–6秒她抬眼看母亲又低头，头和颈部连续自然转动。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a medium shot at eye level as the camera slowly pushes in toward a warm family dining table. On the left side of the frame in the foreground, a seven-year-old girl with dark hair tied in two loose low ponytails and wearing a pastel pink knit sweater sits with a pair of bamboo chopsticks, lightly nudging at a bowl of steamed rice and braised tofu. Across from her on the right, her mother, a woman in her late thirties with a soft wavy shoulder-length bob wearing an oatmeal cardigan beneath an olive-green apron, gently lifts and passes an oval white ceramic platter of stir-fried green vegetables toward the center of the table. In the center midground, the father, a man in his late thirties with short neat black hair and a navy-blue henley shirt, reaches with a stainless steel soup ladle into a steaming porcelain tureen, carefully spooning golden broth into a ceramic bowl. Beside the mother, an energetic ten-year-old boy in a yellow-and-navy striped cotton t-shirt eagerly leans his torso forward, reaching his chopsticks out toward a central serving dish to snatch a glossy braised chicken drumstick. In the background, a cozy kitchen features white subway tiles, wooden cabinetry, and soft warm under-cabinet strip lighting. A rounded pendant lamp hangs directly above the wooden table, casting a soft golden glow over the food and faces. Around five to six seconds into the scene, the daughter on the left smoothly lifts her chin and naturally rotates her neck to look up at her mother, offering a soft smile, before fluidly turning her head back down to continue arranging the food in her bowl, while the son triumphantly sets the drumstick onto his plate and the father smiles as he sets the filled soup bowl down.
overall_soundscape: Audible light clinking of bamboo chopsticks tapping against porcelain bowls, accompanied by the distinct metallic scrape of the stainless steel ladle against the ceramic tureen. 反例

02-16 · 运动质量 · 主体全程静止

深海歌手有没有动

考点唱歌的主角全程有没有自身动作？
观察方法整段观察歌手自身，鱼群、气泡和镜头都可在活动时，主角仍可能全程静止。

正例

歌手双唇唱动、双臂张开，头发与裙摆随水流舒展。

正例原始 prompt

深海音乐MV，澄澈深蓝色水下落下丁达尔光束，气泡上升，海底礁石可见。白纱裙女歌手悬浮在水中，闭目、话筒抵唇；可听见她的深情歌声。歌手轻轻唱动双唇，双臂缓缓张开，纱裙和长发被水流带动舒展。 彩鱼从远处游来并绕她盘旋，水光在鱼身上流转，镜头先缓缓推进，伴着空灵尾音拉远定格深海全景。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a medium shot at eye level as the camera pushes in at slow speed toward an on-screen young East Asian female singer in her early twenties (S1) suspended weightlessly in the center of the frame. Her eyes remain serenely closed as she cradles a vintage brushed-steel microphone against her lightly parted lips. She slowly unfurls both arms outward to her sides, allowing the sheer white fabric of her sleeves to drift and undulate against the water currents. In the right midground, a vibrant school of tropical reef fish with bright turquoise, golden-yellow, and vivid orange striped scales approaches and begins to curve toward her floating form. The camera continues to glide steadily backward until settling into a balanced wide composition, framing her motionless suspended figure against the expansive deep-sea expanse as fine glittering bubbles ascend into the upper frame.

non_diegetic_music: Sustained ambient synthesizer pads combined with slow-tempo bowed acoustic cellos and distant, reverb-drenched harp arpeggios that maintain a steady dynamic floor without percussive beats.

反例

鱼群与气泡在动，歌手的嘴、手臂、头发和裙摆全程锁在同一姿势。

反例原始 prompt

深海音乐MV，澄澈深蓝色水下落下丁达尔光束，气泡上升，海底礁石可见。白纱裙女歌手悬浮在水中，闭目、话筒抵唇；可听见她的深情歌声。从首帧到尾帧，歌手的双唇、双臂、长发与白纱都锁死在同一姿势，像静态剪影；只有身外的鱼和气泡活动。 彩鱼从远处游来并绕她盘旋，水光在鱼身上流转，镜头先缓缓推进，伴着空灵尾音拉远定格深海全景。

02-19 · 运动质量 · 背景活物静止

学校走廊里的三个女孩

考点三名背景女生是否也在活动？
观察方法跟踪背景三名女生。原题专门要求远处男孩呆立，不能用男孩的静止作错误证据。

正例

能分别看到眨眼、转头或挪半步等微小动作。

正例原始 prompt

16:9写实画面，20世纪90年代初苏联解体后的校园。八岁红发女孩穿浅蓝白格纹连衣裙、白色漆皮鞋，怒气冲冲稳步走向水泥地上的橄榄绿帆布书包，踢中书包，包贴地滑行停下。她平静地走近，再踢一次。稳定侧向跟拍贯穿长走廊。远处男孩张嘴呆立，镜头前移使他出画。红发女孩身后的三名女同学各有微小反应，一个眨眼，一个转头，另一个挪动半步。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a medium full shot with eye-level framing, a smooth lateral tracking shot moves along a bleak, cavernous school corridor in early 1990s post-Soviet Russia. The setting features institutional walls coated in peeling mint-green oil paint below faded whitewash, tall multi-paned metal-frame windows admitting diffuse, overcast daylight, cast-iron radiators, and worn grey concrete flooring speckled with hairline cracks. In the center midground, an eight-year-old girl with pale skin, light freckles, and shoulder-length wavy auburn-red hair walks with deliberate, furious determination from right to left. She wears a knee-length light blue and white gingham cotton dress with puffed cap sleeves, ruffled white ankle socks, and glossy white patent leather Mary Jane shoes. Ahead of her on the bare floor lies a battered olive-green canvas school satchel with tarnished brass buckles and frayed strap edges. Maintaining an unblinking, sullen glare, the red-haired girl steps forward and kicks the satchel squarely with the toe of her right shoe. The satchel skids across the floor, sliding several feet before grinding to a halt near the baseboard. Without breaking stride, her expression settles into chilling composure as she steadily advances toward the bag once more. In the far background, a skinny eight-year-old boy in an oversized brown ribbed wool sweater and faded navy trousers stands immobilized against a classroom doorway with his mouth slack and agape; as the camera tracks steadily alongside the girl, the boy drifts toward the frame edge and exits out of view. Behind the girl's path, three female classmates in dark wool school pinafores and oversized hair ribbons stand clustered against the wall, exhibiting subtle reactions: the first girl with dark braids blinks repeatedly in shock, the second blonde girl nervously turns her head away, and the third girl in a grey knitted cardigan timidly shifts half a step back. The red-haired girl reaches the satchel, pauses for half a beat, and firmly drives her white shoe into the canvas bag a second time, sending it skidding further down the hallway.
overall_soundscape: Rhythmic, sharp clicks of patent leather shoes echo audibly against the institutional concrete floor, accompanied by the low ambient rumble of a drafty school hallway and distant muffled corridor murmurs. A pronounced dull thud impacts the canvas backpack, immediately followed by the loud, grating scrape of coarse fabric and metal buckles sliding across gritty cement. After a brief pause filled with steady shoe clicks, a second distinct kicking impact and resonant sliding rustle ring out sharply in the foreground.
non_diegetic_music: A slow, austere cello drone sustained over sparse, low piano notes at a solemn tempo, maintaining a restrained, flat dynamic shape without swells.

反例

主角或镜头在动，背景三人的眼、头、手和脚全程停在原位。

反例原始 prompt

16:9写实画面，20世纪90年代初苏联解体后的校园。八岁红发女孩穿浅蓝白格纹连衣裙、白色漆皮鞋，怒气冲冲稳步走向水泥地上的橄榄绿帆布书包，踢中书包，包贴地滑行停下。她平静地走近，再踢一次。稳定侧向跟拍贯穿长走廊。远处男孩张嘴呆立，镜头前移使他出画。红发女孩身后的三名女同学全程保持同一姿势，眼睛、头、手和脚完全不动，主角和摄影机照常移动。

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium full shot at a level angle as the camera executes a continuous tracking shot moving laterally along an institutional corridor. She is dressed in a crisp light blue and white gingham-patterned cotton dress with short sleeves, ruffled white ankle socks, and polished white patent leather Mary Jane shoes. 03-02 · 时序一致性 · 主体属性不一致(含主体互换)—P0

沙发三人对话里的发型

考点同一人物切镜前后的发型是否接得上？
观察方法9.7秒前后只对比同一白衣女子的发型。衣色、位置用来确认比较对象。

正例

白衣女子在两镜里都留齐肩黑发。

正例原始 prompt

自然窗光的现代都市剧。广角拍三名女子坐在沙发上。左侧棕色皮夹克女子坐在扶手上，声音锐利：“你真的想好了？这条路可不好走。”硬切到中间浅绿大衣女子的紧凑特写，她看手，轻声说：“我知道，但我不想再等了。”硬切右侧白色上衣、黑白条纹半裙的女孩，她黑发齐肩，热情前倾：“怕什么！大不了从头再来！”9.7秒切回左侧女子的特写，右侧白衣女孩仍可在背景辨认。切点前后，白衣女孩都留着同一头齐肩黑发。 左侧女子挑眉、赞许地冷笑：“这还像句人话。我挺你。”

overall_soundscape: A gentle low-frequency hum of distant city traffic and muffled urban ambience filters through the double-glazed apartment windows as leather softly creaks under shifting weight on the armrest. A quiet, dry chuckle escapes under breath before settling into clean room tone.
non_diegetic_music: Sparse acoustic piano playing clean, unresolved mid-register triads at a slow, deliberate tempo, accompanied by a subtle, warm bowed double bass drone that sustains evenly without swelling.

03-03 · 时序一致性 · 主体数量不一致(含主体复制)-P0

梳妆台前多出的女主

考点镜框外是否多出第二个真人？
观察方法先找镜框边界。玻璃内的倒影合理，原人数错误要能指认镜外第二个实体。

正例

梳妆台前一名女子，玻璃里一个与她同步的倒影。

正例原始 prompt

镜头切到一只绘有牡丹的青花瓷瓶，缓缓移动，聚焦穿淡雅古装的女子梳妆。她羞涩地照镜涂口红，木窗和纸扇构成背景。3–5秒梳妆台前只有她一个真人，镜中有且仅有她同步涂口红的倒影。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium shot, the camera trucks right at a slow speed across an ornate dark rosewood vanity table. The framing begins focused on an antique blue-and-white porcelain vase adorned with intricate cobalt-blue painted peony blossoms and swirling foliage, resting on the left side of the polished tabletop. As the camera glides smoothly to the right, the focus shifts past the vase to reveal a graceful young woman in her early twenties seated alone at the vanity. She has a slender build, an oval face, smooth fair skin, and dark almond-shaped eyes cast modestly downward. Her lustrous black hair is elegantly styled in a traditional half-updo fastened with a carved white jade hairpin and a delicate pearl tassel, leaving soft strands resting along her neck. She wears an elegant, pale celadon-green silk Hanfu robe featuring subtle ivory floral embroidery along the cross-collar and flowing wide sleeves. Behind her, the background consists of traditional wooden lattice windows filtering soft, diffused morning sunlight into the chamber, flanked by an unfolded paper fan with ink-painted bamboo motifs mounted on a miniature wooden stand. Diffused natural daylight enters from the right window, casting soft highlights onto the bronze mirror and accentuating the porcelain finish of her skin. The woman sits entirely alone before the vanity, and the polished round bronze mirror on its carved stand reflects only her exact, synchronized image. Blushing shyly, she lifts a thin crimson rouge paper between the fingertips of her right hand and places it against her lips. Her mirror reflection synchronously mirrors the motion as she lightly compresses her lips to transfer the red pigment. She lowers the paper, presses her lips together in a quiet pout to even the color, and tilts her head slightly to inspect the vibrant rouge, a demure smile breaking across her face as her gaze drops bashfully toward the wooden tabletop.
overall_soundscape: The distinct rustle of silk fabric is clearly heard as the woman raises her arm toward her face, followed by the faint, dry slide of the rouge paper against her lips. A soft ambient room tone carries the gentle, audible whisper of an outside breeze passing through the lattice woodwork, accompanied by the delicate, pronounced clink of the jade tassel tapping against the wooden frame of the mirror.
non_diegetic_music: A slow-tempo traditional acoustic piece led by sparse, delicate guzheng plucking over sustained, airy bamboo flute drones, maintaining a steady, quiet dynamic level throughout.

反例

镜框外的侧后方又出现一个相貌相同、能独立转头的人。

反例原始 prompt

镜头切到一只绘有牡丹的青花瓷瓶，缓缓移动，聚焦穿淡雅古装的女子梳妆。她羞涩地照镜涂口红，木窗和纸扇构成背景。3–5秒镜框外、她侧后方多出第二个一模一样的真人，独立转头；镜中仍有正常的倒影。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium shot, the camera pans right at slow speed across an antique wooden dressing table. The frame begins on a tall Ming-style blue-and-white porcelain vase adorned with cobalt-blue hand-painted peony blossoms on glossy white glaze, standing next to an open folding paper fan decorated with monochrome ink wash bamboo. As the camera glides smoothly to the right, it reveals a young East Asian woman in her early twenties with porcelain skin, delicate facial features, and jet-black hair arranged into an elegant traditional updo fastened by an understated white jade hairpin. She wears pale, graceful traditional Hanfu attire consisting of an ivory-white inner crossover robe and an outer celadon-green silk garment with subtly embroidered sleeve cuffs. Seated on a carved rosewood stool, she gazes shyly into a brass-trimmed oval mirror resting on the table. Diffused natural daylight filters through a carved wooden latticed window in the background, casting soft highlights across her cheekbones and the glossy porcelain surface while leaving the room's deeper recesses in cool, muted shadow. The woman dips the tip of her index finger into a miniature red lacquer pot, then gently dabs vermilion rouge onto her lower lip with a modest, blushing smile. From 00:03.000 through 00:05.000, in the physical space directly behind her left shoulder and outside the visual cone of the mirror frame, a second living woman—an exact physical doppelgänger sharing the identical face, pale Hanfu, and jade-pinned hairstyle—remains clearly visible in the dim midground. While the first woman continues oblivious, softly pressing her painted lips together as her lone reflection in the mirror flawlessly mirrors every subtle twitch and smile, the second identical woman behind her independently pivots her head away, turning her gaze in profile toward the darkened right corner of the chamber with a calm, unblinking expression. The first woman tilts her chin to examine her rouge in the glass, while her physical double remains motionless behind her, leaving the mirror showing only a single, undisturbed reflection.
overall_soundscape: A soft, distinct rustle of silk sleeves shifting across polished rosewood is clearly heard as the woman reaches for her rouge pot. A delicate, audible ceramic click sounds when her fingernail brushes against the porcelain rim of the rouge jar. The faint, persistent murmur of a gentle exterior breeze against the paper latticed window provides continuous room tone, followed by an almost imperceptible, dry fabric swish as the duplicate figure behind her slowly turns her neck.
non_diegetic_music: A sparse, slow-tempo solo guqin piece with lingering string bends, underpinned by a continuous, low-register bowed cello drone that maintains a quiet, uniform dynamic throughout.

03-04 · 时序一致性 · 风格不一致-P0

神殿切镜从二维变三维

考点切镜前后是否保持同一种画法？
观察方法看两个镜头的造型媒介与渲染方式。不同人物本身的衣色或造型差异不构成风格错误。

正例

两镜都以二维手绘轮廓和平涂色块塑造人物。

正例原始 prompt

东方玄幻动画。第一镜用二维手绘、硬边色块和轮廓线：赤足白衣人物悬浮在神殿，丝绸随气流飘，青蓝光自体内扩散，顶光照亮眉心朱砂和双臂金璎珞。听到悠远梵音、金属共振。镜头骤切到高空俯视的黑翼女子，黑翼展开、暗金甲胄逆光勾边，低沉冷声说：“你以为觉醒，就能逆天？”切镜后仍是二维手绘轮廓与平涂色块，暗金高光也用手绘线表现。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] 2D-animated, in a low-angle medium wide shot, the camera pushes in slowly toward a slender, androgynous celestial figure floating suspended in mid-air within a vast, ancient oriental pantheon. The character has pale porcelain skin, long flowing black hair drifting freely in supernatural currents, and wears layered, ethereal white silk robes with loose sleeves billowing outward. Golden beaded armlets (yingluo) wrap around both bare pale arms, and a brilliant crimson cinnabar bindi is marked cleanly between the brows. Rendered in traditional hand-drawn cel animation with sharp ink contour lines and hard-edged flat color blocks, vivid cyan-blue spiritual energy diffuses intensely outward from deep within the figure's chest, casting graphic cerulean highlights along the fluttering robes. High above, a pure white divine downlight beams straight down, illuminating the red forehead mark and gleaming along the metallic surface of the golden jewelry. In the background, colossal stone pillars carved with archaic mythical beasts recede into swirling floor mist beneath vaulted wooden shrine rafters. The levitating figure's bare feet point gently downward as their closed eyelids flutter, their chest expanding with a subtle breath as the pulsating light spreads across the sanctuary floor. [Shot 2] At 00:07.500, the camera cuts to a high-angle wide shot looking down from above, pushing in slowly toward a dark-winged woman floating ominously against a swirling overcast sky above the shrine roofs. The warrior woman has sharp, angular facial features, pale skin, and long raven hair whipping violently in high-altitude gale winds. Spanning across the upper frame, an enormous pair of feathered obsidian-black avian wings unfurls with powerful beats, shedding stylized dark feather motes. She is clad in form-fitting dark gold articulated armor, rendered in clean flat shading with stylized hand-drawn golden rim-lines catching a fierce celestial backlight from behind. Looking down with narrowed, mocking crimson eyes and a contemptuous sneer, the young adult female on-screen winged warrior with a low, cold voice (S1) tilts her chin down slightly and speaks chillingly, <d>[Chinese] 你以为觉醒，就能逆天？</d> As she finishes speaking, her left armored hand curls slightly into a sharp claw-like posture while her massive wings give a slow, dominant downward thrust.
overall_soundscape: A distant, resonant Sanskrit vocal hum and low metallic gong reverberation echo in the background, accompanied by the distinct rustle of silk robes fluttering in circulating wind. An audible crystalline hum pulses with the expanding cyan radiance, followed the moment the cut occurs by a dominant, thunderous flap of massive feathered wings slicing through the air and the sharp clinking of dark armor plates against whistling high-altitude winds.
non_diegetic_music: Sustained low orchestral brass and Tibetan singing bowl resonance create a steady, menacing atmospheric drone at a slow tempo, maintaining tension without rhythm or percussive beats.

反例

切镜后人物和甲胄改为立体建模与写实反光，第二镜持续如此。

反例原始 prompt

东方玄幻动画。第一镜用二维手绘、硬边色块和轮廓线：赤足白衣人物悬浮在神殿，丝绸随气流飘，青蓝光自体内扩散，顶光照亮眉心朱砂和双臂金璎珞。听到悠远梵音、金属共振。镜头骤切到高空俯视的黑翼女子，黑翼展开、暗金甲胄逆光勾边，低沉冷声说：“你以为觉醒，就能逆天？”切镜瞬间转为立体三维CG，女子和甲胄出现体积建模、写实材质反光与景深，第二镜一直保持三维。

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] 2D-animated, a medium-wide shot opens with the camera slowly executing a push in inside a celestial temple. 03-05 · 时序一致性 · 色彩／光影不一致-P0

咖啡馆窗光的方向

考点切镜后主光相对人物是否换了方向？
观察方法用同一扇窗和女子的朝向作参照，比较发缘及鼻影，排除仅仅改变摄影机角度。

正例

同一扇窗始终从背后照亮发缘，脸正面留在柔影里。

正例原始 prompt

咖啡馆舒缓纯音乐。戴眼镜的西装男子进门扫视，走向一位背对落地窗坐着的女士，她手边有黑色手包和厚牛皮纸信封。中远景收至中景，男子整理领口；过女士肩拍男子近景：“太太……您怎么约在这儿？”切女士近景，她浅笑：“坐。”开头和后续女士近景都以她身后的同一扇窗为主光，发丝边缘亮、脸正面在柔和阴影中。 过男子肩看她把信封推至桌中央，信封特写。她说：“我要你做我的眼线。”男子推眼镜，低声说：“这不合适……被总裁发现我就完了。”她站起逼近半步，压低声：“你忘了是谁把你留到现在的？”双人中景，他低头，两人都没碰信封。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium wide shot tracking into a medium shot as a slender Asian man in his early thirties with wire-rimmed glasses, wearing a charcoal wool tailored suit and white collared shirt, enters through the glass door of a quiet cafe. He pauses mid-stride, his eyes darting across the warmly lit interior of dark walnut tables and potted monstera plants, before locking onto a sophisticated woman sitting alone at a booth table in the background. She sits with her back turned toward a rainy floor-to-ceiling window, draped in an ivory silk trench coat, with a structured black leather handbag and a thick kraft paper envelope resting on the polished tabletop beside her porcelain coffee cup. As the man approaches her table, he nervously extends a finger to tug and adjust his stiff necktie and collar. [Shot 2] At 00:03.200, the camera cuts to a close-up over the woman's shoulder framing the suited man (S1), who stops beside the empty leather chair opposite her, nervously shifting his weight and whispering anxiously, as the adult male on-screen speaker with glasses (S1) says, <d>[Chinese] 太太……您怎么约在这儿？</d> [Shot 3] At 00:05.400, the shot transitions to a close-up of the elegant mature Asian woman in her late thirties (S2) sitting opposite him, framed from the front with the floor-to-ceiling window serving as the primary backlight behind her, creating a delicate glowing rim along her shoulder-length wavy dark hair while her composed facial features remain in soft, diffuse shadow. overall_soundscape: A gentle ambient cafe hum with quiet rain pattering against glass is audible throughout, accompanied by the distinct muffled chime of the entrance door swinging closed. Crisp footsteps on polished hardwood floor lead into the subtle dry rustle of suit wool as the man adjusts his tie. A soft sliding friction sound is clearly heard as the paper envelope scrapes across the polished wooden table, followed by a faint creak of leather as the woman abruptly stands up.
non_diegetic_music: A mellow, slow-tempo solo jazz piano plays gentle, sparse chords with warm acoustic reverb, maintaining an understated and steady dynamic throughout without swelling.

反例

女子仍坐原位，近景却突然从左脸受光，鼻影转向右侧。

反例原始 prompt

咖啡馆舒缓纯音乐。戴眼镜的西装男子进门扫视，走向一位背对落地窗坐着的女士，她手边有黑色手包和厚牛皮纸信封。中远景收至中景，男子整理领口；过女士肩拍男子近景：“太太……您怎么约在这儿？”切女士近景，她浅笑：“坐。”开头女士明显背光，后续近景中她未挪座，房间也未改灯，主光却突然改从她左脸侧面照入，鼻影转向右侧。 过男子肩看她把信封推至桌中央，信封特写。她说：“我要你做我的眼线。”男子推眼镜，低声说：“这不合适……被总裁发现我就完了。”她站起逼近半步，压低声：“你忘了是谁把你留到现在的？”双人中景，他低头，两人都没碰信封。

03-10 · 时序一致性 · 场景不一致

转圈时冰场换了场景

考点同一次转圈能否保持在同一冰场？
观察方法在6–7秒核查场景的固定建筑和背景，而不仅是角度不同。

正例

切后仍看得到室内冰场的红看台和黄栏杆。

正例原始 prompt

一名短发选手穿白色滑冰鞋，在有红色看台、黄色护栏的室内冰场优雅滑行和转圈；清亮普通话说：“滑冰真好玩”。6–7秒从正面全景切到侧面跟拍，转圈的身体方向和动作连续。切镜后仍能看到同一室内冰场的红看台和黄栏杆。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, wide shot at eye level with a static framing. Centered in a spacious indoor ice arena, a young female figure skater with chin-length straight black hair and an athletic build (S1) wears a long-sleeved navy-blue figure skating dress trimmed with subtle rhinestones and crisp white leather skates with polished silver blades. The smooth white ice sheet beneath her reflects high-bay overhead arena floodlights. In the background, tiered empty red spectator bleachers rise behind bright yellow dasher board railings bordering the perimeter. Gliding forward along a gentle arc toward the camera, the young skater (S1) smiles cheerfully and says in a clear, bright tone, <d>[Chinese] 滑冰真好玩</d>. Immediately after finishing her sentence, she sweeps her arms outward, bends her knees slightly, and pulls into a centered upright spin on her left skate, whipping up a fine mist of ice powder. [Shot 2] At 00:06.500, the camera cuts to a medium-wide side shot that executes a tracking shot to follow her movement from the side. The audio seamlessly continues from the previous shot. The young skater from Shot 1 continues her rotational spin without interruption, her rotation direction and kinetic momentum perfectly preserved across the cut as her arms pull tight against her chest to accelerate the turn. Behind her, the red bleachers and yellow railings from Shot 1 sweep past in a smooth horizontal blur. After several rapid rotations, she opens her arms wide and extends her free leg straight back into a graceful one-foot edge glide, sailing smoothly across the glossy rink surface.
overall_soundscape: The crisp, hollow room tone of a vast indoor ice rink fills the space, underscored by the sharp, pronounced scraping hiss of steel blades slicing into fresh ice. Distinct carving sounds echo off the perimeter as the skater edges forward, followed by an audible, rhythmic whirr and fabric flutter as the spin accelerates. A clean, distinct gliding swoosh is clearly heard as she steps out into an extended one-foot glide.
non_diegetic_music: A light and airy classical piece featuring solo acoustic piano and gentle sustained chamber strings, moving at an allegretto tempo with a steady, lilting rhythm that maintains an even dynamic level without sudden swells.

反例

转圈尚未结束，场地已换成雪山下的露天冰湖和松林。

反例原始 prompt

03-11 · 时序一致性 · 尾帧突变-P0

最后一帧的塔楼

考点塔楼是否恰好只在最后一帧消失？
观察方法对照最后两个实际画面，先确认倒数第二帧塔身和屋顶还在，再检查末帧。若提前消失或逐渐淡出，记录实际发生的变化。

正例

倒数第二帧和最后一帧都保留完整塔楼。

正例原始 prompt

写实奇幻建筑，固定中景。塔基起建，再由下而上出现塔身与内部空间，外部同步完善，最终整座塔楼完整入画。木料碰撞、石块摩擦与轻风声伴搭建渐强；完成时一记轻微辉光音效。无对白、无音乐。成塔后定格，最后一帧仍是完整塔楼及原来的光影。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a medium shot at eye level, the camera maintains a static shot framing an open grassy clearing surrounded by distant pine-covered hills under a soft overcast sky with filtered daylight. Initially, an empty weathered patch of soil sits in the center of the frame. Heavy ashlar granite blocks rapidly emerge and slide together with architectural precision, assembling into a broad octagonal foundation bordered by chisel-marked stone steps. Rising continuously from bottom to top, massive dark oak timber pillars and interlocking slate masonry shoot upward, revealing cross-sections of interior spiraling stone staircases, arched alcoves, and oak floorboards just before thick outer stone walls enclose them. The exterior swiftly details itself in tandem, featuring carved buttresses, narrow arched lancet windows with leaded diamond-paned glass, and wrought-iron sconces mounted along the facade. As the vertical construction reaches its apex, an ornate conical roof shingled with slate tiles and crowned with a bronze finial locks into position, bringing the entire fantasy tower to a complete, towering state centered in the frame. A soft golden luminescence sweeps across the masonry before settling, and the scene holds in a prolonged freeze on the completed tower, maintaining the identical natural lighting, steady shadows, and tranquil mountain backdrop through to the final frame.
overall_soundscape: A distinct hollow clatter of dense timber beams locking together mingles with pronounced grinding and gritty friction of sliding stone blocks, punctuated by an audible whistling breeze that steadily swells in volume as the structure rises. Upon completion, a clear, delicate chime and soft ethereal shimmer tone resounds in the foreground, before settling into faint ambient wind rustling through the distant pine trees.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

04-05 · 美感与画质 · 颗粒感／噪点／杂质／闪烁

白毛衣女孩练琴

考点墙面亮斑和衣服、琴盖上的黑白点是否逐帧闪现？
观察方法先用前 4 秒认清墙面、白毛衣和深色琴盖。再回放第 4–8 秒，分别找大片亮斑的闪灭和小黑白点的换位。

正例

墙面亮度稳定，白毛衣和深色琴盖上都没有跳闪的小点。

正例原始 prompt

生成8秒日式写实生活短片，平视中景从钢琴一侧缓缓环绕至另一侧四分之三视角。年轻女子穿白毛衣坐在琴前，双拳收在胸前给自己打气，轻声说「もう一回だけ…」。她用右手按下一个清澈的单音，左手随后落键，接出温柔流动的旋律，专注平静，肩随旋律轻轻起伏。窗户侧逆光勾出头发和后背的轮廓，保留是枝裕和式的生活观察感。
从她开口到结尾，背景墙、毛衣阴影和琴身的暗部都干净稳定，窗边轮廓光柔和连续。

正例最终 PE prompt

04-06 · 美感与画质 · 颗粒感／噪点／杂质／闪烁

布偶猫巡逻的客厅

考点首尾镜头的平整墙面是否出现杂质？
观察方法在首尾两镜的平整墙面找黑斑，不把猫爬架下方的接触阴影算进去。

正例

米白墙面干净，爬架旁只有与日光同向的柔影。

正例原始 prompt

生成一段温暖明亮的布偶猫爬架广告，所有画外音为同一位温柔的日语女声，每句同步显示相同的日语字幕。镜头一贴近木地板快速向前，扫地机器人穿过客厅，旁白「毎日、この小さな世界をパトロールするのが私の日課。」镜头二仰拍布偶猫抬头，视线牵出墙上原木色猫爬架，旁白「でも、一番気になるのは…あの上。」镜头三跟拍猫跃上踏板、沿剑麻柱攀爬，旁白「一歩、また一歩。木の温もりを感じながら。」镜头四从下方特写透明亚克力太空舱，猫蜷成一团，肉垫与蓬松肚皮轻压透明底，旁白「ふわふわ、ぽかぽか。ここが私の特等席。」镜头五拉远定格：它在高处俯视客厅，右侧出现原木色手写英文“Their Own Little Universe.”，旁白「見守っててね、私たちの小さな宇宙から。」
开头沿地板巡视时，以及结尾拉远时，米白墙面均匀洁净。原木踏板会在墙上投下与日光方向一致的柔影。

04-11 · 美感与画质 · 油腻感／蜡像感

拆银色眼膜的博主

考点未贴眼膜的皮肤是否也呈蜡面油光？
观察方法只看额头和另一侧未贴膜的脸颊，排除产品本身合理的湿润反光。

正例

眼膜有水光，旁边的脸颊仍看得到毛孔。

正例原始 prompt

12秒写实中文口播。20岁白人女博主在浅灰墙、白门框的家里，微笑着展示一对透明包装的银色弯月眼膜。0–3秒把包装靠近镜头：“姐妹们，我最近熬夜真的靠这个眼膜救急。”3–6秒切脸部近景，撕封取出一片贴于眼下，按稳边缘：“它是这种很服帖的水润质地，贴上去冰冰凉凉的。”6–9秒眼部特写，以指腹抚平眼膜。9–12秒半身，她轻拍脸：“想试的可以蹲一下活动，日常囤着真的挺方便。”语速口语化，中文口型同步，自然景深，8K人像细节。
每次切近都能看见鼻翼和面颊细微毛孔。眼膜薄薄一层水光与周围自然皮肤的光泽有清楚区别。

正例最终 PE prompt

反例

额头和未贴膜脸颊也被抹平，转头时一整片厚亮高光一起滑动。

反例原始 prompt

12秒写实中文口播。20岁白人女博主在浅灰墙、白门框的家里，微笑着展示一对透明包装的银色弯月眼膜。0–3秒把包装靠近镜头：“姐妹们，我最近熬夜真的靠这个眼膜救急。”3–6秒切脸部近景，撕封取出一片贴于眼下，按稳边缘：“它是这种很服帖的水润质地，贴上去冰冰凉凉的。”6–9秒眼部特写，以指腹抚平眼膜。9–12秒半身，她轻拍脸：“想试的可以蹲一下活动，日常囤着真的挺方便。”语速口语化，中文口型同步，自然景深，8K人像细节。
从开场到结尾，把额头、双颊和鼻子渲染成抛光蜡面，毛孔被抹平，一整片黏亮的高光随转头滑动，脸上没贴眼膜的地方也像涂了厚油。眼膜本身仍半透明，包装细节清楚。

04-12 · 美感与画质 · 油腻感／蜡像感

甲板对手的头发

考点白衣男子转头时，发丝是否粘成蜡状硬条？
观察方法错例出在白衣男子的头发，时间约5秒，光头海盗不作此项判断。

正例

额前发束粗细不同，散发随风动，高光随各束变化。

正例原始 prompt

15秒电影甲板决斗。0–4秒甲板中景，光头海盗扑向白衣男子，两人兵器相接，船身和索具摇晃。4–8秒切低机位近景，白衣男子转身反击，神情凌厉，海风吹过额前头发。8–12秒从围栏后拍全景，两人周旋，围观者探身，海盗侧滚闪开。12–15秒海盗背后过肩，白衣男子侧身制住对手，两人停下。
第5秒白衣男子旋身时，额前发束有粗细变化，几根散发跟着风动，高光沿发丝自然变化。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a medium shot framed at eye level, the camera shakes slightly with the rhythmic pitch and roll of the wooden ship. On the salt-stained oak deck under bright overcast daylight, a burly, weathered bald pirate wearing a tattered brown leather vest and dark breeches lunges fiercely from the right midground toward the left. Standing in the center midground, an agile young adult man dressed in a loose-fitting white linen poet shirt and dark trousers raises his silver cutlass to parry. Their blades collide with a violent shower of sparks. Above them, thick hemp rigging, deadeyes, and heavy canvas sail corners oscillate against a turbulent gray-blue sea sky. [Shot 2] At 00:04.000, the camera cuts to a low-angle close-up that tilts up as the man in white from Shot 1 executes a swift, balanced turn to counterattack. His brow is sharply furrowed, jaw set in a fierce glare, as gusting ocean wind catches his dark hair. The bangs falling across his forehead show nuanced variations in strand thickness, with delicate loose hairs whipping in the wind while sharp natural daylight glints and shifts dynamically along the individual hair shafts as he spins. [Shot 3] At 00:08.000, the shot transitions to a wide shot filmed from behind the ship's weathered wooden balustrade and coiled mooring ropes in the foreground, panning right across the deck. In the open midground, the man in white and the bald pirate from Shot 1 circle each other warily with weapons raised, until the pirate drops and executes a rapid sideways roll across the planks to evade a thrusting slash. In the background, several scruffy crew members in drab bandanas and patched tunics lean intently over wooden barrels and railings to track the duel. [Shot 4] At 00:12.000, the camera cuts to an over-the-shoulder medium close-up from directly behind the muscular shoulder of the bald pirate from Shot 1, pushing in slowly. The man in white pivots sideways into a decisive disarming lock, his blade pinned firmly against the pirate's weapon crossguard at neck level. Both combatants freeze in a rigid stalemate, their chests heaving with panting breath as sea spray mists lightly between them.
overall_soundscape: A loud metallic clang resonates sharply as the two heavy steel blades clash, accompanied by the distinct creaking and groaning of timber and the pronounced whistling of sea winds through the swaying rigging. As the man pivots and the pirate rolls, the rapid thud and scuff of leather boots against the gritty oak deck planks are clearly heard, followed by the audible flapping of linen fabric and heavy canvas. The final confrontation concludes with a distinct, harsh scraping of locked metal edges and the audible heavy panting of the two combatants.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

反例

约第 5 秒头发粘成同宽硬条，一整片油亮高光随转头滑过。

反例原始 prompt

15秒电影甲板决斗。0–4秒甲板中景，光头海盗扑向白衣男子，两人兵器相接，船身和索具摇晃。4–8秒切低机位近景，白衣男子转身反击，神情凌厉，海风吹过额前头发。8–12秒从围栏后拍全景，两人周旋，围观者探身，海盗侧滚闪开。12–15秒海盗背后过肩，白衣男子侧身制住对手，两人停下。
第5秒旋身近景中，白衣男子的头发表面忽然像一整块抹油的黑蜡，发丝粘合成同宽的硬条，转头时一整片高光跟着滑。脸、衣料、海风和船体运动仍自然；切全景后恢复自然头发。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a medium shot at eye level as the camera shakes slightly with the roll of the vessel, capturing a wooden ship deck on an overcast day. On the left, a burly, middle-aged bald pirate wearing a weathered brown leather vest and dark roughspun trousers lunges forward aggressively, gripping a heavy iron cutlass in his right hand. 04-13 · 美感与画质 · 材质／场景细节不足

鸡胸肉变猫粮

考点变身前的鸡胸肉有没有可辨的食材纹理？
观察方法看碰撞前鸡胸肉表面能否辨认纤维，不把金光转场算进材质问题。

正例

微距能看到肌纤维走向和不均匀的湿润反光。

正例原始 prompt

写实高端宠物食品广告。开场微距，一块新鲜生三文鱼和一块干净生鸡胸肉悬起，两者在空中碰撞。旁白：“将2种极致的营养，凝聚成100%的纯粹。”金色光芒流过后变成颗颗猫粮，掉入猫碗。小猫跑来吃，镜头拉远，客厅地板上有一袋猫粮，虚化背景前渐显中文“一口纯粹 宠爱一生”。
开头微距可见鸡胸肉浅粉色的肌纤维走向、薄膜与不均匀的水润反光；旁边三文鱼油纹分明。

正例最终 PE prompt

[Shot 2] At 00:05.500, the camera cuts to a medium close-up with a slight tilt down. The golden luminescence dissipates as the camera tilts down smoothly, tracking the cluster of dry kibble pieces as they tumble and scatter neatly into a shallow matte off-white ceramic cat bowl resting on a sunlit light-oak floor. 反例

鸡胸肉表面均一，只有塑料般的亮斑，旁边三文鱼仍有油纹。

04-14 · 美感与画质 · 比例失调／透视异常

冰湖上的极寒继承者

考点退镜时是否只有男子的腿被拉长？
观察方法在7.2秒前后看同一男子的头、躯干与腿，区别于全景整体缩小。

正例

头、上身和腿随退镜一起等比缩小。

正例原始 prompt

冬夜校园，月光落在结冰的湖上，冰层泛着冷蓝光。极寒继承者是一名手握手链的男子，站在湖面中央低声用日语说「静寂に還る。」岸上的火焰对照者点起橙焰，另一种冷静的嗓音回应「寒き夜を焼き尽くす。」镜头退到能看清两人站位的全身景别，寒霜沿湖面扩散，和火焰迎面碰撞。画面持续越过第7.2秒。
从第7.2秒到冲撞结束，湖中男子头身与四肢比例自然，双脚贴着冰面，镜头后退时整体等比变小。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, eye-level framing as the camera pulls out smoothly from a medium wide view into an expansive wide shot across a frozen campus lake on a clear winter night. In the center midground, standing flat-footed on the reflective ice surface, is the ice inheritor, a young adult male on-screen speaker (S1) in his early twenties with a slender build, tousled ash-grey hair dusted with frost, dressed in a tailored navy-blue woolen trench coat, dark trousers, and black leather boots. His right hand clutches an intricate silver beaded bracelet that emits a faint crystalline glint under the pale moonlight falling from high above, bathing the cracked ice in cold blue luminescence. Gothic campus stone buildings with arched windows stand silhouetted against the indigo night sky in the far background behind bare, snow-dusted willow trees. The young adult male on-screen speaker with a calm, low voice (S1) lowers his gaze toward the bracelet and murmurs, <d>[Japanese] 静寂に還る。</d> He then raises his head with steady resolve, his knuckles whitening around the beads as sharp, intricate frost patterns rapidly branch out across the ice sheet beneath his boots. On the stone shoreline at frame right, stands the flame counterpart, a young adult male on-screen speaker with a composed, steady voice (S2) of athletic build with short dark burgundy hair, clad in a charcoal-grey high-collar overcoat and rugged dark boots planted firmly on the snowbanks. Facing the frozen lake, the young adult male on-screen counterpart (S2) raises his right hand, snapping his gloved fingers to kindle a blazing sphere of vivid orange flame, and calmly responds, <d>[Japanese] 寒き夜を焼き尽くす。</d> As the camera pulls out continuously, revealing both subjects in full-body framing while their proportions scale down proportionately against the vast frozen lake, the male on the ice (S1) stands grounded as sheets of blue frost surge forward across the ice, meeting a roaring surge of orange fire charging from the shore, colliding head-on into a violent geyser of hissing steam, erupting sparks, and shattered crystalline fragments.
overall_soundscape: A gentle winter wind whistles through bare campus branches, punctuated by the audible metallic clink of silver bracelet beads. A deep resonant hum echoes as frost fissures crackle through the ice surface, followed by a loud, sudden whoosh of igniting flame that transitions into a dominant, explosive foreground crash and sustained hiss as the roaring fire and freezing frost violently collide.
non_diegetic_music: Sustained orchestral cellos and low contrabass, slow tempo, accompanied by subtle crystalline chimes that steadily build with rolling cinematic percussion into a dense, climactic swell.

反例

从 7.2 秒起腿单独变长，头和上身比例照旧，脚仍落在冰面。

反例原始 prompt

冬夜校园，月光落在结冰的湖上，冰层泛着冷蓝光。极寒继承者是一名手握手链的男子，站在湖面中央低声用日语说「静寂に還る。」岸上的火焰对照者点起橙焰，另一种冷静的嗓音回应「寒き夜を焼き尽くす。」镜头退到能看清两人站位的全身景别，寒霜沿湖面扩散，和火焰迎面碰撞。画面持续越过第7.2秒。
从第7.2秒起，湖中男子的双腿从膝上开始像被向下拉长，腿长明显超过正常身形，头、上身和手链保持原大小，脚仍落在同一冰面。岸上人物和环境透视正常，台词与霜火对撞照常。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, wide shot at eye level as the camera pulls out at slow speed with large amplitude across the expanse of a frozen university campus lake on a clear winter night. In the center of the frame on the frozen water stands a pale young adult male inheritor of cold (S1), dressed in a dark navy tailored woolen overcoat, charcoal trousers, and black winter boots. 04-15a · 美感与画质 · 特效／后期包装粗糙

妖王升起时的冰面

考点开场冰纹有没有重复铺贴的痕迹？
观察方法看妖王出场时地表重复的贴片格与锯齿边，落地段用来对照。

正例

冰纹随裂缝不规则分叉，能看出厚薄和下层纹理。

正例原始 prompt

冰雪奇幻短片。冰魇妖王从冰裂中缓缓升起，巨大冰甲与眼中蓝火映在雪峰上，寒气扩散，地面冻结，伴随清脆碎裂声，他低声道：“此地，永封。”白衣寒山剑修踏雪从高处而下，衣袍猎猎，落地时没有扬尘，雪面无声冻结。他抬手凝出透明霜月长剑：“封得住山，封不住人心。”随后银灰冰霜圆环从他脚下展开，与蓝火寒流对撞，冰晶飞散，远处冰殿折射寒光，冰裂与风暴轰鸣交织。
妖王升起的开场，冰纹从裂缝边缘向外不规则分叉，可透过薄冰看到下层纹理；剑修落地的冻结也沿积雪高低蔓延。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, low-angle medium-wide shot as the camera tilts up slowly from a yawning glacial abyss toward towering jagged snow peaks under a tempestuous indigo twilight. Emerging from the deep, glowing crevasse in the center of the frame, the Ice Fiend King (S1)—a colossal male demonic entity clad in massive translucent spires of spiked ice plate armor over a torso of hardened glacial frost, his craggy face chiseled from blue permafrost with twin cerulean flames burning intensely inside cavernous eye sockets—rises upward. Below him, irregular frost fractures spiderweb outward from the rift edges across the permafrost, revealing crystalline strata beneath the transparent upper ice. A dense fog of freezing vapor billows across the terrain, freezing the ground solid as sharp ice needles crystallize on rock surfaces. As blue light glints off his jagged pauldrons, the Ice Fiend King (S1) lowers his head slightly and grunts in a cavernous, rumbling baritone, <d>[Chinese] 此地，永封。</d> [Shot 2] At 00:05.200, the camera cuts to a high-angle medium shot, tracking downward smoothly as a youthful male cultivator (S2) dressed in immaculate white layered silk-and-linen robes with fluttering wide sleeves and silver embroidery glides gracefully downward from an icy precipice on the upper left. His long black hair is gathered in a silver jade crown, trailing behind him in the alpine gale. He lands soundlessly on a windswept snow bank in the foreground center without displacing any loose powder; instantly, intricate frost patterns crawl silently along the undulating contours of the snow drifts beneath his white cloth boots. Straightening his posture with a composed gaze, the swordsman (S2) raises his right hand into the air, condensing sparkling cold mist into a translucent frost-moon longsword that hums faintly with condensed qi, and declares in a clear, resonant voice, <d>[Chinese] 封得住山，封不住人心。</d> [Shot 3] At 00:10.000, the shot transitions to an expansive wide shot as the camera pushes in steadily along the central axis of the frozen valley. The white-clad swordsman (S2) stands firmly in the foreground right, while the colossal Ice Fiend King (S1) looms in the midground left. A gleaming silver-gray ring of concentric frost glyphs erupts outward from beneath the swordsman's feet across the snow, surging forward until it violently collides against an oncoming wall of azure flame and raging blizzard mist unleashed by the demon king. Brilliant hexagonal ice shards and luminous crystalline fragments detonate upward in a violent burst across the screen, illuminated by the distant spires of a monumental translucent ice palace in the background that reflects sharp, cold highlights amidst the swirling vortex.
overall_soundscape: A deep, resonant glacial groaning echoes constantly, paired with the pronounced crisp clatter and sharp cracking of tearing ice sheets as frost spreads across stone. The subtle, quiet whoosh of fabric cutting through the wind transitions into a distinct crystalline hiss as frost freezes the snow layer, followed by the metallic chime of weapon condensation and a dominant, thunderous concussive boom when the frost circles collide with the azure blizzard, accompanied by the bright clatter of spraying ice crystals.
non_diegetic_music: Low sustained orchestral brass drones anchored by heavy taiko drums and bowed contrabass, maintaining a measured tempo that accelerates into a sharp percussive strike at the visual clash.

反例

妖王升起时冰面重复出现同一组低清冰花，贴片边缘有锯齿。

反例原始 prompt

冰雪奇幻短片。冰魇妖王从冰裂中缓缓升起，巨大冰甲与眼中蓝火映在雪峰上，寒气扩散，地面冻结，伴随清脆碎裂声，他低声道：“此地，永封。”白衣寒山剑修踏雪从高处而下，衣袍猎猎，落地时没有扬尘，雪面无声冻结。他抬手凝出透明霜月长剑：“封得住山，封不住人心。”随后银灰冰霜圆环从他脚下展开，与蓝火寒流对撞，冰晶飞散，远处冰殿折射寒光，冰裂与风暴轰鸣交织。
只在开场妖王升起的冻结镜头，把冰面做成一张低分辨率蓝白贴纸，边缘锯齿明显，同一块冰花图案整齐重复铺开。剑修落地时改为有厚薄变化、顺着雪面分叉的冰纹，后续结界与蓝火精细连贯。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a wide shot with a slow Pedestal Up movement reveals an abyssal chasm cleaving a desolate, snow-covered mountain peak under twilight tempest skies. From the deep fissure, an immense, towering ice demon king (S1) emerges, his massive armored silhouette carved from jagged cerulean glacial plates, with intense azure flames flickering inside his hollow eye sockets, casting eerie blue specular highlights onto the surrounding snowy crags. Across the foreground snow, freezing frost spreads outward from the chasm, rendered distinctly as a flat, low-resolution blue-and-white sticker texture with visibly jagged, pixelated aliased edges and a repetitive grid pattern of identical stylized snowflake decals stamping across the ground. Thick pale mist rolls down his bulky shoulders as the towering ice demon king (S1) slightly tilts his craggy head and mutters in a deep, gravelly bass, <d>[Chinese] 此地，永封。</d> [Shot 2] At 00:05.200, the camera cuts to a medium-long shot tracking smoothly downward as a youthful male swordsman in white (S2), sporting a slender athletic build and clad in fluttering, multi-layered snow-white silk robes with pale silver trimmed cuffs, steps gracefully out of the upper gale. His lustrous black hair, partially bound by a pale jade crown, whips fiercely behind him, yet his cloth boots touch the snowbank with zero dust kicked up. Instantly beneath his soles, a complex network of hyper-detailed, organic frost veins spiderwebs outward with varied thickness, branching naturally through the powdery snow. He calmly lifts his right hand forward, gathering suspended moisture into a pristine, translucent frost-moon longsword that solidifies with razor-sharp crystalline facets. The energetic impact detonates into thousands of hyper-fine, sparkling diamond-like ice crystals and violent blizzard squalls. In the far background, a towering ancient palace of translucent blue ice atop a distant cliff refracts piercing cold beams from the collision, while deep fissures violently tear through the valley floor beneath the roaring vortex.
overall_soundscape: The scene opens with a dominant howling alpine blizzard wind and the low, tectonic grinding rumble of opening ice fissures. A pronounced synthetic crunch precedes a crisp, hyper-realistic crackle of organic frost fracturing across the snow, accompanied by the distinct, audible flutter of fine silk robes in the high-altitude gale. A sharp, ethereal crystalline chime rings out loudly as the sword materializes, culminating in a deafening concussive explosion and a violent, rushing roar as the frost barrier violently clashes against the torrential stream of supernatural blue fire.
non_diegetic_music: A low orchestral brass drone coupled with sustained sub-bass pulses, transitioning into rapid, staccato cello ostinatos and resonant cinematic war drums that steadily swell to an intense peak.

04-15b · 美感与画质 · 特效／后期包装粗糙

剑修落地后雪面冻结

考点落地时的冻结边沿是否贴合积雪？
观察方法看男子落地后脚边冻结的边沿与雪面是否服帖，区别于稍后真正的空中结界。

正例

细霜从靴边沿着雪的高低起伏蔓延。

正例原始 prompt

冰雪奇幻短片。冰魇妖王从冰裂中缓缓升起，巨大冰甲与眼中蓝火映在雪峰上，寒气扩散，地面冻结，伴随清脆碎裂声，他低声道：“此地，永封。”白衣寒山剑修踏雪从高处而下，衣袍猎猎，落地时没有扬尘，雪面无声冻结。他抬手凝出透明霜月长剑：“封得住山，封不住人心。”随后银灰冰霜圆环从他脚下展开，与蓝火寒流对撞，冰晶飞散，远处冰殿折射寒光，冰裂与风暴轰鸣交织。
妖王出场时冰层自然龟裂。接着剑修落地，靴边先现细霜，冰纹依据雪面高低向外生长，同现实积雪连在一起。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, low-angle medium wide shot as the camera tilts up at slow speed. In a desolate, wind-swept glacial gorge flanked by jagged permafrost peaks, an enormous crevasse cleaves the frozen earth. Emerging slowly from the dark chasm is the Ice Demon King (S1), an imposing male spectral monarch encased in jagged, translucent dark-cyan ice plate armor, with spiky ice pauldrons and frost riming his collar. Inside his cavernous horned helmet, two incandescent azure flames blaze in place of eyes, casting shimmering blue reflections across the surrounding snow-clad peaks. Crystalline fracture lines visibly crack and fissure across the solid ice floor around the fissure edge, throwing up tiny glittering ice fragments. As freezing mist billows outward from his colossal shoulders, the ice demon king (S1) lowers his head slightly and grunts in a deep, gravelly voice, <d>[Chinese] 此地，永封。</d> [Shot 2] At 00:04.800, the camera cuts to a medium shot and tracks downward to follow a graceful descent before leveling off. A slender young adult male swordsman (S2) with long raven hair gathered into a silver guan and dressed in billowing white silk robes with subtle icy-blue wave embroidery drifts down from a high cliff ridge. His sleeves and sash flutter dynamically in the freezing gale, yet he touches down on the snowdrift with absolute weightlessness, generating zero billowing dust or displacement. The moment his dark embroidered leather boots make contact, fine white rime nucleates instantly beneath his soles, sprouting intricate dendritic frost ribbons that spread along the natural contours of the snow pack to merge with the existing crust. Raising his right palm smoothly toward the center of the frame, concentrated condensation solidifies into a transparent, luminous Frost Moon longsword glistening with diamond sharpness. Looking coolly toward the fissure, the swordsman (S2) replies firmly, <d>[Chinese] 封得住山，封不住人心。</d> [Shot 3] At 00:09.800, the shot transitions to a wide shot as the camera pushes in at medium speed. The swordsman from Shot 2 stands in the foreground left, while the towering ice demon king from Shot 1 looms in the midground right. Perched on sheer precipices in the distant background, monolithic spires of an ancient crystalline ice palace refract harsh white and indigo alpine light. A brilliant silver-gray ring of frosty geometric energy expands radially across the terrain from beneath the swordsman's feet, hurtling across the snow. Simultaneously, the demon king thrusts a massive claw forward, releasing a churning torrent of blue-flamed glacial blizzard. The two opposing fronts clash violently at mid-screen in an explosive shockwave, spraying blinding clouds of shattered ice crystals into the air as the howling blizzard churns across the mountain pass.
overall_soundscape: A crisp, high-pitched crackle of shattering ice rings out distinct and loud as deep fissures split open, followed by a resonant low rumbling thud as the heavy armor rises through the rift. The gentle, silent descent of the swordsman is met with a distinct crystalline hiss as frost threads spread across the crunchy snow crust. The physical condensation of the sword produces an audible metallic chime, which transitions into a deafening boom and loud roaring crash as the frost circle and blue storm collide, showering the scene with the pronounced clatter of falling ice shards against the frozen mountain face.
non_diegetic_music: Low brass drone layered with slow, sustained cello harmonies and rhythmic taiko percussion, gradually accelerating in tempo and swelling in dynamic intensity into a heavy orchestral brass and percussion cadence.

反例

硬边圆片从脚边等速放大，边沿跨过雪面起伏。

反例原始 prompt

冰雪奇幻短片。冰魇妖王从冰裂中缓缓升起，巨大冰甲与眼中蓝火映在雪峰上，寒气扩散，地面冻结，伴随清脆碎裂声，他低声道：“此地，永封。”白衣寒山剑修踏雪从高处而下，衣袍猎猎，落地时没有扬尘，雪面无声冻结。他抬手凝出透明霜月长剑：“封得住山，封不住人心。”随后银灰冰霜圆环从他脚下展开，与蓝火寒流对撞，冰晶飞散，远处冰殿折射寒光，冰裂与风暴轰鸣交织。
开场冰裂与后段结界都精细。只在剑修落地后的无声结冰过程中，让一个扁平、硬边的白蓝色圆片从靴底等速放大，纹理像一张复制的图片，横跨起伏雪面却不随地形变化。

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a low-angle medium shot where the camera tilts up as a massive demonic sovereign, the Ice Demon King (S1), ascends steadily from a yawning glacial chasm amidst jagged blue-tinted ice cliffs. The instrumentation swells steadily with suspended brass and airy choral pads, culminating in an intense, sustained percussion hit during the magical barrier impact.

04-17 · 美感与画质 · 色彩饱和度异常

水上乐园第一人称游览

考点画面是否整体过于浓艳？
观察方法同时看肤色、水面和道具，整体过艳区别于单个鲜色玩具。

正例

肤色、水面和滑梯各有自然的色彩深浅。

正例原始 prompt

皮克斯风格3D动画。第一人称视角沿水上乐园池边平稳向前，两侧游客在水里玩耍，孩子冲下滑梯，阳光照在水面。天空、水池、滑梯和人物皮肤呈现不同颜色。
色彩活泼，水面蓝色有深浅，彩色滑梯保持材质和阴影，游客皮肤温暖自然。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] 3D CG, wide shot at eye level in a continuous first-person POV, the camera smoothly pushes in along a glistening beige textured stone walkway beside an expansive water park pool under a brilliant cyan sky dotted with soft white stylized clouds. To the left, the sparkling pool water grades from translucent turquoise along the edge to rich cerulean in the deeper center, where several stylized visitors with warm, smooth skin tones, round friendly faces, and colorful swimwear wade and lounge on vibrant yellow-and-magenta striped inflatable tubes. To the right, towering intertwined spiral water slides crafted from glossy saturated yellow, lime green, and translucent orange fiberglass loop through the air, casting clean geometric shadows onto the sunlit deck and sun loungers. As the camera glides forward at an even walking pace, a young stylized boy with messy brown hair and bright blue swim trunks zooms out from the curved yellow slide chute, displacing a brilliant white curtain of water spray before landing with both arms raised in the splash basin. Nearby on the left pool ledge, a woman in a coral-red swimsuit adjusts her turquoise-framed sunglasses, turns her head to smile at the splash, and gently kicks her feet in the water as rippling sunlight caustics dance rhythmically across the submerged blue mosaic tiles.
overall_soundscape: A loud and distinct splash dominates the foreground as the boy plunges into the catch pool, accompanied by the pronounced hiss and swoosh of rushing water exiting the slide chute. The gentle, clearly heard sloshing and rhythmic lap of turquoise pool water against tiled gutters continues as the camera moves, followed by the audible trickle of dripping wet tiles along the walkway. Soft ambient murmurs of laughing crowd chatter and a sharp lifeguard whistle chirp echo naturally in the distant background.
non_diegetic_music: A lighthearted orchestral animation cue featuring fast-tempo marimba rhythms, plucked pizzicato strings, and breezy ukulele strumming, complemented by playful glockenspiel chimes and maintaining an even, buoyant volume throughout.

反例

三者连同阴影一起变得刺眼浓艳，影响整幅画面。
