04-18 · 美感与画质 · 表情ai感

惊恐女子的推轨变焦

考点惊恐表情的眼睑与虹膜关系是否自然？
观察方法看眼睑与虹膜之间的眼白是否大到违背真实表情，推轨造成的背景变形属于题面要求。

正例

眼睛逐渐睁大，眼睑仍自然贴着眼球。

正例原始 prompt

惊悚片近景，希区柯克式推轨变焦。女人突然听见意料之外的声响，转向镜头，身后走廊迅速拉伸变形，脸始终保持相近的画幅大小。
惊恐先从眉峰抬起和呼吸停顿显出，再缓慢睁大眼睛。眼睑仍自然贴着眼球，能看出她正在压住惊叫。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, close-up shot at eye level, centered on a woman in her early thirties with a pale, sweat-sheened complexion and dark brown hair loosely secured in a low, messy bun with stray wisps clinging to her temples, wearing a muted olive-green knitted wool cardigan over a cream ribbed cotton tank top. The camera pushes in at medium speed as the narrow hallway behind her rapidly stretches and distorts into exaggerated, elongated depth while her face remains consistently framed in close-up. Initially turned in three-quarter profile toward screen-left, she suddenly whips her head forward to stare directly into the lens. The corridor behind her is lined with peeling damask wallpaper, scuffed dark wood wainscoting, and closed paneled doors with tarnished brass handles receding into shadow. A single exposed bulb in an overhead pendant fixture casts harsh, pale yellowish light downward, producing sharp specular highlights on her forehead and casting deep hollows under her cheekbones. As the perspective warps, her inner brow peaks violently jerk upward into a tight, pained furrow, and her chest freezes mid-rise as her breathing abruptly stops. Her dark brown eyes slowly widen in paralyzed terror, her taut eyelids remaining naturally flush against her eyeballs. Her jaw locks tight and her pale lips press firmly together into a thin, quivering line, visibly straining to stifle a scream as a single drop of sweat trickles down her left temple.
overall_soundscape: A low, droning room tone and the subtle electrical buzz of an overhead bulb are suddenly shattered by a loud, metallic clank and a heavy wooden thud echoing from off-screen. A sharp, distinct fabric rustle is audible as the woman swiftly turns her neck and shoulders toward the camera. Her sudden, choked inhalation is clearly heard in the near field, followed by the pronounced, raspy sound of held breath vibrating softly in her throat.
non_diegetic_music: A solo cello sustains a low, abrasive pedal tone at a very slow tempo, gradually joined by dissonant, scraping violin harmonics that swell in volume. The high-register strings execute a slow microtonal slide upward without percussion or melodic resolution, sustaining a continuous harmonic tension.

反例

虹膜上下左右露出整圈夸张的眼白，并保持约两秒。

反例原始 prompt

惊悚片近景，希区柯克式推轨变焦。女人突然听见意料之外的声响，转向镜头，身后走廊迅速拉伸变形，脸始终保持相近的画幅大小。
女人确认声响的一瞬，双眼突然睁到不自然的极限并维持两秒；上下眼睑远离虹膜，双眼周围露出夸张的整圈眼白。头部形状、背景变焦和其他面部动作都自然。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, close-up framing at eye level, the camera pushes in at medium speed while zooming out to maintain her face at consistent framing while the hallway behind extends and warps in perspective. A woman in her early thirties with a gaunt face, pale complexion, and dark brown hair loosely pinned into a messy low bun with stray strands clinging to her temples stands centered in the midground, wearing an oversized sage-green ribbed knit cardigan over an off-white cotton undershirt. She is initially angled in profile within a narrow, claustrophobic corridor lined with faded damask wallpaper peeling along the dark mahogany baseboards, illuminated by warm tungsten wall sconces that cast harsh, deep shadows down the hallway toward a heavy closed door in the distant background. Suddenly startled by an unexpected noise off-screen, she whips her head and upper torso around to face directly toward the camera, her shoulders tensing instantly. As the corridor behind her rapidly elongates and deepens in distorted perspective, her face remains stable within the frame. The moment she registers and confirms the sound, her expression freezes in pure shock; her eyes suddenly snap open to an unnatural physical limit and hold rigidly for two full seconds, her upper and lower eyelids pulling dramatically away from both dark irises to expose an exaggerated, complete circular ring of white sclera around each eyeball, while her head contours, bone structure, and surrounding facial features remain anatomically normal. After the two-second hold, her eyelids begin to tremble slightly, her chest heaves with rapid shallow breaths, and a single bead of sweat rolls down her right temple as her lips part in silent dread, her wide, horrified gaze locked directly into the lens.
overall_soundscape: A faint electrical hum from the aged hallway sconces and a muted draft provide a low ambient foundation, interrupted by a distinct, loud metallic clatter and sudden floorboard creak echoing from the corridor behind. The quick fabric rustle of her wool cardigan is clearly heard as she turns abruptly, followed immediately by an audible sharp gasp of breath, pronounced rapid panting, and a distinct wet swallow in the foreground as her breathing catches.
non_diegetic_music: Sustained low contrabass drone paired with dissonant high-register bowed violin harmonics, maintaining a slow tempo with a subtle, steady volume swell.

04-22 · 美感与画质 · 光影扁无立体感

五场景时尚造型手册

考点脸和衣领是否有塑造体积的明暗转折？
观察方法看人脸和衣褶有无立体明暗。局部体积不足可与人物整体色温不匹配分开判断。

正例

鼻梁、脸颊、翻领都有与各场景相符的亮面和暗面。

正例原始 prompt

10秒，9:16竖屏时尚造型手册。一位女模特全程同一套米白短外套、深色长裤、尖头鞋，以优雅站姿略微转肩，风轻轻吹动衣摆。每两秒匹配剪辑一次人物剪影，依次是屋顶直升机停机坪、大理石博物馆走廊、霓虹游戏厅、沙漠公路、雾气松林。五个场景中人物造型、身形和服装细节一致。
各场景照明在模特身上形成清楚的体积：鼻梁与脸颊有连贯的明暗转折，翻领和袖褶有阴影；不同环境的光色准确映在她身上。

正例最终 PE prompt

overall_soundscape: A distinct gust of high-altitude wind whips audibly across the rooftop helipad along with a faint drone of distant urban traffic, abruptly cutting into the echoing acoustic space of a marble gallery with quiet room resonance and a soft rustle of wool fabric, followed by the pronounced electronic hum, clicks, and synthetic chimes of retro arcade machines, which immediately transitions into the howling rush of arid desert wind across open asphalt, before settling into the soft, quiet whisper of damp pine needles rustling in a cool forest breeze with distant muted woodland ambiance.
non_diegetic_music: A steady, minimal electronic runway groove driven by a warm analog synthesizer bassline, crisp four-on-the-floor kick, and subtle rhythmic hi-hat patterns, maintaining a consistent mid-tempo pulse with precise percussive downbeats accentuating each environment cut.

反例

服装颜色随场景改变，但多个镜头中脸颊、鼻梁和翻领都平得像色块。

反例原始 prompt

10秒，9:16竖屏时尚造型手册。一位女模特全程同一套米白短外套、深色长裤、尖头鞋，以优雅站姿略微转肩，风轻轻吹动衣摆。每两秒匹配剪辑一次人物剪影，依次是屋顶直升机停机坪、大理石博物馆走廊、霓虹游戏厅、沙漠公路、雾气松林。五个场景中人物造型、身形和服装细节一致。

04-24 · 美感与画质 · 整体偏色（发灰、发绿、发黄等）

督军别墅的家书

考点不同材质是否同时出现整体偏灰？
观察方法用面部、木扶手和大理石三处同时校验整体发灰，注意环境原有的暖色。

正例

可区分暖肤色、棕木扶手和偏冷的大理石。

正例原始 prompt

民国西式别墅一楼客厅，棕木楼梯、暖壁灯与大理石地面。三十岁的督军沈砚之推门入内，皮鞋声打破寂静。二十岁的督军太太苏晚在楼梯前僵住。沈砚之上前握住她的手臂，声音低沉冷硬：“我离城半月，日日派人送来书信，你一封都不曾回我。”苏晚转身甩开他的手，语气轻而坚韧：“督军公务繁忙，心里装着城池兵马，哪里有空在意我一封家书。前日城中流言传遍，说您新纳了戏子入公馆，满城人都看我的笑话，我又何必多言自讨没趣。”保持演员肤色、木材、石材三种可辨的色彩线索。
肤色有温度，深棕木材与偏冷的大理石区分清楚，暗部有层次，白色纹路干净。

正例最终 PE prompt

反例

三处同时蒙灰，原本的冷暖差别变弱，物体细节仍在。

反例原始 prompt

民国西式别墅一楼客厅，棕木楼梯、暖壁灯与大理石地面。三十岁的督军沈砚之推门入内，皮鞋声打破寂静。二十岁的督军太太苏晚在楼梯前僵住。沈砚之上前握住她的手臂，声音低沉冷硬：“我离城半月，日日派人送来书信，你一封都不曾回我。”苏晚转身甩开他的手，语气轻而坚韧：“督军公务繁忙，心里装着城池兵马，哪里有空在意我一封家书。前日城中流言传遍，说您新纳了戏子入公馆，满城人都看我的笑话，我又何必多言自讨没趣。”保持演员肤色、木材、石材三种可辨的色彩线索。
整条片统一蒙上一层明显的中性灰，人物皮肤、木扶手反光和壁灯都被冲淡，浓淡不同的实物颜色像隔着灰纱。整体曝光足够，两人的脸和细节仍清楚，没有局部脏斑。

Her chest heaves under the silk fabric as she squares her shoulders, turning her pale face directly toward him with tear-glazed yet unyielding eyes; the young woman (S2) retorts in a light, resolute tone, <d>[Chinese] 督军公务繁忙，心里装着城池兵马，哪里有空在意我一封家书。前日城中流言传遍，说您新纳了戏子入公馆，满城人都看我的笑话，我又何必多言自讨没趣。</d> As she concludes her line, she lifts her chin in quiet defiance, her lips pressing together tightly, while in the soft-focus foreground the governor's jaw visibly tenses in silent response under the muted neutral-grey lighting.
overall_soundscape: The heavy wooden door creaks open with a deep mechanical latch click, followed immediately by the loud, pronounced clack of leather riding boots striking polished marble in steady, rhythmic strides through the quiet indoor room tone. A distinct rustle of stiff wool fabric and leather strap squeaks accompanies the governor grasping the woman's arm, followed after the line by an audible, sharp friction sound of silk fabric tearing away as she violently jerks her sleeve free.
non_diegetic_music: A low, sparse solo cello line plays at a slow tempo, supported underneath by muted sustained double basses and a solitary piano note struck every few bars, maintaining a flat, subdued dynamic throughout without dramatic swells.

04-25 · 美感与画质 · 构图差（主体不突出、画面层次乱）

陈柚试装时的双人构图

考点双人中景的画幅边缘有没有切掉五官？
观察方法11.6秒看右边界是否压在表姐五官上，和本来就设计为局部脸部特写的镜头区分。

正例

11.6 秒两人的脸都完整入画。

正例原始 prompt

写实轻快的穿搭短片，无字幕。辣妹装女生陈柚拿着同学聚会邀请函，站在带全身镜的衣柜前，身侧是表姐。她一边看衣服，一边叹气：「同学聚会好久不见，穿得太普通没存在感，穿得太夸张又像刻意炫富……」表姐笑着拿出黑色显瘦上衣和高腰包臀开叉半裙：「试试这个，低调又显贵，气场直接拉满。」陈柚换上套装对镜转圈：「天！这一套也太显气质了！显瘦还显高，聚会穿稳赢！」表姐笑着说：「早跟你说，这就是聚会万能套装，低调不张扬，又能悄悄惊艳所有人。」最后陈柚拎包出门，裙摆开叉随步伐打开，光线衬出黑色布料的质感。两人声音清楚，语气自然。
第11.6秒转到两人同框的中景：陈柚在左，表姐在右，都留出脸侧的空间；继续拍到表姐手里的黑色套装，两张脸都在画幅内。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium shot at eye level as the camera executes a slow push in toward the interior of a brightly lit, modern bedroom. In the center foreground, a young female on-screen protagonist in her early twenties with a clear, youthful voice named Chen You (S1), featuring long wavy chestnut hair and wearing a vibrant spicy-girl cropped knit halter top and a distressed denim mini skirt, stands in front of a tall wooden wardrobe fitted with a full-length mirror. overall_soundscape: A crisp rustle of the paper invitation envelope is audible as fingers turn the card, followed by the faint wooden slide and metallic clink of hangers sliding along the closet rack. A pronounced soft fabric swish is clearly heard as the cousin lifts the black outfit into view. A dull metallic clink of a handbag chain and the steady, rhythmic click-clack of confident footsteps echo toward the exit.
non_diegetic_music: Brisk and lightweight modern pop-funk instrumental featuring bright electric piano chords, subtle acoustic guitar strumming, and a steady, bouncy kick-and-hi-hat groove, maintaining a light, breezy tempo and cheerful, consistent volume throughout.

反例

右边缘压在表姐脸上，切掉一只眼和半边嘴。

04-26 · 美感与画质 · 实体结构美感差

桌上的陶瓷茶壶

考点绕拍时壶身同一侧是否始终凹陷？
观察方法随镜头绕行观察壶身固定的凹陷，区分实体造型不协调和视角畸变。

正例

镜头绕行时，壶身呈连贯、圆整的轮廓。

正例原始 prompt

写实产品近景。一把素白釉陶瓷茶壶静置在木桌上，壶身、壶嘴、把手和壶盖完整可见。摄影机从正侧面慢慢绕到前侧三分之四角度，全程保持柔和侧光与均匀焦点。
壶身为对称圆弧，圆盖与壶肩贴合，壶嘴和把手连接自然。从侧面转到斜前方仍能读出同一套完整结构。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, close-up shot at eye level as the camera executes a slow arc shot clockwise from a direct side profile to a front three-quarter angle. Centered in the frame, an unadorned plain white-glazed ceramic teapot sits completely still upon a dark, matte solid wood table with visible fine grain. The teapot showcases a smooth, milky-white glaze with a soft satin luster, featuring a symmetrical globular body, a circular domed lid fitting snugly into the recessed shoulder, an elegantly arched spout, and an organically attached loop handle. The background is softly out of focus, revealing a minimalist neutral-toned plaster surface with subtle organic texture. Soft, diffused directional lighting enters from the side, creating a delicate gradient highlight across the porcelain curves while casting a soft-edged, transparent contact shadow onto the tabletop. As the camera glides steadily around the vessel, the three-dimensional geometry of the spout, shoulder, lid, and handle unfolds in continuous, crisp focus, maintaining a clean and serene product display.
overall_soundscape: A distinct, quiet interior room tone hums steadily in the background, accompanied by the faint, audible whisper of gentle air circulation. The environment remains tranquil, punctuated only by the subtle, microscopic resonance of absolute stillness.
non_diegetic_music: A slow-tempo solo acoustic classical guitar melody played with soft fingerpicking, sustained low warm resonance, and lingering note decay without dynamic swells.

反例

壶身同一侧有固定凹陷，换角度仍能看到它破坏壶形。

反例原始 prompt

写实产品近景。一把素白釉陶瓷茶壶静置在木桌上，壶身、壶嘴、把手和壶盖完整可见。摄影机从正侧面慢慢绕到前侧三分之四角度，全程保持柔和侧光与均匀焦点。
从第一帧到结尾，在壶身正前方做出一大片向内瘪陷的鼓包折痕，圆弧一侧因此明显失衡；这处缺陷是稳定的实体形状，随转镜仍在同一位置。壶嘴、盖、把手、瓷釉和曝光都正常。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a close-up framing at eye level, the camera executes a slow arc shot, smoothly revolving from a direct profile view toward a front three-quarter angle. Centered in the frame sits a minimalist plain white-glazed ceramic teapot, resting stably on the dark, satin-finished surface of a solid walnut table that exhibits faint natural wood grains and subtle reflections. The teapot features a pristine arched handle on one side, an impeccably sculpted pouring spout on the opposite side, and a matching snug-fitting ceramic lid crowned with a small spherical finial. Across the front belly of the teapot, a broad, deeply sunken inward dent with pronounced folding creases visibly alters and unbalances the vessel's curved silhouette, forming a fixed, tangible physical flaw while the surrounding eggshell glaze remains smooth, intact, and lustrous. A broad softbox off-camera casts soft, directional side lighting across the porcelain surface, illuminating the smooth contours with gentle highlights while casting delicate, shifting shadows into the recessed crevices of the sunken crease. In the softly blurred background, a neutral warm-toned studio backdrop dissolves into smooth bokeh. Throughout the smooth revolving movement, the camera maintains crisp, uniform focus on the teapot, revealing the dimensional depth and unchanging geometry of the dent as the viewing angle shifts.
overall_soundscape: A distinct, quiet interior room tone fills the space with an audible, faint hum of soft ventilation. A subtle, low-frequency atmospheric presence hums steadily in the background as the camera tracks, devoid of any sudden jarring impacts or intrusive noise.
non_diegetic_music: A sparse ambient soundscape led by gentle, sustained synthesizer pads in a slow tempo, overlaid with occasional soft, reverberant solo piano chords that decay naturally without any dramatic swell.

04-27 · 美感与画质 · 生成异常内容

琉璃宫殿短时花屏

考点特效中是否出现整幅不可读的花屏？
观察方法正常的七彩折射和碎片有场景深度，故障的一秒连背景轮廓也完全不可见。

正例

彩色折射与碎片之间仍能辨认人物和宫墙。

正例原始 prompt

10秒奇幻短片。琉璃宫殿的透明水晶墙折射七彩光，衣袍晶莹半透明的幻境行者站在殿心，低声道“梦境由我而定”。碎梦者从紫色裂纹中现身冷笑回应“你错了”。水晶剑以七彩折光划破空间，无数悬浮裂片在空中翻飞碰撞。
7.5–8.5秒继续呈现从剑势中心飞出的水晶碎片，透过碎片还能定位宫墙、行者和对手，画面在高速动作中连续可辨。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium shot with a gentle push in toward the center of an expansive glaze palace. Monumental translucent crystal walls rise on all sides, refracting bright shafts of light into iridescent rainbow caustic bands dancing across the mirror-polished crystalline floor. In the center midground stands an ethereal young adult male on-screen Dream Walker with a steady, resonant timbre (S1), featuring flowing silver hair and wearing a semi-transparent, opalescent crystalline robe that shimmers with soft chromatic dispersion. Looking forward with a focused gaze, the Dream Walker (S1) murmurs in a low, composed tone, <d>[Chinese] 梦境由我而定</d>. Immediately behind him, a jagged vertical fissure of violet void energy violently tears open in mid-air. Stepping forward from the purple rift, an imposing adult male on-screen warrior known as the Dream Breaker (S2), clad in jagged dark-purple crystalline armor with glowing violet irises, sneers and retorts with a harsh chuckle, <d>[Chinese] 你错了</d>. [Shot 2] At 00:04.800, the camera cuts to a dynamic tracking shot following the Dream Walker from Shot 1 as he pivots swiftly, drawing a luminous broadsword carved from faceted prismatic crystal. The crystal blade carves a wide arc through space, producing radiant rainbow light streaks that slice directly into the dimensional fracture. The blade's impact shatters the rift into thousands of suspended, razor-sharp crystal shards that burst outward, tumbling and colliding violently in mid-air. [Shot 3] At 00:07.500, the shot transitions to a close tracking shot traveling rapidly alongside the swirling burst of airborne crystal shards radiating from the sword strike's epicenter. Each rotating, diamond-hard fragment acts as a moving prism, continuously refracting the background while keeping the towering palace walls, the Dream Walker from Shot 1 holding his strike follow-through on the left, and the staggered Dream Breaker from Shot 1 on the right visually discernible and spatially coherent through the transparent, spinning debris.
overall_soundscape: A deep crystalline palace room tone and faint ambient glass chime hum continuously in the background, accompanied by the distinct rustle of flowing silk. A sudden, loud electrical rip sounds as the purple rift tears through space, followed by an audible low sneer. The swing of the crystal blade generates a pronounced, whistling whoosh, instantly succeeded by the loud, resonant shattering of glass and a dense clatter of tumbling crystal shards colliding in mid-air.
non_diegetic_music: Sustained glass harp pads and low orchestral strings, mid-tempo, layered with shimmering bell tones that steadily pulse without melodic swell.

反例

7.5–8.5 秒错乱色块和扫描线盖住全画面，期间声音连续。

反例原始 prompt

10秒奇幻短片。琉璃宫殿的透明水晶墙折射七彩光，衣袍晶莹半透明的幻境行者站在殿心，低声道“梦境由我而定”。碎梦者从紫色裂纹中现身冷笑回应“你错了”。水晶剑以七彩折光划破空间，无数悬浮裂片在空中翻飞碰撞。
只在7.5–8.5秒用整屏错乱的彩色矩形块与破碎扫描线覆盖真实画面，一秒内完全看不清人物、宫墙或水晶；8.5秒瞬时恢复到正常碎片碰撞场景。音轨连续，前后镜头都正常。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium wide shot, pushing in at a slow speed toward the center of an opulent glazed crystal palace. Towering translucent quartz columns support vaulted arches overhead, refracting ambient soft white light into delicate rainbow caustics across the polished crystal floor. In the center stands the Dream Walker (S1), a slender young adult male with waist-length flowing silver hair, dressed in a gossamer, semi-translucent crystal-woven white robe that shimmers with prismatic colors as he moves. The Dream Walker (S1) lowers his gaze slightly and whispers in a calm, clear tone, <d>[Chinese] 梦境由我而定</d> Behind him to the right, a jagged violet fissure violently tears open the air with flickering purple light. [Shot 4] At 00:08.500, the camera cuts to a close-up shot, executing an arc shot that curves around the suspended debris. The audio seamlessly continues from the previous shot. The glitch pattern abruptly clears to reveal the floating crystal shards from Shot 2 tumbling dynamically through prismatic beams of palace light. overall_soundscape: A deep, resonant crystalline resonance hums loudly in the background, accompanied by the distinct crisp rustle of sheer fabric. 04-28 · 美感与画质 · 色彩不协调-P1

珍珠耳环与油画花园

考点背景色块的组合是否抢走主体？
观察方法与“饱和度太高”区分：每个单色本身不刺眼，失衡的是背景色相组合和面积分配。

正例

花叶色彩相互呼应，少女和珍珠仍是视线落点。

正例原始 prompt

油画风格中景。一名戴珍珠耳环的女孩站在花园小径边发呆，脸的侧面朝向光，花丛与树丛围绕她。镜头缓慢靠近侧脸，珍珠、小径、花叶均可辨。
花园以灰绿、浅粉和暖米色为主，花丛的色相有呼应，女孩肤色清楚，珍珠亮点自然吸引视线。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Oil painting style, a medium shot at eye level framed with visible canvas texture and delicate impasto brushwork slowly pushes in toward the profile of a young woman in her late teens standing beside a garden pathway. Positioned slightly to the right of center, her face is turned in profile toward camera left where soft natural morning light illuminates her features. She has luminous, porcelain fair skin, subtle rose-tinted cheeks, and dark hair neatly tucked under an ochre-yellow and muted beige wrapped linen headscarf. She wears a warm sand-beige wool bodice accented by a crisp, folded white linen collar. Dangling from her left earlobe is a large teardrop-shaped natural pearl earring, casting a pronounced bright specular highlight that stands out against the soft shadow beneath her jawline. She stands motionless beside a pale beige crushed-stone pathway, surrounded by dense garden beds filled with dusty sage-green foliage, pale pink open garden roses, and creamy wild blossoms. In the background, muted olive-green trees blend into a painterly, atmospheric garden backdrop. Her chest rises and falls with a faint, steady breath as her gaze remains fixed into the distance in tranquil daydreaming. A stray wisp of hair at her temple and nearby rose leaves flutter faintly in a gentle draft. The camera steadily glides closer into a close-up of her profile, keeping the glowing pearl, her clear skin, and the textured floral foliage sharply defined as she blinks once with quiet stillness.
overall_soundscape: A distinct, gentle rustle of leaves and rose petals is audible as a soft garden breeze drifts through the foliage, accompanied by the clear, distant chirp of songbirds among the trees. The faint, subtle rustle of linen and wool fabric is heard as the young woman breathes slowly, blending into a quiet outdoor room tone.
non_diegetic_music: Solo acoustic classical guitar, slow tempo, playing a soft fingerpicked melody with sustained low cello resonance underneath, maintaining a steady and quiet dynamic.

反例

紫红、橙棕、蓝绿和冷黄占据相近面积，彼此争夺视线，各单色饱和度适中。

反例原始 prompt

05-02 · 音频指令遵循 · 台词严重错误(重复／乱序／缺失／多余／含糊)

羽绒服广告的四句文案

考点羽绒服出镜时，产品介绍是否念全？
观察方法原样抄下听到的广告词。反例完整漏掉产品介绍分句，结尾祝福仍齐全。

正例

在“这是暖天鹅”之后，能听到“这是能穿的极地天鹅绒羽绒服”。

正例原始 prompt

拍一则新年羽绒服广告，依次给一根鹅绒、暖色灯下的天鹅、穿着极地天鹅绒羽绒服的模特三个镜头，最后定格在衣领。女旁白用普通话念：“这是根鹅绒，这是暖天鹅，这是能穿的极地天鹅绒羽绒服，新年穿得暖，生活过得暖。”

05-03 · 音频指令遵循 · 台词轻微错误(读音／漏字／错字／多个字)

深海神殿的一字之差

考点末句的指定用字是否说对？
观察方法回放女皇末句，“沉”变为“淹”，仅一字替换。

正例

女皇说“光，只会沉没”。

正例原始 prompt

冷蓝色水下神殿里，长发的潮汐之子手臂浮起珊瑚荧光。他用低沉的普通话说“深海仍有光。”戴珊瑚冠的深渊女皇从漩涡里出现，冷冷地答：“光，只会沉没。”两句尾音随水下回声散开。

[Shot 2] At 00:07.500, the camera cuts to a medium-wide shot with a slow push in on an ominous underwater vortex churning in the opposite aisle of the temple, from which the Abyss Empress (S2) emerges. The adult female on-screen sovereign (S2) possesses pale alabaster skin, sharp aristocratic features, and obsidian hair floating weightlessly around an ornate crown forged of jagged crimson sea-coral and glistening black pearls. She wears a voluminous midnight-blue gown of semi-translucent sea-silk that billows outward in the swirling current. overall_soundscape: A deep underwater room tone and muffled aquatic rumble are dominant in the foreground, layered with the distinct crystalline hum of activating coral bioluminescence, followed by the audible rushing whoosh of a churning underwater vortex and pronounced hollow reverberations echoing across submerged stone pillars.
non_diegetic_music: Low sustained cello drone and bowed glass tones, slow tempo, with subtle ethereal high-register metallic shimmer underneath, maintaining a steady, solemn atmosphere without percussive swells.

听到“光，只会淹没”，“沉”被换成“淹”。

05-04 · 音频指令遵循 · 旁白／画外音／内心独白错误-P0

雪山口的内心独白

考点英文独白是否以心声呈现？
观察方法词句和音色相同。观察她有没有张嘴发声，区分内心独白与当场讲话。

正例

她闭着嘴，观众仍能听到完整的内心独白。

正例原始 prompt

中景跟拍一名骑白马穿过雪山口的女战士。白马缓步吐出白雾，雪落在她的眉梢。用她沙哑、低沉的女声缓慢说英文“They hoped the winter would bury us. They don't know what's coming.”镜头拍到她始终抿紧嘴，英文是贴耳的内心独白，身外可听见马蹄踏雪。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium tracking shot. The camera performs a smooth, steady tracking shot moving alongside a female warrior in her late twenties as she rides a massive white horse slowly across a windswept mountain pass. The warrior possesses weathered, chiseled features, cold-reddened cheekbones, and piercing hazel eyes framed by dark, frosted eyebrows and delicate ice crystals clinging to her dark eyelashes. She wears heavy dark leather armor over a thick charcoal wool tunic, a fur-lined mantle dusted with fresh snow, and sturdy leather riding gloves gripping the reins. Her chapped lips are pressed firmly together in a resolute, unyielding line. The white warhorse plods forward through knee-deep powdery snow, its frosted mane swaying rhythmically as thick plumes of white vapor billow from its flared nostrils into the freezing air. Towering slate-gray mountain crags crusted with rime ice flank the snowy saddle behind them under an overcast, diffused winter sky, with soft cool-toned backlight illuminating the drifting snow flurries and the horse's breath. As the rider steadily gazes forward through the blustering flakes, an adult female voiceover with a low, raspy, deliberate cadence (S1) is heard in close, intimate proximity, as the female warrior speaks in voiceover (S1), <d>[English] They hoped the winter would bury us. They don't know what's coming.</d> with her lips remaining completely closed and her jaw set firm throughout. Following her internal monologue, the warrior blinks slowly against the biting wind, subtly tightening her grip on the reins as the horse continues its deliberate, heavy stride toward the edge of the pass.
overall_soundscape: The deep, rhythmic crunch and muffled thuds of horse hooves packing down fresh powder snow are dominant in the foreground. Distinct warm snorts and puffing exhalations from the horse's nostrils are clearly heard alongside the faint metallic clink of bridle rings and the creak of strained saddle leather. A low, continuous howl of mountain wind whistles across jagged rocks in the ambient background, carrying the soft rustle of heavy fur and wool shifting with the rider's posture.
non_diegetic_music: A sustained, low-register cello drone layered with a sparse, slow contrabass pulse, maintaining a steady, ominous tempo without dynamic swells.

反例

词句相同，她却对着山口逐字张嘴说出来。

反例原始 prompt

中景跟拍一名骑白马穿过雪山口的女战士。白马缓步吐出白雾，雪落在她的眉梢。用她沙哑、低沉的女声缓慢说英文“They hoped the winter would bury us. They don't know what's coming.”她抬头面向山口，嘴随英文逐字开合，声音从马背上的她口中传来；身外可听见马蹄踏雪。

05-05 · 音频指令遵循 · 开放式台词不合理发挥—P0

同事在桌边核对工作

考点开放式办公对白是否围绕工作？
观察方法对照人物动作和谈话内容。反例句子流畅，但同事从头到尾都在聊星座，没交流工作。

正例

同事边翻材料边交流工作，具体措辞可以变化。

正例原始 prompt

写实办公室里，一名设计师和一名同事面对面坐在桌边，桌上摆着样品、色卡、笔记本和日程表。两人边翻色卡边轻声交谈。设计师说“蓝色样品明天能送到吗？”同事看着日程表回答“能，我下午把确认单发给你。”

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a medium two-shot with a slow push in captures a modern, sunlit corporate design studio where two professionals sit opposite each other across a pale oak worktable. On the left sits a young adult East Asian female designer (S1) in her late twenties, wearing a ribbed cream-colored crewneck sweater, her dark chin-length bob tucked behind small silver hoop earrings. Opposite her on the right sits a young adult East Asian male colleague in his early thirties, dressed in a crisp navy-blue Oxford shirt with rolled-up sleeves. The table between them is organized with scattered design materials: several fanned-out Pantone paper color swatches, tactile textured fabric sample swatches in varied shades of navy and cerulean, an open black dot-grid notebook with handwritten pencil annotations, and a ceramic coffee mug. Soft daylight streams through floor-to-ceiling frosted glass partitions in the background, supplemented by diffuse overhead linear LED track lights. S1 holds a fan of color cards in her hands, her thumb gently spreading the color chips before pausing at an indigo swatch; she lifts her gaze toward her colleague, slightly tilting her head as the young female designer (S1) asks softly, <d>[Chinese] 蓝色样品明天能送到吗？</d> [Shot 2] At 00:07.200, the camera cuts to a medium close-up over the designer's shoulder with a subtle pan right, focusing directly on the male colleague (S2) behind the desk. In the foreground, the soft out-of-focus curve of S1's shoulder and sweater frames the composition. S2 wears thin round tortoiseshell glasses perched on his nose. Before him on the wooden surface lies an open printed weekly planner grid with penciled calendar dates and checkmarks, next to a matte black gel pen. As S1 finishes her question, the male colleague (S2) shifts his eyes downward to inspect the calendar row, slides his index finger across the Tuesday column, and nods reassuringly before looking up to meet her eyes, as the male colleague (S2) replies in a steady, friendly tone, <d>[Chinese] 能，我下午把确认单发给你。</d> He picks up the gel pen and jots down a brief checkmark beside the entry, while in the foreground S1's shoulder gently drops with a subtle, relieved nod.
overall_soundscape: A gentle, low ambient office hum and air conditioning airflow provide a steady baseline, punctuated by the audible rustling and papery flutter of thick color cardstock being fanned across the desk. A distinct, clear soft thud occurs as a finger taps onto the desktop, followed by the quiet friction of fabric shifting against a chair, and concluding with the sharp, pronounced click of a retractable gel pen and the faint scratching of a nib writing against paper.
non_diegetic_music: A light, minimal acoustic guitar melody playing fingerpicked arpeggios at a moderate, calm tempo, accompanied by soft, warm upright bass notes beneath without dynamic swells.

反例

从头到尾谈星座和恋爱运势，办公材料始终没进入谈话。

反例原始 prompt

写实办公室里，一名设计师和一名同事面对面坐在桌边，桌上摆着样品、色卡、笔记本和日程表。两人边翻色卡边轻声交谈。设计师说“今晚的星座运势怎么说？”同事看着日程表回答“水瓶座今天最适合谈恋爱。”

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium shot, the camera pushes in slowly toward an open-plan modern creative studio. In the center midground, a young female on-screen designer in her late twenties with a relaxed, soft-spoken voice (S1), wearing an oversized beige linen blazer over a white crewneck tee with thin silver wire-rimmed glasses and dark hair pinned into a loose bun, sits across a natural oak desk. 05-06 · 音频指令遵循 · 唱歌／播报／ASMR等呈现形式错误

红桥重逢的日语说话方式

考点四句日语是日常对话还是唱出来的？
观察方法对白内容、声量和说话人均相同。听音节是否有固定音高与延长的歌唱尾音。

正例

四句均低声说出，句尾自然收住。

正例原始 prompt

固定全景，男女从红桥两端跑到中央，站定喘息，拥抱、轻吻。脚步、风声、衣料摩擦与舒缓配乐层次分明。四句日语依次为，男「やっと……会えたな。」女「うん、ずっとここに来るって信じてた。」女「離れないで。」男「もう、離さない。」两人靠近彼此，呼吸间用低声交谈，末句自然收声。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a wide shot, the camera maintains a static shot capturing a traditional arched vermilion red wooden bridge stretching across a calm mountain river gorge. Flanking both ends of the bridge, damp stone lanterns and wind-swept Japanese pine trees frame the scene against misty, cool-toned hills under soft, overcast late-afternoon natural light. From the far left, a young Japanese man in his early twenties (S1) with tousled black hair, wearing an open navy blue wool overcoat over an ivory knit sweater, dark trousers, and leather boots, sprints onto the red planks. Simultaneously from the far right, a young Japanese woman in her early twenties (S2) with shoulder-length straight black hair, dressed in a loose beige trench coat over a pleated skirt and brown ankle boots, rushes forward with outstretched arms. Meeting at the bridge's elevated apex in the center of the frame, both skid to a halt, chests heaving and shoulders rising and falling in sync as they catch their breath. The man (S1) draws her into a tight, protective embrace, his hands gripping the back of her coat while she clings to his shoulders, followed by him leaning down to press a gentle kiss to her temple. Loosening the embrace slightly to look into her face, the young man with a low, breathy voice (S1) whispers, <d>[Japanese] やっと……会えたな。</d> Looking up with glistening eyes and a faint nod, the young woman with a soft, gentle voice (S2) replies, <d>[Japanese] うん、ずっとここに来るって信じてた。</d> Tightening her grip around his lapels, the woman (S2) buries her face into the curve of his neck and murmurs softly, <d>[Japanese] 離れないで。</d> Enfolding her securely against his chest, the man (S1) rests his chin against her hair and whispers in return, <d>[Japanese] もう、離さない。</d> The two stand held together in the center of the red bridge as the wind gently ruffles their coats and hair.
overall_soundscape: Rapid wooden clatter and heavy thuds of running leather boots resonate along the bridge planks, which abruptly transitions into distinct, pronounced panting and audible breathing at the center. Dominant rustling of thick wool and cotton fabric is clearly heard as the bodies collide into a tight embrace, underlaid by the steady whistling hiss of cool mountain wind and the distant rushing murmur of the river below.
non_diegetic_music: Acoustic piano melody played at a slow tempo, accompanied by warm, sustained low cello notes and sparse acoustic guitar plucks, maintaining a soft, consistent dynamic level with no dramatic swell.

05-07 · 音频指令遵循 · 台词归属错误

客厅里谁说了哪句话

考点两句台词是否由指定角色说出？
观察方法看开口的人并听声线，第二、三句归属互换，语序照旧。

正例

白西装男人追问“这次又为了什么”，沙发上的妇人说“别掺和”。

正例原始 prompt

真人短剧里，白衣女子上前，红裙女子偏头冷笑。白西装男人站在旁边，年长妇人坐在沙发上。红裙女子先问“你还敢回来？”。男人接“这次又为了什么？”，沙发上的妇人制止他“别掺和...”。白衣女子回答“有些事，必须说清楚。”众人低语叹气，红裙女子轻笑“说清楚？就凭你，算什么东西？”镜头推近白衣女子紧握的拳头。

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, wide shot at eye level as the camera pushes in slowly across a luxurious modern living room with marble flooring, a recessed ceiling with hidden warm strip lights, and tall glass windows in the background showing evening city lights. 05-10 · 音频指令遵循 · 非语言人声音效错误／缺失-P1

雨棚下八戒的哈欠

考点画面里的哈欠是否有对应人声？
观察方法紧盯左侧八戒，从张嘴到闭嘴听有没有吸气与哈欠声。

正例

八戒张嘴吸气，接着有拖长的哈欠呼气声。

正例原始 prompt

水墨动画里，师徒四人在木棚下避雨。雨点打在棚顶，悟空晃了晃叶子伞，沙僧擦马背，白马甩尾。画面左侧的八戒张大嘴打了一个长哈欠。张嘴时响起低低的吸气声，接着是一声拖长的“哈——”，最后闭嘴；棚顶雨声持续。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Ink wash animated, wide shot with a slow, deliberate Push In toward a rustic shelter during a downpour. The scene is rendered in traditional Chinese ink and watercolor brushwork with soft gradients of black ink, slate gray, and subtle earthy pigments on textured Xuan paper. A weathered wooden pavilion with rough timber pillars and a thatched bark roof stands beside dripping bamboo stalks, while fine diagonal rain streaks slant through the misty mountain background. Beneath the dry roof, the four journeying pilgrims seek shelter: in the background center sits the serene monk Tang Sanzang in a reddish-orange kasaya robe over pale robes, holding prayer beads in quiet meditation. In the midground right, the Monkey King, Sun Wukong, clad in a gold-yellow tunic, tiger-skin kilt, and red neckerchief, lifts a broad, dripping green lotus leaf and playfully shakes it, sending ink-drawn water droplets scattering outwards. Next to him, the burly, dark-bearded monk Shaseng in an indigo tunic uses a coarse dry cloth to rhythmically rub down the damp back of the White Dragon Horse, who stands calmly and swishes its long white tail back and forth. On the left side of the frame, seated lazily against a wooden pillar with his legs outstretched and large belly bulging beneath a loose black monk robe, an anthropomorphic pig demon on-screen speaker with a lazy, guttural tone (S1) leans his head back, his floppy ears drooping as he draws in a low, distinct wheezing breath through his snout, stretching his jaws wide open into a cavernous yawn as the pig demon (S1) exhales, <d>[Chinese] 哈——</d>. His small eyes squeeze shut into tight slits, his rounded snout wrinkles, and at the end of the long exhalation, he lets his mouth snap shut with a soft smack of his lips, settling comfortably back into the post while the rain continues to fall steadily outside.
overall_soundscape: A steady, dominant downpour creates a continuous wet hum as raindrops drum loudly against the thatched wooden roof and patter onto puddle-strewn soil. A distinct rustle and sharp flick of droplets are audible as Wukong shakes the wet leaf umbrella, followed by the soft, rhythmic swish of Shaseng's cloth rubbing the horse's coat and the faint swoosh of the horse's tail. Bajie's yawn produces a clearly heard, low nasal inhale, culminating in a pronounced lip smack and teeth clack as his jaws close shut.
non_diegetic_music: A sparse acoustic guqin melody played at a very slow tempo, accompanied by occasional sustained low notes on a xiao flute, maintaining a quiet, steady dynamic level throughout.

反例

In the center, Tang Sanzang, a serene Buddhist monk with a shaved head and refined features, sits upright in deep calm atop a low wooden bench, wearing a pale grey inner tunic beneath an ochre-tinted kasaya robe while holding a string of dark prayer beads in his lap. To his right, Sun Wukong, an agile monkey king with gold-tinged brown fur, wearing an ochre tunic, red neck scarf, and a spotted tiger-skin kilt, crouches deftly on a crossbeam, holding a broad green plantain leaf umbrella over his shoulder. At the far right of the frame, Sha Wujing, a tall and broad-shouldered monk with indigo-tinged skin, curly dark beard, and a string of large prayer skulls over a slate-grey robe, stands beside the White Dragon Horse, using a coarse hemp rag to wipe streaks of rainwater from the steed's sleek white muscular flank. Wukong playfully tilts and shakes his leafy umbrella, scattering fine black ink droplets into the air. The White Dragon Horse shifts its weight slightly, its silky white tail swishing back and forth in a smooth arc. On the left, Zhu Bajie's eyelids droop heavily before he slowly stretches his mouth wide open into an exaggerated, leisurely, silent yawn that puffs his cheeks and lifts his snout, holding the wide-mouthed posture for several tranquil seconds before his jaw gradually relaxes and his lips softly close back together. Tang Sanzang slowly slides a single wooden bead between his fingers, remaining perfectly motionless in the damp shelter as rain continues to streak down the misty background.
overall_soundscape: Distinct, rhythmic taps and hollow clatters of heavy raindrops drumming on the thatched and timber shelter roof dominate the foreground, followed by the audible wet swishing and rustling of the horse's tail sweeping against its hindquarters. A soft, continuous background hum of falling rain and distant water drips into puddles accompanies the scene, while the wiping of the damp cloth against the horse's back creates a subtle, pronounced rhythmic rubbing sound without any vocalization from the yawning pig demon.
non_diegetic_music: Sparse solo guqin plucks played at a slow tempo with long natural decays, accompanied by faint, sustained low-register xiao flute tones maintaining a flat dynamic shape throughout.

05-12 · 音频指令遵循 · 动物叫声错误／缺失-P0

狗被碰醒时的叫声

考点狗惊醒时响起的是哪种叫声？
观察方法先用鼾声定位狗，再听惊醒时的发声类别。猫爪的存在不能解释从狗嘴发出的猫叫。

正例

停下鼾声后，狗张嘴吠叫。

正例原始 prompt

第一视角贴地穿过安静的客厅，两只猫爪走到地毯边。狗熟睡打鼾，猫爪垫落地发出轻响。爪尖碰鼻，狗猛然惊醒，鼾声停止。狗抬头的瞬间发出一声短促的受惊吠叫，随后房间恢复安静。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, an ultra-low-angle POV glides forward at a deliberate, stealthy creeping pace just two inches above a polished amber oak parquet floor. In the lower third of the frame, two ginger-and-white tabby cat paws with pink pads step forward in alternating, measured strides. Soft morning daylight filters horizontally through sheer white linen curtains on the left, casting long, diffuse window-frame shadows across the floorboards and highlighting swirling dust motes in the quiet living room. In the midground ahead sits an oval braided cream wool rug atop which an adult golden retriever with thick honey-colored fur lies curled on a dark charcoal-grey memory foam dog bed, its ribcage rhythmically rising and falling in deep slumber. In the background, a low mid-century walnut credenza supports a ceramic lamp, stacked art magazines, and a terracotta potted snake plant beside an upholstered beige armchair. The camera advances smoothly until the feline paws pause at the frayed fringe of the wool rug. The right white-tipped paw extends upward and outward into the foreground, slowly reaching toward the sleeping retriever's face. The soft pink pad and delicate tip of a single unsheathed claw lightly tap against the dog's moist black nose. The dog's whiskers twitch violently, its eyelids snap wide open with dilated pupils, and the steady snoring instantly cuts out. The dog sharply jerks its head upward in mid-gasp, ears flopping back, barking once in a loud, startled burst directed straight at the camera. The canine then lowers its chin slightly, blinking in groggy bewilderment and tilting its head, while the cat paw remains frozen in place in the foreground as the tranquil room settles back into motionless stillness.
overall_soundscape: A distinct, rhythmic soft patter of cushioned feline pads and faint claw clicks on polished hardwood is heard in the foreground, transitioning into a barely audible rustle across wool rug fibers. The deep, rumbling wheeze of steady canine snoring is pronounced in the midground, accompanied by a faint wet tap as the paw contacts the nose. The snoring abruptly ceases as the dog delivers a loud, sharp startled bark that briefly reverberates against the walls, followed by the audible rustle of fabric as its paws shift on the bed before sinking into quiet room tone.
non_diegetic_music: A sparse, slow-tempo solo pizzicato violin plays delicate plucked staccato notes that subtly fade out completely the moment the dog stirs, leaving the startled bark and subsequent room tone clearly audible without accompaniment.

反例

狗张嘴的同一刻传出猫叫。

反例原始 prompt

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, ground-level point-of-view shot, the camera executes a tracking shot gliding forward inches above a polished light-oak hardwood floor across a peaceful, sunlit living room. Its ears slowly swivel forward in bewildered confusion, tilting its head slightly to one side as the cat paw rests still on the rug edge, before the room returns to motionless quiet.
overall_soundscape: Audible deep, rhythmic snoring from the sleeping dog dominates the foreground, accompanied by the distinct soft patter of cat paw pads on the hardwood floor as the paws advance. A loud, sharp snort is clearly heard the moment the claw taps the dog's nose, abruptly cutting off the snore. 05-13 · 音频指令遵循 · 音效错误／缺失-P0

山林交锋的刀鸣

考点交锋时是否有指令指定的刀鸣？
观察方法反例从交锋到余响都没有指令要求的刀鸣。对白和风声保留。

正例

刀刃相碰能听见金属刀鸣，随后有余响。

正例原始 prompt

水墨山林中，青石上的刀客缓慢抽出山纹弯刀，低声说“此山，不容匪行。”山匪首领从树影中走出，回答“山林自有强者居之。”下一瞬二人身影交错，两刀相碰，枝叶被震落。兵刃接触时响起带短促金属尾韵的刀鸣，风声和刀鸣在山谷回荡。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium shot with a slow push in. In a misty mountain forest rendered with ink-wash atmospheric depth, an adult male on-screen swordsman (S1) in his late twenties, with keen dark eyes, coarse black hair tied into a half-topknot, and dressed in a weathered charcoal-grey linen robe with leather arm bracers, sits crouched on a moss-covered blue-grey boulder at the frame center. Tall, spindly pine trees and drifting white fog layer the background under diffused, cool dawn light filtering through damp foliage. S1's right hand firmly grips the wrapped hilt of a curved scimitar adorned with etched mountain-ridge patterns, slowly drawing the gleaming steel blade from its scabbard with measured restraint. As a silver glint catches the blade edge, the swordsman (S1) tilts his chin downward, eyes narrowing, and murmurs in a steady, low gravelly register, <d>[Chinese] 此山，不容匪行。</d> [Shot 2] At 00:05.100, the camera cuts to a medium wide shot with a slow pan right. From the deep shadows beneath crooked ancient pine trunks on the left, an imposing adult male on-screen bandit chief (S2) in his late thirties steps deliberately forward into a patch of dim natural sidelight. He possesses a broad muscular build, a scarred cheek, unkempt dark hair spilling over broad shoulders, and wears a coarse dark brown hemp tunic strapped with iron pauldrons and ragged animal pelt trimmings. Grasping a wide-headed cleaving broadsword resting against his shoulder, the bandit chief (S2) halts his stride in the midground, raises his brow with a sneering smirk, and replies in a gruff, resonant tone, <d>[Chinese] 山林自有强者居之。</d> [Shot 3] At 00:09.300, the shot transitions to a dynamic profile tracking shot tracking laterally along the stony forest path. Both combatants suddenly surge forward simultaneously, their silhouettes darting across the misty clearing as their robes whip violently in the rushing draft. In the center of the frame, the swordsman from Shot 1 and the bandit chief from Shot 2 cross paths at blinding speed, their heavy blades colliding mid-motion in a brilliant burst of golden friction sparks. The violent shockwave shudders through the surrounding pine boughs, knocking loose clusters of emerald pine needles and dead autumn leaves that flutter frantically downward through the swirling haze as both warriors complete their lunging follow-through, landing back-to-back in low crouches with blades still vibrating.
overall_soundscape: A distinct metallic rasp is clearly heard as the scimitar slides out of its wooden scabbard, accompanied by the gentle ambient rustle of pine needles and damp mountain wind whispering through the trees. Heavy, thudding boot steps land on wet soil and fallen leaves as the bandit advances, followed by a sudden explosive whoosh of displaced air as both men dash forward. A loud, sharp clang of colliding steel erupts with a dominant, pronounced metallic ring that sustains a piercing high-frequency resonance, which transitions into the flutter of falling leaves and the prolonged reverberation of wind and blade hum echoing through the mountain valley.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

反例

从交锋到结束只能听见风等原有声音，刀鸣整段缺席。

反例原始 prompt

05-14 · 音频指令遵循 · BGM类型／氛围错误缺失-P1

理顺耳机后的音乐片段

考点耳机接上后是否响起要求的音乐？
观察方法原题只要求一小段音乐。可判的是这段音乐缺失。音乐具体流派由创作者选择。

正例

插头接好后，能听到一小段音乐。

正例原始 prompt

男子坐在安静的桌前，举起缠成一团的耳机说“看好这招。”他晃动线头，一扯理顺，说“三十秒搞定，不用任何工具。”听见线缆轻擦。耳机塞入耳朵，插头“咔哒”接上手机，他朝镜头点赞。接头扣紧后响起一小段轻快音乐，随点赞收尾。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium shot at eye level, the camera pushes in at a slow speed toward a clean-shaven young East Asian man in his mid-20s with short textured black hair (S1), who is seated centered behind a warm-toned solid oak desk. He wears a heather-gray cotton crewneck sweatshirt and dark canvas trousers. In the quiet, softly lit study room, the background shows a shallow-depth-of-field arrangement of a minimalist bookshelf with neatly stacked design books, a ceramic mug, and a potted succulent, all illuminated by soft diffused daylight spilling from a large window off to the left complemented by a warm-white LED desk lamp. 05-15 · 音频指令遵循 · 乐器错误缺失-P0

鎏金沙粒与古筝泛音

考点大鼓旁的第二件乐器是否为古筝？
观察方法大鼓和旁白一致，分辨第二件乐器的拨弦起音与吹奏长音。

正例

鼓声之外有古筝拨弦的起音和泛音余韵。

正例原始 prompt

黑色画面上，金色沙砾自左向右汇成“历史”二字。低沉的中国大鼓逐拍响起，旁白说“历史的长河在此刻奔涌而出。”鼓的间隙有另一件乐器在宽阔空间里留下一声长长的余响。那是古筝的泛音，拨弦起音后留下清亮的弦振尾声。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] 3D CG, medium close-up, the camera slowly pulls out across an infinite void of deep obsidian black space. From the far-left edge of the frame, a dense, turbulent current of incandescent golden sand grains surges horizontally toward the right, emitting a warm, radiant amber luminescence and casting fine dynamic shadows across adjacent particles. As the luminous ribbon of sand sweeps through the midground, the individual grains begin to decelerate and coalesce, drawn inward by invisible calligraphic vectors to assemble the two traditional Chinese characters "历史" in bold, elegant brushstroke forms at screen center. During the deliberate pauses between the percussive strikes, an audible, sharp fingertip pluck of a metallic string produces a crystalline guzheng chime, sustaining a clear and pronounced trailing vibration that slowly decays into silence alongside a faint, low-frequency atmospheric hum.
non_diegetic_music: Traditional Chinese bass drum (tanggu) and solo guzheng, slow tempo, characterized by sparse, unhurried drum strikes at steady intervals, punctuated by isolated high-register guzheng harmonic plucks that sustain long natural decay tails over a flat dynamic shape with no orchestral swells.

反例

拨弦泛音被铜号吹出的长音取代，鼓和旁白仍在。

反例原始 prompt

05-18 · 音频指令遵循 · 禁音效失败-P0

地下车库逃离的结尾声轨

考点结尾是否多出限制以外的音效？
观察方法开场允许车辆警报。检查的是原题限定只留三种声音的结尾，新增的车铃属于额外音效。

正例

收尾只听见脚步、喘息和灯管嗡鸣。

正例原始 prompt

冷光地下车库里，远处汽车警报回荡，白衬衫男子把帽衫男子按在车门上问“钥匙在哪？别逼我再问一遍。”对方回答“你找错人了。”两人拉扯，碰撞车身后跌倒，白衬衫男子说“你跑不掉的。”全段没有配乐。帽衫男子跑向出口。收尾切成只有他的急促脚步、喘息和灯管电流嗡鸣，空车位里再没有其他声音。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a medium shot with the camera performing a Shake Slightly at eye level. In the foreground of a dim subterranean parking garage bathed in harsh, cold blue-tinted fluorescent overhead strip lighting, an adult male on-screen speaker in his early thirties with trimmed dark hair, wearing a rumpled white cotton dress shirt with rolled-up sleeves and dark slacks (S1), forcefully pins a slender young male on-screen speaker in his early twenties wearing a charcoal-gray fleece hooded sweatshirt with the hood up and dark denim jeans (S2) against the driver-side door of a dusty dark sedan. 高空坠光的禁配乐镜头

观察方法听底层是否存在连续有音高和旋律走向的弦乐，区分碎裂音效与配乐。

正例

I can feel it.</d> Her chest rises and falls with a measured, shallow breath as thin vapor drifts from her parted lips into the cold damp night air.

黑白夜市的禁台词长镜头

考点无台词长镜头里是否出现可辨的发言？
观察方法原题禁的是台词。反例多出一句可听清的招呼，摊车和人流本身符合情境。

正例

夜市环境热闹，整条听不到明确台词。

正例原始 prompt

黑白电影风格的1950年代边境夜市。风衣男子警觉地正面走向镜头，镜头后退跟拍，两侧摊贩推车摆货，行人擦肩而过。一镜到底，听得到脚步和货物碰响，全程不用配乐。男子只侧头观察，走过布摊时摊主也只抬眼，两人都未开口说话。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Vintage film, medium shot at eye level, where the camera performs a backward tracking shot down a narrow, shadowy alleyway of a 1950s border night market. Centered in the frame, an alert man in his late thirties with a lean build, chiseled jaw, and dark stubble strides forward with measured, deliberate steps. He wears a charcoal-toned, double-breasted gabardine trench coat with the belt fastened snugly around his waist and the collar partially turned up, paired with dark wool trousers and a wide-brimmed felt fedora pulled low over his brow. On both sides of the corridor, weathered wooden handcarts and makeshift folding tables are packed with border trade wares—stacked ceramic bowls, bundles of tin cups, wicker baskets overflowing with dried roots, and hanging dried peppers. Pedestrians wearing flat tweed caps and thick woolen shawls shuffle in the opposite direction, their shoulders brushing past the man in the midground and foreground. As he approaches a fabric stall framed by draped rolls of rough linen and striped wool bolts, the trench-coated man turns his head slightly to the left, his eyes darting cautiously beneath the fedora brim. The cloth vendor, an elderly man in a dark knit cardigan and stained canvas apron, pauses his hands on a rolled bolt of cotton and slowly tilts his head upward, making brief, tense eye contact. Neither man utters a word. Breaking the silent stare, the trench-coated man shifts his gaze forward, subtly pulls his right lapel tighter against the damp night chill, and presses ahead as the camera steadily glides backward, keeping pace with his advancing figure while stall lanterns and shadowy market clutter drift past the edges of the frame.
overall_soundscape: Firm, distinct leather shoe soles click with audible clarity against damp cobblestones, maintaining a steady, rhythmic cadence. In the midground, the wooden wheels of handcarts groan and rattle over stone pavers, followed by the pronounced metallic clatter of tin cookware shifting against stacked porcelain bowls. The subtle rustle of heavy wool and trench coat fabric scraping against passing strangers is clearly heard, blended with a low, distant murmur of crowd murmurs and the faint, dry flapping of canvas awnings caught in the night draft.

06-08 · 基础音质 · 持续底噪-P0

静置茶壶的贯穿底噪

考点没有发声动作时，底噪是否贯穿全片？
观察方法分别试听首、中、尾，检查是否有稳定而不属于静置茶壶的噪声底。

正例

茶壶静置，首、中、尾都只有很轻的室内底声。

正例原始 prompt

明亮安静的室内，桌上放着一只静止的白瓷茶壶。壶周围没有人，镜头缓缓推进，从桌面全景移到壶嘴特写。只保留极轻的室内底声，开头、中段和结尾都安静，推进本身不产生显著声响。

07-08 · 音画同步 · 画面与BGM节拍／音调不同步—P2

街头说唱动作与鼓点

考点动作最低点是否连续落在鼓的重拍上？
观察方法盯住手的最低点或鞋底落地，数连续四拍。比较的是身体和伴奏，别跟着歌词口型判。

正例

连续四次挥手到最低点或鞋底落地都踩中重拍。

正例原始 prompt

路灯下，浅蓝衬衫的金发男生直视镜头慢步向前，用中文说唱“在黑夜中迷失方向，找不到回去的光。你问我想去向何方，只有你的目光能让我不彷徨。”左侧歌词依次出现，后面穿红卫衣和白外套的男生失焦跟随。镜头缓慢后退，唱词与口型同步，沉重的鼓点节拍稳定。主唱每到重拍就向下挥手并踏出一步，手掌落到最低点和脚踩地面都踩在鼓上。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium shot at eye level as the camera executes a Tracking Shot backward along a wide city pavement at night. Under the direct pool of an overhead amber streetlamp, an athletic young blond male in his early twenties (S1) with tousled golden hair, wearing a loose-fitting light blue cotton button-down shirt over a white crewneck undershirt and dark trousers, walks slowly forward while keeping his intense gaze locked onto the lens. In the softly blurred background, two companions trail behind out of focus, one clad in an oversized red fleece hoodie and the other in a cream-white track jacket. Wet asphalt underfoot glimmers with urban reflections beneath the dark sky, flanked by concrete curbs and distant out-of-focus street signs. Synchronizing his mouth movements precisely to the beat, the blond singer (S1) performs his verses in Chinese, <d>[Chinese] 在黑夜中迷失方向，找不到回去的光。你问我想去向何方，只有你的目光能让我不彷徨。</d> Along the left side of the frame, clean white Chinese subtitles appear sequentially in time with the delivery: "在黑夜中迷失方向", "找不到回去的光", "你问我想去向何方", and "只有你的目光能让我不彷徨". On four consecutive primary musical downbeats, the young man (S1) thrusts his palm downward and plants his foot firmly onto the pavement, the lowest dip of his hand and the sole of his sneaker striking the ground in exact unison with the percussion, punctuated by a subtle nod of his chin.
overall_soundscape: A distinct, solid sneaker thud strikes the asphalt on each rhythmic downbeat as the singer strides forward, paired with the audible rustle of cotton sleeves cutting through the air on every hand pump. A low, persistent urban room tone hums softly in the background alongside the faint whisper of a nocturnal breeze over pavement.
non_diegetic_music: A steady mid-tempo boom-bap hip-hop instrumental featuring a dominant, punchy low-end kick drum and a restrained snare doubling selected main beats, leaving each midpoint clear of percussion accents, supported by continuous low synth sub-bass without melodic swells.

反例

连续四次动作都落在相邻两个重拍正中，唱词和口型仍可合拍。

反例原始 prompt

路灯下，浅蓝衬衫的金发男生直视镜头慢步向前，用中文说唱“在黑夜中迷失方向，找不到回去的光。你问我想去向何方，只有你的目光能让我不彷徨。”左侧歌词依次出现，后面穿红卫衣和白外套的男生失焦跟随。镜头缓慢后退，唱词与口型同步，沉重的鼓点节拍稳定。主唱每一步和挥手都落在两个重拍的正中间，连续四次如此；唱词、口型与歌词字幕仍对齐。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium shot at eye level, pulling out smoothly down an asphalt roadway at night. In the center foreground, a young adult male with tousled blonde hair, wearing a loose light blue button-down shirt over a plain white t-shirt, dark trousers, and sneakers (S1) walks forward at a measured pace, gazing intensely straight into the lens. A single overhead streetlamp casts a cone of warm amber top-light down onto his blonde hair and shoulders, while cool navy shadows and wet reflections coat the damp asphalt behind him. In the soft-focus background, a second young adult male clad in an oversized crimson hoodie beneath an open white windbreaker follows behind him, his features blurred in shallow depth of field. As the camera retreats, clean sans-serif Chinese lyrics appear sequentially along the left side of the frame in luminous white text: "在黑夜中迷失方向", followed by "找不到回去的光", then "你问我想去向何方", and finally "只有你的目光能让我不彷徨". He slightly tilts his chin down, drops his left shoulder, and extends an open palm toward the lens on the final phrase before letting his arm swing naturally as he maintains an unflinching forward gaze.
overall_soundscape: Rhythmic, distinct thuds of rubber-soled sneakers stepping on damp asphalt occur in a steady cadence, accompanied by the audible rustle of cotton and nylon fabric from walking motions. A quiet, low-frequency nighttime urban room tone with a faint distant breeze hums continuously in the background.
