07-12 · 音画同步 · 环境-画面音效未同步-P0

云海与室内环境声

考点台词空隙里的环境声是否符合可见空间？
观察方法先定位云海的空间，再只听台词间隙。原题没有指定环境声，听到的场所和可见场所冲突。

正例

云海上空能听到开阔的风声。

正例原始 prompt

全景拍一位束发女侠由山巅飘起，衣袖舒展如凤凰，下方云层翻涌。镜头旋转推进到脸部特写，她用沉静偏低的女声说“他们说，我该隐于黑暗？”嘴型和台词对齐。声音像从开阔的高空传来，有气流沿袖缘掠过，两字间的留白里能听见风逐渐远去。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, wide shot tilting slightly upward as the camera pushes in continuously toward a young female on-screen martial artist in her early twenties with a calm, low-pitched voice (S1). She stands poised atop a craggy, windswept granite mountain peak before levitating gracefully into the vast sky. Her lustrous black hair is tightly bound in a high topknot secured by an ornate ebony hairpin, with wisps of bangs whipping against her determined, fair-complexioned face. She is dressed in layered martial robes of deep crimson and charcoal silk, embellished with delicate gold phoenix embroidery along the hems; her wide, billowy sleeves catch the fierce updraft, fanning out expansively like the wings of a soaring phoenix. Below her floating silhouette, an immense sea of dense, milky-white and slate-gray clouds churns and rolls turbulently across the abyss, illuminated by the cool blue ambient glow of twilight and faint amber backlighting along the horizon. As she ascends smoothly into the open atmosphere, the camera pushes in toward her face, closing the distance past the fluttering crimson fabric into an intimate facial close-up. Her dark eyes fixate steadily forward with a quiet, unwavering intensity, her chin slightly raised, and her shoulders relaxed against the rushing altitude wind. With lip movements precisely synced to her speech, the martial artist (S1) speaks in a calm, low voice, <d>[Chinese] 他们说，我该隐于黑暗？</d> As she pauses mid-sentence, her lips remain firmly closed while the surrounding wind currents softly drift by, her steady gaze never wavering from the void before her.
overall_soundscape: A dominant, howling rush of high-altitude wind fills the air, accompanied by the distinct, rhythmic whipping and snapping of heavy silk fabric fluttering fiercely against the gale. A low, continuous atmospheric rumble resonates from the swirling cloud inversion below, followed by an audible whistling airflow that softens into a faint breeze between spoken words.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

反例

画面仍是云海，台词空隙却听到近处茶匙碰杯、咖啡机蒸汽和室内低语。

反例原始 prompt

全景拍一位束发女侠由山巅飘起，衣袖舒展如凤凰，下方云层翻涌。镜头旋转推进到脸部特写，她用沉静偏低的女声说“他们说，我该隐于黑暗？”嘴型和台词对齐。台词和口型都正常，留白里却传来近处茶匙碰杯、咖啡机蒸汽和室内人群低语，仿佛话筒一直放在封闭的咖啡馆。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a wide shot opens from a slightly low angle, framing a solitary, jagged granite mountain peak piercing through an expansive sea of dense, churning white and slate-grey clouds beneath a pale twilight sky. In the center of the frame atop the precipice stands a graceful female warrior in her early twenties (S1) with sharp, poised facial features and obsidian-black hair bound high in a sleek topknot secured with a silver hairpin and fluttering black ribbons. She is dressed in layered martial robes crafted from heavy crimson and ink-black silk, featuring expansive sleeves intricately embroidered with golden phoenix feather patterns. As the shot unfolds, she rises weightlessly from the crag, floating upward into the open air as her voluminous sleeves spread wide like the wings of an ascending phoenix, billowing dramatically against the updrafts. Simultaneously, the camera performs a fluid Push In combined with a slow Roll Clockwise, spiraling closer through the atmosphere toward her. As the framing tightens into an intense face close-up, her steady, unflinching dark eyes lock forward with quiet resolve. In precise synchronization with her mouth movements, the young female on-screen warrior with a calm, low-pitched voice (S1) says, <d>[Chinese] 他们说，我该隐于黑暗？</d> Following the spoken question, her lips close firmly, her jaw tightens with subtle defiance, and her gaze remains fixed ahead as loose wisps of hair drift gently across her temple while she hovers suspended above the abyss.
overall_soundscape: A faint, distant mountain draft whistles softly in the background, starkly overridden by the pronounced close-up acoustic environment of an indoor coffee shop. The distinct metallic clinking of a teaspoon tapping repeatedly against the rim of a ceramic cup rings out clearly in the foreground, followed immediately by the loud, sharp hiss and sputter of a pressurized espresso machine steaming milk. In the quiet pauses surrounding the spoken voice, the muted, overlapping chatter and low murmur of indoor cafe patrons are clearly heard, punctuated by a distant clatter of porcelain saucers settling onto a wooden counter.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

07-13 · 音画同步 · 音效音色错误—P0

玻璃杯落地的材质声

考点同步的碰地声听起来是否像玻璃？
观察方法先核对声音发生时点，再辨玻璃碎裂与空塑料弹跳的质感。

正例

玻璃碰石地时清脆碎裂，细碎落点有轻响。

正例原始 prompt

固定近景，桌边一只杯子滑落。镜头拍清透明薄玻璃杯落在石地上碎成数块，碎片散开，房间很安静。碰地的一刻有清脆高亮的碎裂声，几颗碎片随后在石地上轻跳，落点和声响相合。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a low-angle close-up captures a static shot framed at the perimeter of a dark walnut wood tabletop in the upper frame and a smooth, cool-toned slate stone floor stretching across the lower two-thirds of the composition. Soft, cool natural daylight filters from an off-screen window on the left, casting a sharp specular gleam along the polished wooden rim and throwing delicate reflections onto the floor tiles. Perched precariously on the tabletop edge, a cylindrical, thin-walled transparent glass cup slowly loses traction, sliding past the rounded wooden lip. The clear cup tilts forward and falls free into the air, tumbling downward until it forcefully collides with the stone surface. Upon impact, the fragile glass instantly shatters into jagged triangular shards, curved rim segments, and tiny glistening slivers that spray outward in a wide radial arc across the gray flagstones. Several larger transparent fragments bounce lightly off the stone, spinning and skittering along the floor before friction brings them to a halt, leaving the broken glass scattered motionless across the slate in the quiet room.
overall_soundscape: A subtle, faint ambient room tone provides an underlying stillness, abruptly broken by a loud, pronounced, and high-pitched crash of shattering glass upon the hard stone surface, followed by distinct, crisp ricochet clicks and delicate chiming taps as stray fragments bounce and slide across the slate floor before settling into silence.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

反例

碎裂时间正确，声音却像空塑料杯弹地，碎片也发出塑料轻响。

反例原始 prompt

固定近景，桌边一只杯子滑落。镜头拍清透明薄玻璃杯落在石地上碎成数块，碎片散开，房间很安静。碰地的一刻响起空塑料杯弹地般的“扑、哒”，散开的碎片落地仍是短促的塑料轻响，时点全部落在对应动作上。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a static close-up shot angled downward toward the juncture between the beveled edge of a dark walnut dining table in the upper frame and a smooth, cool-toned gray slate stone tile floor stretching across the lower two-thirds of the frame. Soft, diffuse morning light enters from a large off-screen window on the left, casting a faint linear sheen across the polished wood varnish and throwing gentle specular highlights onto the curved lip of a thin, completely transparent cylindrical glass tumbler sitting right on the table's perimeter. The tumbler begins to creep outward over the edge, its flat base losing purchase on the glossy surface before tipping forward into empty air. The glass cup tumbles downward in free fall, twisting through the shadowed space beneath the tabletop overhang. Upon striking the hard slate floor, the delicate body instantaneously fractures into numerous sharp, jagged transparent shards and slivers that burst outward in an irregular radial spread. The largest base section skids several inches across the stone texture, while smaller triangular fragments bounce and tumble with minute secondary hops, sliding across the grout seams before friction arrests their movement, leaving the scattered glass glittering in stillness across the floor as airborne dust motes drift slowly through the quiet room.
overall_soundscape: A deep, quiet ambient room tone establishes a faint hum in the background, interrupted abruptly as the falling cup impacts the stone ground with a distinct, hollow, plastic-like thud and double-clack, followed immediately by a rapid sequence of short, crisp plastic taps and clicks as the scattering shards bounce and skitter across the slate floor before settling into absolute silence.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

08-07 · 音频表现力 · BGM／唱歌／乐器不好听-P1

温室 MV 的唱腔

考点两个长音是否唱在伴奏的音高上？
观察方法听两个长音和伴奏的音高关系。字幕与演唱词仍可对应。

正例

“light”和“night”延长时都能稳稳落在伴奏音高上。

正例原始 prompt

梦幻歌曲MV。玻璃温室里，女生握麦克风深情演唱：“In this garden of light, I find my way through the night.” 她蹙眉、睁眼，让话筒靠近唇边，随旋律轻晃。下方浮出轻盈英文歌词，镜头逆时针缓慢环绕，叶片与玻璃光影交错。歌声空灵，长音音高稳定，最后的“night”自然归到旋律的落音。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium shot at eye level as the camera arcs counterclockwise around the subject at slow speed. In the center of a humid, Victorian-style glass greenhouse, a young East Asian female singer in her early twenties (S1) stands framed by towering monstera leaves, hanging ferns, and iron-ribbed glass windows lightly misted with condensation. She has soft facial features, faint shimmer eye makeup, and wavy, dark brown shoulder-length hair loosely falling over her bare shoulders. She wears an ivory layered-tulle slip dress with thin straps, holding a vintage ribbed chrome microphone close to her chest with both hands cradling the metallic stem. Warm ambient daylight filters through the glass ceiling, complemented by warm golden fairy lights strung among the overhead rafters that cast dappled leaf shadows and luminous rim light along her silhouette. With her brow slightly furrowed in deep emotional immersion, she opens her dark brown eyes and raises the microphone closer to her lips. Gently swaying her shoulders to the rhythm, the young singer (S1) sings with an airy, resonant soprano register, <d>[English] In this garden of light, I find my way through the night.</d> Directly beneath her in the lower third of the frame, delicate semi-translucent glowing white serif text floats above the foliage, displaying "In this garden of light, I find my way through the night." As the camera smoothly revolves, lush palm fronds sweep through the foreground, momentarily slicing the golden rim light into soft bokeh discs. She sustains the closing word "night" with a pitch-stable, floating vibrato before naturally lowering her pitch onto the melody's final resting note, her eyelashes gently fluttering downward as her mouth softly closes on the trailing breath.
overall_soundscape: A gentle greenhouse ambient tone is audible throughout, underscored by the distinct soft rustle of shifting tropical leaves. The singer's quiet intake of air before phonation is clearly heard in the foreground, accompanied by the faint friction of tulle fabric as she sways, followed by a distant, subtle clink of water droplets tapping against overhead glass panels.
non_diegetic_music: Atmospheric dream-pop ballad arrangement featuring lush reverb-heavy synth pads, delicate acoustic guitar fingerpicking, and a warm electric bass pulse at a slow, floating tempo, without percussion.

反例

两个长音持续跑调，歌词和节拍仍能跟上。

反例原始 prompt

梦幻歌曲MV。玻璃温室里，女生握麦克风深情演唱：“In this garden of light, I find my way through the night.” 她蹙眉、睁眼，让话筒靠近唇边，随旋律轻晃。下方浮出轻盈英文歌词，镜头逆时针缓慢环绕，叶片与玻璃光影交错。唱词、拍点清楚，只有“light”和“night”两个长音持续游离于伴奏调性之外，拖出明显走调的音高，尾音与伴奏和弦冲撞。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a medium close-up at eye level where the camera executes a continuous, slow counter-clockwise arc shot around a young East Asian woman in her early twenties (S1) standing in the center of a lush Victorian glass greenhouse. She has delicate features, soft wavy brown hair cascading over her bare shoulders, and wears an ethereal ivory silk-chiffon dress with delicate translucent ruffles along the neckline. Her slender fingers are curled securely around the body of a polished vintage chrome microphone mounted on a slim black metal stand. Surrounding her in the midground and background are dense tropical monstera leaves, tall potted fiddle-leaf figs, and hanging ferns, backlit by soft, hazy afternoon sunlight that pierces through the ornate green-iron greenhouse panes, casting shifting organic dappled shadows across her porcelain skin. With her brow furrowed in emotional intensity, she keeps her eyes closed momentarily before slowly fluttering them open, swaying her torso with the gentle rhythmic cadence of the song. Bringing the microphone closer until it nearly brushes her glossy lips, she delivers her vocal line with clear rhythmic articulation, <d>[English] In this garden of light, I find my way through the night.</d> While her tempo and syllabic clarity remain crisp, her voice stretches the vowel on "light" into a sharp, off-key wandering pitch, and sustains the final syllable of "night" in a jarring, unanchored microtonal dissonance that audibly grates against the chordal harmony. Floating near the bottom center of the frame in an elegant, translucent white cursive font, the on-screen text reads "In this garden of light, I find my way through the night." As the camera sweeps past an out-of-focus green palm frond in the foreground, she lets out a delicate, lingering exhalation, her shoulders dropping slightly while holding her gaze wistfully into the distance.
overall_soundscape: A distinct greenhouse air tone hums gently in the background, accompanied by the faint rustling of broad leaves brushing together. The subtle tactile friction of fingers adjusting their grip on the cool metal microphone stand is clearly heard, followed by a pronounced, intimate breath intake against the microphone mesh before each vocal attack, with quiet damp ambient drips echoing faintly in the far distance.
non_diegetic_music: Slow-tempo dream pop backing track with shimmering synth pads, gentle electric guitar arpeggios, and a warm, steady bass groove at 72 BPM, whose polished major-seventh chords clash starkly against the dissonant, pitch-drifted sustained vocal notes.

08-09 · 音频表现力 · 环境音不真实-P1

两个孩子在岸边泼水

考点背景海浪是否暴露出机械重复？
观察方法把近处真实的泼水与后方重复的海浪底声分开听。

正例

远处浪声每次涌起、退去的时长和形态有变化。

正例原始 prompt

两个孩子在海边站起身。男孩用双手捧水泼向女孩，女孩开心笑着回击，飞溅的水花打湿衣服。近处每次泼水都有短促的水花声，两人的笑声自然。背后的海浪有推上沙岸、泡沫散开、向后退潮的宽阔声音，起落间距略有变化。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a medium wide shot with a slow push in at low angle, two children rise from the wet sand at the shoreline. On the left, an eight-year-old boy with sun-tanned skin, tousled short dark hair, wearing a navy-and-white striped cotton crewneck t-shirt and beige cargo shorts, pushes himself up from a crouch. Opposite him on the right stands a seven-year-old girl with shoulder-length brown hair tied into two low pigtails, dressed in a lemon-yellow linen sleeveless top and denim shorts, brushing wet sand off her knees. The setting is an expansive beach during late afternoon; warm, directional sunlight glints off the damp, mirror-like sand and sea spray, while the background features gentle ocean swells that roll toward the coast, cresting in white foam before flattening against the shore. The boy grins playfully, bends swiftly at the knees, cups both hands into the shallow clear water, and flings an arc of sparkling sea water directly toward the girl. The airborne splash hits her chest and shoulders, visibly soaking and darkening the fabric of her yellow top. The girl recoils playfully with a bright, spontaneous laugh, hunching her shoulders and raising an elbow to shield her face as droplets scatter across her sunlit skin. [Shot 2] At 00:07.200, the camera cuts to a medium shot from a reverse three-quarter angle over the boy's shoulder, tracking slightly right as the girl from Shot 1 retaliates. Her damp bangs cling lightly to her forehead, and her face lights up with an open, delighted smile. She immediately stoops down, plunges both hands into the foamy ripple, and scoops a heavy spray of sea water upward toward the boy from Shot 1. The dense cluster of water droplets catches the golden sunlight as it arcs through the air, splattering visibly against the boy's striped shirt and wetting his arms. The boy stumbles back a half-step in the shallow surf, raising both forearms in mock defense while laughing aloud. Frothy sea foam spreads around their bare feet, bubbling and fizzing on the shore before slowly receding outward, while both children continue shifting their weight and laughing in the glittering shallow surf.
overall_soundscape: The scene opens with the soft squelch of wet sand underfoot as the children stand, followed immediately by a distinct, sharp splash and slosh of hands scooping into seawater. A pronounced splat and hiss of airborne water droplets striking wet cotton fabric and bare skin repeats clearly with each counter-splash, mingled with audible, natural children's giggles and bright chuckles in the foreground. In the background, a broad and continuous ocean swell crashes with a low rumble, followed by the audible fizzing crackle of white foam dissipating on the sand and the resonant, rhythmic whoosh of water sliding back toward the sea.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

反例

后方同一小段水声精确循环，前景孩子的泼水仍自然。

反例原始 prompt

两个孩子在海边站起身。男孩用双手捧水泼向女孩，女孩开心笑着回击，飞溅的水花打湿衣服。近处每次泼水都有短促的水花声，两人的笑声自然。背后的“海浪”从头到尾像一段半秒长的水流录音被等间隔循环，每次起伏、尾音和音量完全一样，循环接点规律可闻。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium wide shot from a slightly low angle as the camera pushes in slowly across a wet shoreline. In the center midground, two children—a young East Asian boy around seven years old wearing a blue-and-white striped cotton t-shirt and navy shorts, and a young East Asian girl around seven years old wearing a light yellow cotton sundress with twin pigtails—stand up from crouching in the shallow surf. The boy bends forward, cups both hands into the foamy seawater, and vigorously flings a crest of glistening water forward toward the girl, sending bright droplets arcing through the air. Natural, diffuse daylight from an overcast coastal sky illuminates the wet sand and ripples around their bare feet, casting subtle reflections, while the calm sea horizon stretches across the background. [Shot 2] At 00:05.100, the camera cuts to a medium shot facing the young girl from Shot 1 as the camera pans right smoothly. Water droplets from the splash strike her yellow dress, creating visible dark damp spots across her chest and shoulders. The girl flinches with a bright grin, blinking away sea spray before letting out an audible, joyful chuckle. She leans down with bent knees, plunges both hands into the shallow water, and scoops upward with force, launching a wide curved sheet of sparkling sea spray back toward the boy off-screen to the right. [Shot 3] At 00:09.800, the camera cuts to a side-angle medium shot framing both children from Shot 1 in profile against the open sea as the camera trucks right at a steady pace. The arc of water thrown by the girl strikes the boy squarely across his soaked striped shirt, causing him to stagger back half a step with his arms flailing lightly while laughing openly with crinkled eyes. Both children continue alternately cupping and splashing water at each other in playful exchange, their bare feet kicking up white froth in the wet sand under the pale daylight as fine spray glistens in the air around them.
overall_soundscape: A distinct, sharp splash sound is clearly heard with each cupping and tossing motion of water, followed by audible pattering of droplets hitting wet cotton fabric and the shallow water surface. Natural, bright children's giggles and cheerful laughter ring out in the immediate foreground, followed by soft sloshing of bare feet stepping through shallow surf. In the background, a peculiar wave sound plays continuously like an identical half-second water flow audio sample repeating at exact, regular intervals, each wave swell, decay tail, and volume level sounding mechanically uniform with distinct audible loop points.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

08-10 · 音频表现力 · 动物叫声不真实-P1

人物变熊后的第一声

考点变身后首次张嘴发出的是什么动物声？
观察方法聚焦变身后第一次出声，对照可见动物与声源种类。

正例

熊第一次张嘴时听到熊吼。

正例原始 prompt

镜头绕着一名低头的人物旋转并推近。人物突然抬头，张嘴大声咆哮，表情激动、带一点喜剧色彩，随后在画面中变成体型壮硕的熊。变身完毕、熊第一次张嘴时，是低沉有胸腔共鸣的熊吼，开始和结束都与这次张嘴吻合。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, the camera executes an arc shot around the central subject, rotating smoothly from a three-quarter profile toward a low-angle frontal medium close-up while pushing in. In the center of a damp pine forest clearing littered with fallen pine needles and mossy boulders, a stocky man in his early thirties stands with his head bowed low, wearing a coarse earth-brown wool tunic, riveted leather vambraces, and a weathered fur-trimmed leather vest. Soft, diffused daylight filters through the tall fir canopy, casting cool silver rim light across his shoulders while thin mist drifts through the deep green background. His shoulders shudder slightly before he suddenly snaps his head up toward the lens, his eyes popping wide and his eyebrows arching in a comically exaggerated, fierce grimace. He stretches his jaws wide to unleash a loud, impassioned roar. Mid-bellow, his torso visibly swells and broadens: thick, coarse dark-brown grizzly fur rapidly sprouts across his skin and engulfs his garments, his jaw elongates into a massive snout, and his arms thicken into heavy, muscular forelimbs, executing a seamless in-frame transformation into a colossal brown bear. Upon completing the metamorphosis, the massive beast firmly plants its heavy front paws onto the earth, twitching its rounded ears and letting out a brief puff of condensation. The bear then tilts its heavy skull backward and levers its jaws wide apart, exposing ivory fangs and a pink palate as a deep, booming roar with heavy chest resonance reverberates outwards, the vocalization starting cleanly the instant the maw gapes open and terminating precisely as its heavy black-lipped jaws clamp shut.
overall_soundscape: A distinct rustle of dry leaves and pine needles stirs under shifting boots, immediately overtaken by a loud, theatrical human bellow dominant in the foreground. Heavy tearing creaks and a rapid series of fleshy thuds accompany the transformation, followed by the pronounced thump of massive paws striking damp forest soil. A deep, guttural, chest-resonating bear roar vibrates cleanly through the air, perfectly synchronized to begin as the bear's jaws gape open and cut off cleanly as the jaws snap shut, followed by the faint whistle of wind through towering pine boughs.
non_diegetic_music: Low sustained contrabass and cello drone at a slow tempo, building steadily with a mid-tempo war drum pulse and a swelling low brass crescendo during the transformation, then the score alone stops as the bear roars.

反例

同一张熊嘴发出鸡鸣，开闭时点仍然合拍。

反例原始 prompt

镜头绕着一名低头的人物旋转并推近。人物突然抬头，张嘴大声咆哮，表情激动、带一点喜剧色彩，随后在画面中变成体型壮硕的熊。变身完毕、熊第一次张嘴时，是一声清脆高亢的鸡鸣，起止精确对应熊的嘴部动作，和变身前的人类喊声清楚分开。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium shot at eye level, the camera performs an Arc Shot around a solitary man while slowly closing in toward him. The man, in his late twenties with a rugged build and unkempt shoulder-length dark brown hair, is initially crouched low with his head bowed, his shoulders rising and falling with rapid breaths. He wears a frayed dark olive linen tunic, distressed leather arm bracers, and coarse grey wool trousers. He sits in a misty forest clearing surrounded by tall, damp spruce trunks and a forest floor carpeted with dark pine needles, scattered grey river stones, and patches of deep green moss. Diffuse cool blue twilight blankets the background, while a soft warm rim light from an off-screen campfire grazes his hair and the contours of his shoulders. Suddenly, he jerks his head upward, eyes bulging with an exaggerated, comedic expression of ferocious fury, his mouth stretching wide open into a theatrical, wide-mouthed roar. As the camera continues its smooth circular path, his body rapidly swells within the frame: coarse, glossy dark brown fur rapidly sprouts over his face and limbs, the seams of his tunic splitting and falling away as his musculoskeletal frame morphs seamlessly into an enormous, muscular brown bear. The transformation settles as the massive bear drops heavily onto all fours, shaking its broad head and twitching its rounded ears. The bear then plants its heavy front paws firmly on the damp soil, stretches its neck upward, and opens its massive black-lipped jaws wide to reveal sharp canine teeth; the wide-open mouth remains held in place for the full duration of a sharp, high-pitched rooster crow, before snapping shut abruptly with a subtle twitch of its snout, leaving the bear blinking its dark beady eyes with a comically bewildered gaze.
overall_soundscape: A loud, strained human roar dominant in the foreground, accompanied by the distinct rustle of coarse linen and sharp tearing sounds as seams burst over damp earth. A brief interval follows with the bear silent and only the heavy thud of massive paws hitting soft mossy soil. Immediately upon the jaws parting, a piercing, loud, and crystal-clear rooster crow rings out sharply in the foreground, perfectly synchronized to start at the first opening of the jaws and end with an audible wet snap the moment the jaws close.
non_diegetic_music: Low cello drone paired with driving, mid-tempo orchestral timpani rolls and brass swells that crescendo during the physical metamorphosis, with the score alone cutting off just before the jaws open.

08-12 · 音频表现力 · 声场混响不匹配-P1

跨次元少女的回声

考点近讲台词的混响是否符合开阔花地？
观察方法用最后一字落下后的空间尾声，与画面开阔花地对照。

正例

女孩的话音贴近，停顿和句末的尾声短而自然。

正例原始 prompt

固定近景，焦点在女孩的面部和伸向镜头的手上。左侧黑白蝴蝶扇动素描线翅，右侧彩色帝王蝶穿过星芒。女孩握紧拳头，左右两半脸同时说：“就算打破次元的界限，我也想触碰真实的你。”话音落下，墨迹和光芒在指尖交融，两侧蝴蝶越过中线。背景是一片开阔、柔软的花地；声线空灵坚定。对白仿佛从眼前近处传来，每个停顿自然收住，尾韵轻短。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, static shot framing a close-up centered on a 17-year-old girl and her outstretched hand. Her face is split directly down the center along a vertical dimensional boundary: the left half is rendered in delicate monochrome graphite sketch art with fine hatched shading and a pale silver iris beneath wispy, hand-drawn black hair strands, while the right half features smooth photorealistic skin, a radiant amber eye, and soft chestnut hair softly catching ambient light. She wears a high-collared shirt that transitions from rough charcoal-textured fabric on the left to soft cream-colored linen on the right. In the foreground, her hand extends toward the lens with an open palm before steadily curling into a tight, deliberate fist. In the midground on the left, a black-and-white sketch butterfly flutters with papery line-art wings, shedding faint carbon dust; on the right, a vivid orange-and-black Monarch butterfly navigates through shimmering, microscopic star-shaped sparkles. Looking directly into the camera with unwavering focus, both sides of her mouth move in unison as the ethereal, resolute young female on-screen speaker (S1) speaks directly toward the lens, <d>[Chinese] 就算打破次元的界限，我也想触碰真实的你。</d> Upon concluding her speech, she loosens her knuckles slightly, allowing wisps of liquid black ink and vibrant golden light to coil and merge around her fingertips. The sketch butterfly flutters smoothly past the vertical midline toward the right, while the Monarch butterfly crosses to the left, their respective dust and light trails intersecting seamlessly. Behind her, an expansive, softly blurred meadow of wildflowers spreads across the background, shifting from monochrome ink washes on the left to vibrant golden-hour greens and purples on the right, illuminated by a gentle frontal key light complemented by cool edge lighting on the sketch half and warm rim lighting on the colorful half.
overall_soundscape: Her close-miked voice has a short, natural tail with no distinct repeat, even on the final “你”. A distinct, delicate fluttering of paper-like butterfly wings mixes with the softer, organic wing hum of the Monarch in the foreground, accompanied by the quiet rustle of linen fabric as her knuckles tighten. A faint, audible crackle and crystalline chime resonates as the ink and starlight particles blend at her fingertips, layered over a soft, continuous breeze whispering through meadow grass in the background.
non_diegetic_music: Sparse acoustic celesta and glass harmonica chords played at a slow tempo, supported by a gentle, sustained low cello drone that holds an even dynamic level without swells.

反例

每句停顿都传回很长的硬反射，像在封闭大厅，画面却是开阔花地。

反例原始 prompt

固定近景，焦点在女孩的面部和伸向镜头的手上。左侧黑白蝴蝶扇动素描线翅，右侧彩色帝王蝶穿过星芒。女孩握紧拳头，左右两半脸同时说：“就算打破次元的界限，我也想触碰真实的你。”话音落下，墨迹和光芒在指尖交融，两侧蝴蝶越过中线。背景是一片开阔、柔软的花地；声线空灵坚定。每一小句都拖出两秒以上空水泥大厅般的硬质混响，下一句起声时上一句仍在清晰反弹，尤其在“真实的你”后能听出多次回音。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a static close-up shot framed at eye level, the camera holds perfectly still on the face of a teenage girl (S1) and her hand extending directly toward the lens. The girl, approximately seventeen years old, is visually split along a razor-sharp vertical centerline: the left half of her face, braided dark hair, and collar are rendered in fine monochrome graphite sketch lines and cross-hatching, while the right half features smooth photorealistic skin, flowing glossy chestnut hair, and a soft lavender knit neckline under warm sunlight. 08-13 · 音频表现力 · 台词机械感-P0

短句“就是现在”的机械感

考点四个字的重音和连读是否自然？
观察方法即使四字全对，仍可单独听出重音、连接和尾音是否机械。

正例

“就是现在”有自然的轻重、连接和收尾。

正例原始 prompt

中近景表现虚构片场的古装男子拉开道具弓。绷紧声轻响，呼吸慢而沉，他停顿一下，压低声音说：“就是现在。”随后一声“嗖”掠过，远处传来闷响；男子放下弓，呼出一口气。这四个字带自然连读和轻重，“现在”略加重，尾字随呼气收住。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium close-up, eye-level framing, the camera pushes in at slow speed. Centered in the frame stands an adult male actor in his late twenties with a focused, resolute expression (S1), styled in ancient Chinese martial attire featuring layered charcoal-grey textured linen robes, dark leather armguards fastened with bronze rivets, and his long black hair swept into a high topknot bound by a carved wooden hairpin. He stands on a cinematic studio soundstage dressed as an ancient nocturnal encampment, flanked by weathered wooden palisades, hemp sandbags, and subtle studio mist catching the lights, while the dark silhouette of a lighting stand is softly blurred in the deep background. Overhead studio fresnel fixtures cast a sharp, cool blue rim light along his jawline and the curved silhouette of the wooden prop bow, balanced by a warm directional key light from camera-right illuminating the intense concentration in his eyes. He firmly draws back the bowstring with leather-wrapped fingers, the taut line quivering slightly as his chest rises and falls with a deliberate, slow, deep breath. He pauses motionlessly at full draw, eyes locked on an off-screen mark, slightly tilts his chin down, and the male actor (S1) lowers his voice to an intimate, resolute whisper with natural liaison and deliberate emphasis on the final two words, trailing into his breath, <d>[Chinese] 就是现在。</d> Right after the line, an off-screen whistling sound passes overhead, punctuated by a distant muffled impact against wood; his tensed knuckles immediately relax, his shoulders drop slightly in relief, and he slowly lowers the prop bow toward his hip while exhaling a long, audible breath of air.
overall_soundscape: The pronounced creak of drawn bowstring tension is clearly heard in the foreground alongside deep, slow audible breathing, followed by the sharp, distinct whoosh of an arrow whistling off-screen and a loud dull thud reverberating from a distant wooden target, concluding with the soft rustle of linen robes and a distinct sigh of exhalation.
non_diegetic_music: Sustained low sub-bass drone, very slow tempo, paired with a faint bowing on a suspended cymbal that remains sparse and steady with no swell.

反例

四字等长、等高、等响，每个音像单独拼接。

反例原始 prompt

中近景表现虚构片场的古装男子拉开道具弓。绷紧声轻响，呼吸慢而沉，他停顿一下，压低声音说：“就是现在。”随后一声“嗖”掠过，远处传来闷响；男子放下弓，呼出一口气。这四个字每个字长度、音量、音高完全一致，每个字之间停同样长的短间隔，像四个语音片段逐字拼接；发音清楚，呼吸仍自然。

反例最终 PE prompt

He wears a dark charcoal layered linen hanfu with a maroon under-collar, his long black hair partially bound in a high topknot with a simple wooden hairpin while loose strands frame his sharp, pale face. His left arm extends forward gripping a traditional dark-lacquered recurve prop bow, while his right gloved hand slowly pulls the bowstring taut toward his cheek. 09-03a · 音质与表现力补充、音频参考生 · 音乐听感不好，一段欧美片，在餐厅，并且是类似恐惧这类的发展叙事，用了中国江南小调和丝竹乐器的配乐。

赌场餐厅的配乐选择

考点配乐的情绪是否配合恐惧逐渐加深的叙事？
观察方法判断的是轻快游园情绪与当前叙事的冲突。不要把某种乐器或地域音乐本身视为错误。

正例

克制的低弦随推轨和低语维持紧张感。

正例原始 prompt

欧美写实悬疑片。一名穿西装的男子静立在赌场内与游戏厅相连的餐厅，透过门能看到旋转的老虎机。摄影机做一次希区柯克式推轨变焦，男子在画面中大小稳定，身后的餐桌和街机空间被逐渐拉长。保留老虎机的机械旋转声、远桌人群的低语和持续的低频环境音，气氛紧张压抑。
当镜头开始推轨，以克制的低弦长音和不安的低音脉冲进入配乐；节拍慢，直到背景被拉得最深才轻轻收紧，环境声始终可辨。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in an eye-level medium shot, the camera slowly pushes in toward a motionless man while the lens simultaneously zooms out, keeping his physical proportion locked in center frame while the background elongates into exaggerated depth. The subject, a Caucasian male in his late thirties with a sharp jawline, light facial stubble, and slicked-back dark brown hair, stands upright wearing a tailored charcoal-grey wool suit jacket, matching trousers, a crisp white dress shirt with a spread collar, and a matte black silk necktie. His posture remains rigid as his eyes stare fixedly forward; his jaw muscles clench twice, his throat bobs in a subtle swallow, and his chest rises with shallow, disciplined breaths. He stands in the foreground of a dim, moody casino dining room. Surrounding him in the midground are polished dark mahogany dining tables adorned with brass hooded lamps, folded cloth napkins, and half-empty highball glasses catching amber reflections. Behind him, an open wide archway exposes the neighboring casino arcade, where rows of upright slot machines display spinning mechanical reels adorned with glowing symbols alongside illuminated neon marquee signs. Lighting is low-key, with warm tungsten pools cast downward by table fixtures while vivid cyan and magenta neon light filters through the doorway, creating high-contrast cool rim lighting along the man's shoulders and temples. As the camera's physical forward movement and optical zoom divergence continue smoothly, the dining tables, chandeliers, and distant flashing slot machines stretch into a deepening, receding tunnel of space while the man maintains his exact scale.
overall_soundscape: The mechanical whir and rhythmic clatter of spinning slot machine reels are clearly heard from the open doorway in the background. A pronounced, persistent low-frequency HVAC rumble and electrical hum fills the room tone, interwoven with faint, muffled whispers from distant restaurant patrons and the subtle clink of silverware against porcelain. The man's faint, shallow breathing remains distinct in the foreground as the ambient casino drone continues uninterrupted.
non_diegetic_music: Sustained low-register double bass drones accompanied by a slow, repetitive sub-bass pulse at a measured tempo, gradually tightening in rhythmic density toward the end without increasing in volume, allowing the diegetic environment to remain fully audible.

反例

镜头仍在加深恐惧，乐句却轻快摇曳，听感像轻松游园。

反例原始 prompt

欧美写实悬疑片。一名穿西装的男子静立在赌场内与游戏厅相连的餐厅，透过门能看到旋转的老虎机。摄影机做一次希区柯克式推轨变焦，男子在画面中大小稳定，身后的餐桌和街机空间被逐渐拉长。保留老虎机的机械旋转声、远桌人群的低语和持续的低频环境音，气氛紧张压抑。

09-04 · 音质与表现力补充、音频参考生 · 滑雪的音效材质不对。

雪板穿旗门

考点雪板切雪时的摩擦声是否像松雪？
观察方法看板刃确实接触粉雪，再听材质是否像金属刮硬地。此例的事件时机正确。

正例

板刃接触雪地时响起有颗粒的沙沙声，离雪即停。

正例原始 prompt

第一人称从雪山顶俯冲，雪粉飞溅，切航拍，一个小黑点在白雪上划出S形。跟着滑雪者穿旗门、飞跃跳台，落地加速冲过终点。风在耳旁呼啸，腾空短暂安静，落地有撞击，终点响起欢呼。
在雪板贴地切出S弯、掠过旗门时，听见有颗粒、带摩擦的松雪沙沙声，速度越快越密。离雪腾空时切雪声消失。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, close-up POV shot, the camera dives forward at high velocity down a steep mountain ridge. The glossy tips of carbon alpine skis and black composite ski poles extend into the bottom edges of the frame, angled sharply down a pristine white slope. Billowing plumes of fine, powdery white snow erupt outward and upward, catching the glare of crisp, direct morning sunlight that illuminates jagged alpine peaks against a clear azure sky. [Shot 2] At 00:03.600, the camera cuts to an extreme wide aerial shot tilting down while tracking forward high above an expansive glacier bowl. A lone skier appears as a small dark silhouette in a dark technical suit, carving elegant, fluid S-shaped tracks across the untouched expanse of glaring white snow, flanked by rugged granite crags and long blue-tinted shadows cast by the ridgeline. [Shot 3] At 00:07.200, the shot transitions to a medium tracking shot following closely behind and low alongside the skier at fast speed. The athlete, wearing a streamlined matte-black and fluorescent-yellow ski suit with a silver mirrored helmet visor, leans hard into aggressive edge carves, grazing past alternating flexible red and blue slalom gates that whip backward upon impact. White granulated snow sprays outward in dense fans from the sharp steel edges. Approaching a raised snow kicker, the skier crouches low, explodes off the lip, and soars into a high, suspended arc against the brilliant blue sky with ski tips perfectly parallel. [Shot 4] At 00:11.300, the camera cuts to a low-angle tracking shot panning right as the skier lands heavily on the downslope. Snow puffs outward from beneath the ski bases as the skier absorbs the downward force with bent knees, quickly tucks into a low aerodynamic bullet position, and accelerates across packed snow. Flanked by bright orange safety fences, inflatable sponsor arches, and spectators waving winter gloves, the skier rockets across the bright red finish line mark.
overall_soundscape: A distinct, rushing roar of high-altitude wind howling past the ears is clearly heard throughout the high-speed descent, accompanied by the pronounced, granular hiss and dense friction scrape of ski edges slicing into packed snow during rapid carving turns. The moment the skier goes airborne, the carving friction instantly cuts out into a brief, hushed whoosh of atmospheric wind, followed by a loud, heavy thud and sharp slap of skis impacting the slope. As the skier speeds across the finish line, dominant cheers, excited clatter of ski poles, and enthusiastic crowd shouting erupt loudly in the foreground.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

时点仍对，声音却像硬金属刮过水泥。

09-08b · 音质与表现力补充、音频参考生 · 音色不一致

佐佐木晚班车独白（中文）

考点中文内心独白是否整段缺失？
观察方法从挤进车厢听到微笑结束。先找可辨的中文男声。若整段没有人声，记录独白缺失，音色无法比较。

正例

进车厢后能听到男子以心声说“今天又累了一整天啊”等中文台词。

正例原始 prompt

日式写实短剧。傍晚繁华的东京车站，成年男子佐佐木先生匆匆赶晚班车。先从站台跟拍他穿过人群，再拍列车驶入、开门，他侧身挤上车；车门边切面部近景，他嘴唇始终闭合，露出一点疲倦的微笑。站台有列车、脚步和人群声，全程没有背景音乐。脚本中的内心独白是中文「今天又累了一整天啊，晚上去超市买点东西放松一下吧……」。独白时段从他挤进车厢开始，直到近景微笑结束。
成片中确实能听到他在心里用成年男子略疲惫的低声完整念出独白，居中清晰，仍听得见车厢和外面的人群。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a medium tracking shot, the camera follows an adult Japanese man in his late 30s, Sasaki, from behind and to the side as he navigates quickly through a bustling Tokyo train station at dusk. Sasaki has neatly parted but slightly tousled black hair, weary eyes with subtle dark circles, and is dressed in a dark charcoal wool two-piece suit over a white cotton dress shirt with a slightly loosened silk tie, clutching a black leather briefcase in his right hand. Commuters in neutral trench coats and dark coats stream across the textured beige tiled platform around him, passing beneath glowing hanging directional signs displaying Japanese kanji and route numbers. Cool overhead strip lighting mingles with the fading indigo daylight visible through upper platform vents. Sasaki deftly steps around a standing passenger, checks his wristwatch, and quickens his stride toward the platform edge as train headlights sweep across the safety partition glass. [Shot 2] At 00:05.200, the camera cuts to a medium static shot framing the platform screen doors as a silver commuter train with emerald green side stripes smoothly halts, its metal body gleaming under fluorescent station lamps. With an audible melodic chime and pneumatic hiss, the automated platform doors and the train's double doors glide open simultaneously. Commuters quickly surge forward, and Sasaki from Shot 1 turns sideways to squeeze through the opening into the packed compartment, tucking his briefcase close to his torso between standing passengers. As he edges his way past the threshold, an adult male voiceover with a weary, low tone (S1) reflects internally, <d>[Chinese] 今天又累了一整天啊，<scenetrans></d> while his lips remain completely closed. Sasaki exhales quietly through his nose, turning his body toward the interior glass divider beside the train entrance. [Shot 3] At 00:10.300, the shot transitions to a close-up where the camera pushes in at slow speed toward Sasaki (S1) as he stands braced against the glass partition adjacent to the closed carriage door. The audio from the previous shot continues over the transition. Warm interior carriage lighting softly illuminates his face from above, while the blurred lights of the platform slowly begin to shift outside the door window behind him. Standing shoulder-to-shoulder with other silent commuters, the tired salaryman (S1) finishes his thought in voiceover, <d>[Chinese] <scenetrans>晚上去超市买点东西放松一下吧……</d> with his mouth remaining still throughout. Upon finishing his internal reflection, Sasaki's tensed shoulders visibly drop an inch, the fatigue around his brow softens, and his closed lips gently curve into a subtle, weary smile.
overall_soundscape: A bustling platform ambiance fills the foreground with the distinct, loud click-clack of rushing leather shoes echoing off station tiles, mingled with low murmurs of the moving crowd and clear, resonant bilingual train arrival announcements over overhead speakers. The heavy deceleration of the train brings a pronounced mechanical whine and the screech of steel brakes, followed immediately by the bright electronic two-tone chime and an audible pneumatic hiss of sliding doors opening. Inside the carriage, fabric rustles audibly as passengers brush against one another, settling into a subdued, muffled metallic hum of the idling train car.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

反例

直到近景微笑结束，只有站台和车厢声，整段听不到人声。

反例原始 prompt

日式写实短剧。傍晚繁华的东京车站，成年男子佐佐木先生匆匆赶晚班车。先从站台跟拍他穿过人群，再拍列车驶入、开门，他侧身挤上车；车门边切面部近景，他嘴唇始终闭合，露出一点疲倦的微笑。站台有列车、脚步和人群声，全程没有背景音乐。脚本中的内心独白是中文「今天又累了一整天啊，晚上去超市买点东西放松一下吧……」。独白时段从他挤进车厢开始，直到近景微笑结束。
把整个独白时段的人声轨设为静音，成片里一句内心话也听不见；保留同样的闭口微笑、脚步、车门声和站台环境音，不要把他的台词改成别人念或改作字幕。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a medium shot using a tracking shot moving backward ahead of a Japanese man in his late thirties named Mr. Sasaki, who strides briskly through a bustling crowd on an evening platform at Tokyo Station. Mr. Sasaki has an average, slender build, neat short black hair slightly ruffled by movement, and tired eyes behind thin wire-rimmed glasses, wearing a charcoal-gray tailored wool suit jacket and matching trousers, a crisp white collared dress shirt with a loosened navy-and-silver striped silk tie, and carrying a structured black leather briefcase in his right hand. He navigates between midground commuters dressed in muted trench coats and dark overcoats moving in opposite directions, passing illuminated suspended overhead signs displaying station names in Japanese and English, silver metal safety barrier railings, and yellow tactile paving tiles set into the gray composite platform floor. Overhead rows of linear fluorescent fixtures cast cool white, directional light across the platform, glinting off metallic surfaces and highlighting the weary tension in his furrowed brow as he swivels his head left to check an overhead digital departure display, clutching his briefcase tighter against his hip while accelerating his pace. [Shot 2] At 00:05.300, the camera cuts to an eye-level medium-wide shot using a static shot framed perpendicular to the tracks, capturing the sleek silver exterior of an arriving commuter train as it decelerates and comes to a halt along the platform edge. Bright interior halogen lighting streams through the train's wide rectangular windows, illuminating passengers seated inside. Pneumatic sliding double doors part with a mechanical hiss, and Mr. Sasaki from Shot 1 steps forward from the center foreground, adjusts his posture, turns his torso sideways, and carefully squeezes past entering and exiting commuters through the narrow doorway into the crowded train car, gripping his briefcase close to his chest and ducking his head slightly to clear the overhead frame. [Shot 3] At 00:10.400, the camera cuts to a close-up shot using a static shot framed inside the carriage, focusing squarely on the face of Mr. Sasaki from Shot 1 as he stands propped beside the frosted glass partition of the train doorway. The bright, sterile ceiling lights of the carriage illuminate his features from above, casting soft shadows beneath his cheekbones and tired eyes. His lips remain firmly closed throughout, with no audible dialogue or voiceover, as his shoulders gently drop in a slow release of physical tension and the corners of his mouth subtly lift into a faint, exhausted smile of quiet relief while he gazes slightly downward at his shoes.
overall_soundscape: A loud ambient murmur of bustling commuters and the distinct, pronounced clatter of hurried leather shoes against tiled platform pavement dominate the foreground. The heavy mechanical rumble of an arriving commuter train steadily crescendos, accompanied by the sharp hiss of pneumatic brakes and clear electronic chime tones as sliding train doors open. Inside the carriage, the muffled hum of the ventilation system and the solid thud of train doors sliding shut are clearly audible alongside distant platform chatter.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

09-11 · 音质与表现力补充、音频参考生 · 音色差异

教堂里的管风琴

考点长按琴键时，声音是否属于管风琴？
观察方法盯住手指长按不动的一段，对比持续管音与击弦后衰减的钢琴声。

正例

手指按住时管音饱满持续，换键时声部衔接。

正例原始 prompt

教堂演奏短片。一名穿正装的男士端坐管风琴前，镜头近拍手指起落弹琴，保持一个长按的和音，再硬切至男士脸部，他仰头深情开唱：“The old church glow echoes my song，Night wind carries my longing along.” 键盘乐器旋律在演唱下延续，演奏与手部动作同步。
键被持续按下时，教堂管风琴的音高稳定、音量由气柱维持，有饱满延续的管音；换键时听得到声部衔接，随后人声自然进入。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, close-up shot with a slow push in toward the polished wooden manuals of a grand pipe organ inside a stone cathedral. Framed tightly in the lower center, the manicured hands of an adult man in his late thirties with fair skin and neatly trimmed nails articulate across the ivory and ebony keys. He wears a tailored dark navy wool suit jacket with crisp white shirt cuffs secured by understated silver cufflinks. The ornate mahogany console features polished pull-stop knobs flanking the dual keyboard tiers, bathed in warm amber candlelight from nearby brass wall sconces and cool diffused daylight filtering from high gothic windows. The hands fluently press and release individual keys before settling firmly into a complex, sustained multi-finger chord, the tendons in his wrists visibly taut under the held harmonic pressure. [Shot 2] At 00:05.500, the camera cuts to a medium close-up, low-angle static shot framing the man (S1) from the chest up. The audio from the pipe organ continues uninterrupted over this visual. The adult male singer (S1) has slicked-back dark brown hair, sharp refined facial features, a clean-shaven jawline, and wears the dark navy suit paired with a stiff white dress shirt and a dark silk necktie. Behind him in the cathedral midground, rows of towering polished zinc and tin organ pipes rise toward soaring stone ribbed vaulting. His shoulders subtly shift as his arms work the unseen keyboard below frame. Tilting his head upward toward the vaulting with glistening eyes, the man (S1) draws a measured breath and sings passionately in a warm, resonant baritone, <d>[English] The old church glow echoes my song，Night wind carries my longing along.</d> His brow contracts with emotional weight as his vocal cords modulate the vibrato, his lips gently parting on the sustained final consonant while his gaze lingers reverently on the illuminated high arches.
overall_soundscape: A deep, resonant pipe organ drone fills the space with continuous acoustic airflow, producing a dominant in the foreground, vibration-heavy chord that sustains seamlessly. Audible subtle wooden mechanical clicks occur as keys depress and release across the manuals, accompanied by the faint rustle of wool suit fabric against the console ledge. Soft ambient cathedral reverb carries a distinct whisper of cool drafts drifting through the cavernous stone chamber.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

反例

一按键就有钢琴式击弦音头，仍按住时声音已经逐渐衰减。

反例原始 prompt

教堂演奏短片。一名穿正装的男士端坐管风琴前，镜头近拍手指起落弹琴，保持一个长按的和音，再硬切至男士脸部，他仰头深情开唱：“The old church glow echoes my song，Night wind carries my longing along.” 键盘乐器旋律在演唱下延续，演奏与手部动作同步。
从第一下按键到硬切后的人声伴奏，整条乐器声都换成纯钢琴音色：每个音头有清脆的击弦感，按住键时响度逐渐衰减。画面中的乐器仍为教堂管风琴，音符、节奏、同步和男士演唱都照常。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a close-up from a high angle, the camera pushes in at slow speed toward the tiered keyboard manual of a massive cathedral pipe organ. The hands and wrists of a man in his thirties are centered, framed by the tailored sleeves of a charcoal-black wool suit jacket and crisp white cotton shirt cuffs fastened with polished silver cufflinks. His slender, disciplined fingers press down firmly on the ivory and ebony keys, playing a measured musical phrase before holding down a full harmonic chord. Visually, the instrument is an ornate church organ with brass stop knobs and carved mahogany side panels bathed in warm amber candlelight, yet the sound emanating is that of a pure acoustic grand piano, each key strike delivering a distinct hammer transient that decays naturally into sustained warmth. [Shot 2] At 00:05.200, the camera cuts to a medium close-up from a low angle, where the camera holds a static shot centered on the adult male on-screen singer with a resonant tenor voice (S1). The audio seamlessly continues from the previous shot as the acoustic piano accompaniment sustains uninterrupted. The man (S1), dressed in the charcoal wool suit, white dress shirt, and a dark silk tie, lifts his chin and tilts his head upward toward the high stone arches. His expressive eyes shine with solemn passion, his throat and chest lifting as the well-dressed man (S1) sings with deep emotion, <d>[English] The old church glow echoes my song，Night wind carries my longing along.</d> In the softly blurred background, the grand vertical pipes of the organ rise into the gothic church's vaulted ceiling, illuminated by the warm, diffused glow of high chandeliers and backlit stained-glass windows.
overall_soundscape: The mechanical clack of weighted keyboard keys depressing is clearly heard against the wooden console, accompanied by the distinct rustle of stiff wool suiting. A pronounced, cavernous stone cathedral room tone hangs in the space, underscored by a faint whistle of night wind buffering against tall exterior glass, while crisp acoustic piano hammer strikes and their long, rich harmonic decays ring out loudly across the vaulted hall.
non_diegetic_music: N/A

11-12 · 参考一致性的文字改编 · 场景参考不像

电商直播间的开场

考点第一镜建立的空间是否为直播间？
观察方法只验开场建立的是哪一种空间。

正例

开场便有白色产品桌、补光灯和陈列架。

正例原始 prompt

年轻女主播妆容精致，手持一支正红色口红，微笑面对镜头。从第一镜起就能看到直播间的白色产品桌、补光灯与陈列架，随后切主播中近景。她热情地说：“想要一秒显白提气场，这支无可替代的经典正红，就是你的本命色！”随后将口红涂到嘴唇上，微笑展示。商业广告布光，肤质自然、口红鲜亮。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, medium wide shot at eye level as the camera executes a slow push in. In the center of the frame behind a pristine white glossy display table stands a young East Asian female livestreamer in her early 20s with delicate features, immaculate porcelain makeup, and soft wavy dark hair falling over her shoulders, wearing an elegant ivory satin button-up blouse (S1). She smiles warmly toward the camera while holding up an open, sleek black-and-gold lipstick tube that reveals a vibrant, sharp-cut true-red lipstick bullet. The modern livestream studio is visible around her, featuring two diffused circular LED ring lights flanking the table, softboxes on stands, and warm backlit white tiered display shelves adorned with neatly arranged cosmetic bottles in the background. High-key commercial beauty lighting illuminates the scene evenly, accentuating the flawless texture of her skin and casting radiant catchlights in her eyes. She tilts the lipstick slightly to let the light reflect off the red bullet, nodding subtly in greeting. [Shot 2] At 00:04.500, the camera cuts to a medium close-up shot at eye level as it holds a static shot on the livestreamer (S1) framed from the chest up against the softly out-of-focus shelves. Radiating enthusiasm, the young livestreamer (S1) leans forward slightly, maintains eye contact with the lens, and speaks with clear, engaging cadence, <d>[Chinese] 想要一秒显白提气场，这支无可替代的经典正红，就是你的本命色！</d> Immediately upon concluding her line, she raises the lipstick to her mouth and smoothly glides the scarlet bullet across her upper and lower lips with practiced ease, coating them in a luminous, highly pigmented red. Lowering the lipstick tube slightly to chest height, she presses her lips together gently and beams an effortless, confident smile directly into the camera, tilting her chin up slightly to display the radiant, velvety finish of the lipstick.
overall_soundscape: A subtle, clean indoor studio room tone hums underneath as a distinct crisp click sounds from handling the metal-accented lipstick casing, followed by the soft rustle of satin sleeve fabric as her arm raises into frame. The smooth, subtle friction of the lipstick gliding onto lips is clearly heard, concluding with a light, distinct smack of lips parting into a smile.
non_diegetic_music: Light commercial electronic pop track featuring bright synthesizer plucks, crisp finger snaps, and a steady mid-tempo bass pulse that maintains an even, upbeat dynamic across the scene.

反例

第一镜出现厨房水槽和吊柜，直到下一镜才进入直播间。

反例原始 prompt

年轻女主播妆容精致，手持一支正红色口红，微笑面对镜头。第一镜清楚显示她坐在家用厨房操作台前，身后是水槽和吊柜；切入第二镜时才出现直播间的白色产品桌、补光灯与陈列架。她热情地说：“想要一秒显白提气场，这支无可替代的经典正红，就是你的本命色！”随后将口红涂到嘴唇上，微笑展示。商业广告布光，肤质自然、口红鲜亮。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a medium shot at eye level as the camera slowly pushes in on an energetic young East Asian female livestream host in her mid-twenties with immaculate, porcelain-smooth makeup (S1), seated centered behind a domestic granite kitchen island. Between her manicured fingers, she holds a sleek black-and-gold cased lipstick with its cap on, tilting the tube so its gilded trim catches the soft ambient kitchen light. In the background sits a stainless steel undermount sink with a gooseneck faucet and matte gray hanging overhead cabinets. Warm practical under-cabinet lighting combines with diffused daylight from an off-frame window, illuminating her face naturally as she tilts her head slightly and flashes a charming, welcoming smile directly into the lens. [Shot 2] At 00:03.800, the camera cuts to a medium close-up at eye level holding a static shot of the host (S1) from Shot 1, now situated in a high-end livestream studio. In the foreground sits a glossy white acrylic presentation desk neatly arranged with clear acrylic cosmetic risers, metallic lipstick stands, and elegant packaging boxes. 橱窗里的萤火环

考点圆环是否由分开的光点组成？
观察方法核对环的组成是颗粒还是连续光带。

正例

暖金光点沿环移动，相邻两点之间能看见空隙。

正例原始 prompt

雨后傍晚，固定中近景。橱窗内一只玻璃瓶被轻轻揭开，十余颗暖金小光点沿同一圆环慢慢转动，点与点之间留有可见间隙，绕过瓶颈后慢慢熄灭。玻璃与街景保持写实。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a static medium close-up shot frames an antique shop window display in a city at dusk after a recent rainfall. In the foreground, an ornate transparent glass apothecary bottle rests upon a polished dark walnut display stand, its cylindrical body catching sharp reflections from both the cozy interior boutique lighting and the exterior street. The background reveals a photorealistic urban streetscape through the large windowpane, where blurred red taillights and warm streetlamps shimmer across wet asphalt, while scattered raindrops slowly trickle down the outer surface of the glass. A slender, fair-skinned adult hand wearing a dark charcoal wool coat sleeve reaches into frame from the right, delicately gripping the glass bottle stopper and lifting it vertically away from the neck before receding smoothly out of frame. Immediately, approximately fifteen warm-golden specks of light drift gently upward from the open mouth of the bottle. The luminous motes smoothly arrange themselves along a uniform horizontal ring, maintaining distinct, clearly visible intervals between each separate glowing point. The circular halo rotates slowly and steadily around the bottle's neck, casting flickering golden highlights across the inner curve of the glass and illuminating subtle dust motes in the immediate air. As the ring completes its slow revolution around the glass lip, the golden points gradually diminish in brightness, gently dimming and extinguishing one after another into the ambient evening air, leaving the bottle standing silently amid the cool twilight reflections.
overall_soundscape: A distinct, gentle glass squeak and light suction click are clearly heard as the bottle stopper is unsealed, accompanied by a muffled ambient thrum of raindrops pattering against the heavy exterior windowpane. Faint tire swishes across wet street asphalt resonate in the background, paired with a pronounced, faint airy chime as the golden motes rise, transitioning into absolute quiet room tone as they fade away.
non_diegetic_music: Sparse acoustic piano playing slow, delicate high-register notes accompanied by a warm, sustained cello drone underneath, maintaining a steady, low-volume dynamic throughout.

反例

同样绕圈，却是一条没有空隙的连续实心光带。

反例原始 prompt

雨后傍晚，固定中近景。橱窗内一只玻璃瓶被轻轻揭开，一条连续的暖金实心光带沿同一圆环慢慢转动，整圈连成一片，绕过瓶颈后慢慢熄灭。玻璃与街景保持写实。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a static medium close-up shot, a clear vintage glass apothecary bottle with faceted shoulders rests on an oiled dark walnut display pedestal inside an upscale boutique window. Beyond the thick display glass, coated with delicate diagonal rain rivulets and clustered droplets, an evening city street glistens with wet asphalt, blurred headlights in cyan and amber, and distant umbrella-carrying pedestrians in soft twilight bokeh. Inside the warm, dimly lit shop interior, a soft overhead tungsten spotlight illuminates the polished glass surface, catching crystalline highlights on its rim. At the center of the frame, a slender right hand with neat fingernails and a charcoal-grey cashmere sleeve enters from the upper right, gently grasping the faceted spherical glass stopper and lifting it upward with deliberate slowness. As the stopper clears the rim, a bright, solid band of warm golden light ignites at the lip, smoothly gliding along the circular perimeter in a continuous unbroken sweep. The glowing golden ribbon completes a full seamless luminous ring, casting rich amber caustics and refractions across the bottle's inner contours and the damp window pane. The band of light then glides downward in a tight helical orbit around the slender neck of the bottle, lingering as a brilliant glowing collar before its luminescence softens, steadily dimming and dissolving until it fully extinguishes, returning the glass vessel to its quiet, realistic transparency amidst the ambient rainy dusk reflections.
overall_soundscape: A gentle outdoor ambience of distant rainfall and tires hissing on wet pavement is clearly heard through the window pane, followed by the faint, distinct clink of the glass stopper releasing from the bottle neck. As the golden light emerges, a soft, sustained crystalline hum resonates in the foreground, which transitions into a muted ambient room tone as the light smoothly fades.
non_diegetic_music: Solo acoustic piano playing sparse, gentle high-register chords over a very soft, sustained cello drone at a slow tempo, maintaining a delicate and steady dynamic throughout without swells.

纸袋上的文字标识

考点纸袋标识的第一个字是否正确？
观察方法逐字核对第一字。能读清但字错属于文字标识响应。

正例

清楚读到“青禾咖啡”，第一个字是“青”。

正例原始 prompt

固定近景，早晨的咖啡店柜台。店员将米色纸袋递向镜头，袋身正面的深绿色无衬线字样写着完整的“青禾咖啡”四字。镜头停留两秒，字迹清晰，纸袋和背景在柔和自然光下。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a static close-up shot at eye level, a bright and tidy specialty coffee shop counter unfolds in the calm hours of the morning. In the midground behind the polished light oak wooden counter, an East Asian barista in their mid-20s with short black hair tucked behind their ears wears a clean olive-green canvas apron over an ivory crewneck cotton t-shirt. In the softly blurred background, a matte black dual-group espresso machine emits a wispy curl of faint steam, flanked by neatly stacked ceramic cups on a metal tray and a small amber glass vase holding sprigs of dried eucalyptus on a floating wooden shelf. Diffused, warm morning sunlight pours from a large off-screen window on the left, casting soft-edged shadows and illuminating floating dust motes in the air. The barista securely holds a tailored, matte beige kraft paper takeaway bag with twisted paper handles using both hands, gently lifting it from the prep area. With a smooth and steady motion, the barista pushes the bag forward toward the camera, placing it centered in the crisp foreground. The front surface of the paper bag is held flat and fully visible to the lens, displaying sharp, deep green sans-serif printed text that clearly reads "青禾咖啡". The barista pauses, steadying the bag on the wooden surface while meeting the viewer's eye line with a courteous, gentle nod and a relaxed smile. The camera maintains its stationary frame as soft ambient morning light plays across the crisp fiber texture of the paper bag and the counter's wood grain, with the barista holding a polite, welcoming posture as steam faintly rises in the soft-focus background.
overall_soundscape: A gentle morning cafe room tone with a faint, low hum from the refrigeration unit, followed by the distinct, crisp rustle of thick kraft paper as the bag is handled and extended forward. An audible, clean soft tap resonates as the bottom of the paper bag meets the polished oak counter, accompanied by the distant, pronounced metallic clink of a ceramic cup being set on a saucer in the background and a subtle hiss of steam dissipating.
non_diegetic_music: Acoustic fingerstyle guitar accompanied by an airy, soft upright piano in a slow, unhurried tempo, maintaining an even, gentle dynamic shape with no sudden swells.

反例

清楚读到“清禾咖啡”，第一个字多了三点水。

反例原始 prompt

固定近景，早晨的咖啡店柜台。店员将米色纸袋递向镜头，袋身正面的深绿色无衬线字样始终清晰地印成“清禾咖啡”，第一字是三点水的“清”。镜头停留两秒，字迹清晰，纸袋和背景在柔和自然光下。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a close-up framing from a straight-on eye-level angle with a static shot captures a serene morning coffee shop counter. In the midground, a clean-shaven East Asian male barista in his early twenties with short black hair wears a dark forest-green canvas apron over a plain white cotton crewneck t-shirt. The counter is made of smooth, warm-toned polished oak, illuminated by soft, diffused morning sunlight pouring in from a large window off to the side, while the blurred background displays a polished stainless-steel espresso machine with a warm metallic luster, neatly stacked ceramic coffee cups, and amber glass syrup bottles on a wooden shelf. As the clip begins, the barista gently holds a crisp beige kraft paper takeout bag with both hands and smoothly extends it forward toward the camera. The front panel of the bag faces squarely into the lens, displaying deep-green sans-serif Chinese lettering clearly printed across the center: "清禾咖啡", with the three-dot water radical of the first character distinctly visible and sharp. The barista rests the flat base of the paper bag steadily onto the wooden counter surface in the foreground, letting his fingers slowly release the folded top edge. He offers a polite, subtle nod, his shoulders relaxing downward as a calm, hospitable half-smile forms across his face. The camera maintains its steady composition on the stationary bag, whose texture and deep-green typography remain perfectly crisp under the gentle, non-directional morning light, while faint micro-drifts of ambient steam slowly dissipate in the soft-focus background behind him.
overall_soundscape: A faint electrical hum and soft ambient boiler warmth fill the room tone, as the crisp, dry rustle of the kraft paper bag folding is distinctly audible in the foreground. This is followed by a clear, muted thud as the flat bottom of the paper bag meets the polished oak counter, accompanied by the subtle fabric rustle of the barista's apron as he nods.
non_diegetic_music: A solo nylon-string acoustic guitar plays an unhurried, fingerpicked arpeggio pattern at a slow tempo, joined beneath by a steady, low-register upright bass without any dynamic swells or percussive accents.

11-15 · 参考一致性的文字改编 · 构图／景别／站位参考不像

站台上两人的左右站位

考点人物、物件与画面左右侧是否对应？
观察方法以画面边界作坐标，核对物件与人物、人物与左右侧的对应。

正例

拿票的蓝外套在左，拿书的红围巾在右。

正例原始 prompt

固定双人中景，两位成年人在空旷站台面对面，衣服和手中的物件清楚可见。穿蓝外套拿车票的人站画面左侧，戴红围巾拿书的人站右侧。广播响起，两人互相点头，始终处于同一镜头。

正例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, in a static medium two-shot framed at eye level, two adults stand facing each other across the center of an expansive, open-air train station platform under an overcast slate-gray sky. On the left side of the frame, an adult male in his early thirties with trimmed dark hair and a calm expression wears a structured navy-blue wool pea coat over charcoal trousers, holding a printed rectangular train ticket between his gloved right fingers at waist height. On the right side, facing him in profile, an adult female in her late twenties with her dark hair gathered beneath a voluminous crimson knitted wool scarf wears a tailored beige trench coat, holding a thick hardcover book bound in dark green cloth with both hands at chest level. The concrete platform stretches behind them with yellow textured tactile paving strips along the platform edge, illuminated by cold overhead fluorescent tubes suspended from industrial iron girders and distant amber platform signals glowing softly in the misty distance. As an automated chime chimes from an unseen public address speaker above, the man in the blue coat slightly lowers his gaze to the paper ticket before raising his eyes back to her face. In response, the woman in the red scarf offers a gentle, reassuring nod with a subtle softening around her eyes, shifting her grip slightly on the hardcover book. The man returns the deliberate nod with a slight dip of his head and shoulders, standing grounded as a chilly draft lightly flutters the corner of the paper ticket and rustles the fringed edge of her wool scarf while both maintain steady eye contact.
overall_soundscape: A hollow, low-frequency wind whistles continuously across the vacant concrete platform, accompanied by the distant mechanical hum of idle rail equipment. A distinct two-tone electronic chime chimes overhead, followed by an audible, reverberant public address voice echoing throughout the station structure. In the foreground, the faint rustle of the paper ticket shifting in gloved fingers and the soft friction of heavy wool fabric are clearly heard against the ambient station air.
non_diegetic_music: Sparse acoustic piano playing isolated, slow-tempo chords in the mid-register, supported underneath by a sustained, low-register cello bow with a flat dynamic contour.

反例

人和物件仍对应，但蓝外套在右、红围巾在左。

反例原始 prompt

固定双人中景，两位成年人在空旷站台面对面，衣服和手中的物件清楚可见。穿蓝外套拿车票的人站画面右侧，戴红围巾拿书的人站左侧。广播响起，两人互相点头，始终处于同一镜头。

反例最终 PE prompt

integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, a medium two-shot at eye level, where the camera holds a static shot framed symmetrically on a spacious, vacant outdoor train platform. On the left side of the frame stands an adult woman in her early thirties, with her dark hair gathered into a neat low chignon, dressed in an ankle-length charcoal wool overcoat and a thick, vibrant crimson-red knitted scarf draped loosely around her neck; her hands, clad in dark wool gloves, clasp an olive-green clothbound hardcover book at waist level. On the right side of the frame, facing her in profile, stands an adult man in his mid-thirties with trimmed dark hair and a light stubble, wearing a structured navy-blue tailored wool trench coat over a high slate-gray knit collar, holding a crisp rectangular cream-colored railway ticket between his right thumb and fingers. Between them, the damp concrete platform floor stretches out, bordered by a strip of textured yellow tactile paving. In the background, weathered steel canopy columns recede into a soft, cool evening haze, flanked by empty iron rails and distant amber platform lanterns that cast a warm overhead glow across their coats and faces, contrasting with the overcast indigo twilight sky. As an overhead public address chime chimes across the station, the woman gently tilts her head forward and delivers a slow, meaningful nod of parting, her expression serene yet tinged with quiet emotion. The man in the blue coat tightens his fingers slightly on the ticket, lowers his gaze momentarily, and returns a firm, gentle nod of mutual understanding, while a faint passing breeze lightly stirs the fringe of her red scarf.
overall_soundscape: A distinct, pronounced two-tone electronic station chime rings out overhead, followed by the muffled hum of an echoing platform public address broadcast. A clearly heard gust of cool wind whistles softly through the steel roof girders and causes heavy wool fabric to rustle audibly. Distant low rumble of idle train engines on remote tracks hums steadily in the background alongside the faint, crisp snap of paper as the railway ticket shifts slightly in hand.
non_diegetic_music: Sparse acoustic piano playing slow, deliberate chords accompanied by a low, sustained cello drone, holding an even and quiet dynamic shape throughout without swells.
